
================================================================================
ФАЙЛ: animationManager.js
================================================================================

// js/animationManager.js
export class AnimationManager {
    constructor(upperAnimGroups, lowerAnimGroups) {
        this.upper = upperAnimGroups;
        this.lower = lowerAnimGroups;

        this.currentUpperBase = null;
        this.currentLowerBase = null;

        this.forcedUpper = null;
        this.forcedLower = null;

        this.upperAction = null;
        this.lowerAction = null;
        this.actionsPlayingCount = 0;

        this.lockBase = false;

        this._savedBlendingState = null;

        // 🆕 Скорости анимаций (по умолчанию 1.0)
        this.upperSpeed = 1.0;
        this.lowerSpeed = 1.0;
    }

    // Временное отключение/включение blending на сцене
    setGlobalBlending(enable) {
        const anyGroup = Object.values(this.upper)[0] || Object.values(this.lower)[0];
        if (!anyGroup) return;
        const scene = anyGroup._scene;
        if (!scene.animationPropertiesOverride) return;

        if (this._savedBlendingState === null) {
            this._savedBlendingState = {
                enableBlending: scene.animationPropertiesOverride.enableBlending,
                blendingSpeed: scene.animationPropertiesOverride.blendingSpeed
            };
        }

        scene.animationPropertiesOverride.enableBlending = enable;
        scene.animationPropertiesOverride.blendingSpeed = enable ? this._savedBlendingState.blendingSpeed : 0;
    }

    restoreBlending() {
        if (this._savedBlendingState === null) return;
        const anyGroup = Object.values(this.upper)[0] || Object.values(this.lower)[0];
        if (!anyGroup) return;
        const scene = anyGroup._scene;
        if (scene.animationPropertiesOverride) {
            scene.animationPropertiesOverride.enableBlending = this._savedBlendingState.enableBlending;
            scene.animationPropertiesOverride.blendingSpeed = this._savedBlendingState.blendingSpeed;
        }
        this._savedBlendingState = null;
    }

    // Остановка всех циклических анимаций
    stopAll() {
        this.stopUpperBase();
        this.stopLowerBase();
    }

    stopUpperBase() {
        if (this.currentUpperBase && this.upper[this.currentUpperBase]) {
            this.upper[this.currentUpperBase].stop();
            this.currentUpperBase = null;
        }
    }

    stopLowerBase() {
        if (this.currentLowerBase && this.lower[this.currentLowerBase]) {
            this.lower[this.currentLowerBase].stop();
            this.currentLowerBase = null;
        }
    }

    // 🆕 Новый метод для установки скорости анимаций
    setAnimationSpeed(upperSpeed, lowerSpeed) {
        this.upperSpeed = Math.max(0, upperSpeed);
        this.lowerSpeed = Math.max(0, lowerSpeed);

        // Применяем к текущим базовым анимациям
        if (this.currentUpperBase && this.upper[this.currentUpperBase]) {
            this.upper[this.currentUpperBase].speedRatio = this.upperSpeed;
        }
        if (this.currentLowerBase && this.lower[this.currentLowerBase]) {
            this.lower[this.currentLowerBase].speedRatio = this.lowerSpeed;
        }
    }

    // Новый метод для запуска верхней базовой анимации
    startUpperBase(animName) {
        if (!this.upper[animName]) {
            console.warn(`startUpperBase: анимация ${animName} не найдена`);
            return;
        }
        this._startBase('upper', animName);
    }

    setForcedUpper(animName) {
        if (this.forcedUpper === animName) return;
        this.forcedUpper = animName;
        if (animName && !this.lockBase) {
            this._startBase('upper', animName);
        }
    }

    setForcedLower(animName) {
        if (this.forcedLower === animName) return;
        this.forcedLower = animName;
        if (animName && !this.lockBase) {
            this._startBase('lower', animName);
        }
    }

    _startBase(part, animName) {
        const groups = part === 'upper' ? this.upper : this.lower;
        const current = part === 'upper' ? this.currentUpperBase : this.currentLowerBase;
        const group = groups[animName];
        if (!group) return;

        if (current && groups[current]) {
            groups[current].stop();
        }

        // 🆕 Применяем текущую скорость анимации
        const speedRatio = part === 'upper' ? this.upperSpeed : this.lowerSpeed;
        group.start(true, speedRatio, group.from, group.to, false);

        if (part === 'upper') {
            this.currentUpperBase = animName;
        } else {
            this.currentLowerBase = animName;
        }
    }

    resetActions() {
        if (this.upperAction) {
            this.upperAction.stop();
            this.upperAction = null;
        }
        if (this.lowerAction) {
            this.lowerAction.stop();
            this.lowerAction = null;
        }
        this.actionsPlayingCount = 0;
    }

    // Запуск одноразового действия
    startAction(animName, fullBody, onEnd, options = {}) {
        const { disableBlending = false, lockBase = false } = options;
        if (!this.upper[animName] && !this.lower[animName]) {
            console.warn(`startAction: анимация ${animName} не найдена`);
            return;
        }

        this.resetActions();
        this.stopAll();

        if (lockBase) {
            this.lockBase = true;
        }

        if (disableBlending) {
            this.setGlobalBlending(false);
        }

        if (this.upper[animName]) {
            this.currentUpperBase = null;
        }
        if (fullBody && this.lower[animName]) {
            this.currentLowerBase = null;
        }

        let count = 0;
        const onOneEnd = () => {
            count--;
            if (count === 0) {
                if (disableBlending) {
                    this.restoreBlending();
                }
                if (lockBase) {
                    this.lockBase = false;
                }
                if (onEnd) onEnd();
                this.resetActions();
            }
        };

        // 🆕 Для действий тоже применяем скорость (но не меньше 0.5 для читаемости)
        const actionSpeed = Math.max(0.5, part => part === 'upper' ? this.upperSpeed : this.lowerSpeed);

        if (this.upper[animName]) {
            this.upperAction = this.upper[animName];
            this.upperAction.onAnimationGroupEndObservable.addOnce(onOneEnd);
            this.upperAction.start(false, Math.max(0.5, this.upperSpeed), this.upperAction.from, this.upperAction.to, false);
            count++;
        }
        if (fullBody && this.lower[animName]) {
            this.lowerAction = this.lower[animName];
            this.lowerAction.onAnimationGroupEndObservable.addOnce(onOneEnd);
            this.lowerAction.start(false, Math.max(0.5, this.lowerSpeed), this.lowerAction.from, this.lowerAction.to, false);
            count++;
        }
        this.actionsPlayingCount = count;
    }

    // Логика выбора нижней анимации на основе движения и стресса
    getDesiredLowerFromMovement(moveState, stressLevel = 0) {
        if (!moveState.hasVelocity) {
            if (stressLevel > 50) {
                if (this.lower['Tired_Hunched']) return 'Tired_Hunched';
            }
            const idleCandidates = ['Idle_Loop', 'idle', 'standing_idle', 'Idle'];
            for (let name of idleCandidates) {
                if (this.lower[name]) return name;
            }
        } else {
            if (stressLevel > 50) {
                if (this.lower['Zombie_Walk_Fwd_Loop']) return 'Zombie_Walk_Fwd_Loop';
            }
            if (moveState.isCrouching) {
                const crouchCandidates = ['Crouch_Fwd_Loop', 'Crouch_Walk', 'crouch_forward'];
                for (let name of crouchCandidates) {
                    if (this.lower[name]) return name;
                }
            }
            if (moveState.isRunning) {
                const runCandidates = ['Sprint_Loop', 'Run Anime', 'Jog_Fwd_Loop', 'Running'];
                for (let name of runCandidates) {
                    if (this.lower[name]) return name;
                }
            }
            const walkCandidates = ['Walk_Loop', 'Walk_Formal_Loop', 'walk', 'Walking'];
            for (let name of walkCandidates) {
                if (this.lower[name]) return name;
            }
        }
        return null;
    }

    // Логика выбора верхней анимации на основе состояния и стресса
    getDesiredUpperFromState(stateName, stressLevel, weaponDrawn) {
        if (weaponDrawn && this.upper['Sword_Idle']) {
            return 'Sword_Idle';
        }
        if (stressLevel > 50 && this.upper['Dizzy']) {
            return 'Dizzy';
        }
        if (stateName === 'defend' && this.upper['Defend']) {
            return 'Defend';
        }
        return null;
    }

    // Установка базовых анимаций (циклических) – учитываем lockBase
    setBaseAnimations(desiredUpper, desiredLower) {
        if (this.lockBase) return;

        const upperToPlay = this.forcedUpper || desiredUpper;
        if (upperToPlay && this.upper[upperToPlay]) {
            if (upperToPlay !== this.currentUpperBase) {
                this._startBase('upper', upperToPlay);
            }
        } else if (!this.forcedUpper && desiredLower && this.upper[desiredLower]) {
            if (desiredLower !== this.currentUpperBase) {
                this._startBase('upper', desiredLower);
            }
        }

        const lowerToPlay = this.forcedLower || desiredLower;
        if (lowerToPlay && this.lower[lowerToPlay]) {
            if (lowerToPlay !== this.currentLowerBase) {
                this._startBase('lower', lowerToPlay);
            }
        }
    }
}

ФАЙЛ: autonomousBehaviour.js

// js/autonomousBehaviour.js
// Инстинктивные реакции персонажа на основе Utility AI,
// субъективного состояния (PerceivedCondition), памяти (EpisodicMemory)
// и доверия к игроку (TrustModel).

export class AutonomousBehaviour {
    constructor(scene, characterRoot, krl, perceptionSystem, staminaSystem, healthSystem, movementSystem, episodicMemory, perceivedCondition, trustModel) {
        this.scene = scene;
        this.characterRoot = characterRoot;
        this.krl = krl;                       // KinematicRagdollLayer (управляет позами через activatePose)
        this.perception = perceptionSystem;
        this.stamina = staminaSystem;
        this.health = healthSystem;
        this.movement = movementSystem;
        this.memory = episodicMemory;
        this.perceived = perceivedCondition;
        this.trust = trustModel;

        this.fearLevel = 0;
        this.fearDecayRate = 0.3;
        this.fearIncreaseRate = 0.4;

        this.currentAction = 'none';
        this._lastActionScore = 0;
        this._actionLockTimer = 0;
        this._actionLockTime = 0.5;

        this._idleTimer = 0;
        this._lastInputTime = performance.now() / 1000;

        this.actions = [
            'idle_alert',
            'block_hand',
            'dodge',
            'retreat',
            'freeze',
            'twitch',
            'sanctuary_pull'
        ];

        this.TRUST_THRESHOLD = 0.5;
        this.FEAR_THRESHOLD = 0.6;
    }

    _sigmoid(x, x0, k = 5) {
        const t = k * (x - x0);
        return 1 / (1 + Math.exp(-t));
    }

    _invSigmoid(x, x0, k = 5) {
        return 1 - this._sigmoid(x, x0, k);
    }

    _scoreAction(action, ctx) {
        const { fearLevel, nearestThreat, perceivedHealthRatio, perceivedStaminaRatio, trust, sanctuaryDistance } = ctx;

        let dist = nearestThreat ? nearestThreat.distance : 999;
        let telegraph = nearestThreat ? nearestThreat.telegraphProgress : 0;
        let damageZone = nearestThreat ? nearestThreat.damageZone : 'any';
        let subtype = nearestThreat ? nearestThreat.subtype : 'generic';
        const fearBonus = this.memory.getFearBonus(subtype);

        let score = 1.0;

        switch (action) {
            case 'idle_alert':
                score *= this._sigmoid(fearLevel, 0.05, 10);
                score *= this._invSigmoid(fearLevel, 0.25, 10);
                score *= this._invSigmoid(dist, 8, 2);
                break;

            case 'block_hand':
                if (trust < this.TRUST_THRESHOLD || fearLevel > this.FEAR_THRESHOLD) {
                    score = 0;
                } else {
                    score *= this._sigmoid(fearLevel, 0.2, 8);
                    score *= this._invSigmoid(fearLevel, 0.6, 8);
                    score *= this._invSigmoid(dist, 2.5, 4);
                    score *= this._sigmoid(telegraph, 0.2, 10);
                    if (damageZone === 'head') score *= 1.5;
                    if (damageZone === 'torso') score *= 1.2;
                    if (perceivedStaminaRatio < 0.3) score *= 0.4;
                    score *= (0.5 + trust * 0.5);
                }
                break;

            case 'dodge':
                score *= this._sigmoid(fearLevel, 0.3, 8);
                score *= this._invSigmoid(fearLevel, 0.7, 8);
                score *= this._invSigmoid(dist, 2.0, 5);
                score *= this._sigmoid(telegraph, 0.15, 8);
                if (damageZone === 'leg' || damageZone === 'head') score *= 1.3;
                score *= this._sigmoid(perceivedStaminaRatio, 0.3, 8);
                break;

            case 'retreat':
                if (trust < this.TRUST_THRESHOLD || fearLevel > this.FEAR_THRESHOLD) {
                    score = 0;
                } else {
                    score *= this._sigmoid(fearLevel, 0.5, 8);
                    score *= this._invSigmoid(perceivedHealthRatio, 0.4, 6);
                    score *= this._sigmoid(perceivedStaminaRatio, 0.2, 6);
                    score *= (1 - trust * 0.5);
                }
                break;

            case 'freeze':
                score *= this._sigmoid(fearLevel, 0.85, 12);
                score *= this._invSigmoid(perceivedHealthRatio, 0.2, 10);
                score *= 1.5;
                break;

            case 'twitch':
                score *= this._invSigmoid(fearLevel, 0.4, 6);
                score *= fearBonus > 0.3 ? 2.0 : 0.3;
                if (this._idleTimer < 2) score *= 0.1;
                break;

            case 'sanctuary_pull':
                if (trust < this.TRUST_THRESHOLD || fearLevel > this.FEAR_THRESHOLD) {
                    score = 0;
                } else {
                    score *= this._sigmoid(fearLevel, 0.4, 8);
                    score *= this._invSigmoid(sanctuaryDistance, 4, 2);
                    score *= (1 - trust * 0.6);
                }
                break;

            default:
                score = 0;
        }
        return score;
    }

    update(deltaSec, playerInputActive) {
        if (window.gameplayMode !== 2) {
            this.fearLevel = Math.max(0, this.fearLevel - this.fearDecayRate * deltaSec);
            return;
        }

        if (!this.perception.snapshot) return;

        const snapshot = this.perception.snapshot;
        const now = performance.now() / 1000;

        if (playerInputActive) {
            this._lastInputTime = now;
        }
        this._idleTimer = now - this._lastInputTime;

        this.memory.update(deltaSec);

        let maxFear = 0;
        let nearestThreat = null;
        for (const t of snapshot.threats) {
            const distFactor = Math.max(0, 1 - t.distance / this.perception.mediumRadius);
            const fearBonus = this.memory.getFearBonus(t.subtype);
            const threatFear = t.fearIntensity * 0.15 * distFactor + fearBonus * distFactor;
            if (threatFear > maxFear) {
                maxFear = threatFear;
                nearestThreat = t;
            }
        }

        for (const p of snapshot.projectiles) {
            const distFactor = Math.max(0, 1 - p.distance / this.perception.farRadius);
            const projFear = p.speed * 0.05 * distFactor;
            if (projFear > maxFear) {
                maxFear = projFear;
                nearestThreat = {
                    direction: p.direction,
                    distance: p.distance,
                    subtype: 'projectile',
                    damageZone: 'any',
                    isAttacking: true,
                    telegraphProgress: 1,
                    fearIntensity: 4
                };
            }
        }

        if (maxFear > this.fearLevel) {
            this.fearLevel = Math.min(1.0, this.fearLevel + this.fearIncreaseRate * deltaSec * (1 + maxFear));
        } else {
            this.fearLevel = Math.max(0.0, this.fearLevel - this.fearDecayRate * deltaSec);
        }

        const playerPushingTowardDanger = this._isPlayerPushingToDanger(nearestThreat);
        const isSafe = this._isInSanctuary(snapshot);
        this.trust.update(deltaSec, isSafe, playerPushingTowardDanger);

        if (this._actionLockTimer > 0) {
            this._actionLockTimer -= deltaSec;
            return;
        }

        const ctx = {
            fearLevel: this.fearLevel,
            nearestThreat,
            perceivedHealthRatio: this.perceived.getHealthRatio(),
            perceivedStaminaRatio: this.perceived.getStaminaRatio(),
            trust: this.trust.getTrust(),
            sanctuaryDistance: snapshot.sanctuaries.length > 0 ? snapshot.sanctuaries[0].distance : 999
        };

        let bestAction = 'none';
        let bestScore = 0;
        for (const action of this.actions) {
            const score = this._scoreAction(action, ctx);
            if (score > bestScore) {
                bestScore = score;
                bestAction = action;
            }
        }

        if (bestAction !== this.currentAction && bestAction !== 'none') {
            console.log(`[Autonomous] action: ${bestAction} (score: ${bestScore.toFixed(2)}), fear: ${this.fearLevel.toFixed(2)}, trust: ${this.trust.getTrust().toFixed(2)}`);
            this._actionLockTimer = this._actionLockTime;
        }

        this._applyAction(bestAction, ctx);
        this.currentAction = bestAction;
        this._lastActionScore = bestScore;
    }

    _isPlayerPushingToDanger(nearestThreat) {
        if (!nearestThreat) return false;
        const moveState = this.movement.getMovementState();
        if (!moveState.isMoving) return false;
        const moveDir = new BABYLON.Vector3(moveState.moveX, 0, moveState.moveY).normalize();
        const toThreat = nearestThreat.direction.scale(-1);
        const dot = BABYLON.Vector3.Dot(moveDir, toThreat);
        return dot > 0.3 && nearestThreat.distance < 5;
    }

    _isInSanctuary(snapshot) {
        for (const s of snapshot.sanctuaries) {
            if (s.distance < 2.5) return true;
        }
        return false;
    }

    // ----------------------------------------------------------------
    // Применение действий (используем новый API KRL)
    // ----------------------------------------------------------------
    _applyAction(action, ctx) {
        // Сброс всех KRL‑целей (руки и голова)
        if (this.krl) {
            this.krl.deactivatePose();   // обе руки
            this.krl.clearBoneTarget('pelvis');
            this.krl.clearBoneTarget('spine_03');
            this.krl.setLookTarget(null);
        }
        this.movement.isEvading = false;

        const { nearestThreat } = ctx;

        switch (action) {
            case 'idle_alert':
                this._applyAlert(nearestThreat);
                break;
            case 'block_hand':
                this._applyBlock(nearestThreat);
                break;
            case 'dodge':
                this._applyDodge(nearestThreat);
                this.movement.isEvading = true;
                break;
            case 'retreat':
                this._applyRetreat(nearestThreat);
                this.movement.isEvading = true;
                break;
            case 'freeze':
                this._applyFreeze();
                break;
            case 'twitch':
                this._applyNervousTwitch();
                break;
            case 'sanctuary_pull':
                this._applySanctuaryPull(ctx);
                break;
            default:
                this.movement.externalSpeedLimit = 1.0;
                break;
        }
    }

    _applyAlert(threat) {
        // Без смещений корпуса, чтобы не мешать игроку
    }

    _applyFreeze() {
        // Физика сама застынет — никаких команд
    }

    _applyRetreat(threat) {
        if (!threat) return;
        const retreatDir = threat.direction.scale(-1);
        this.movement.externalBias.addInPlace(retreatDir.scale(0.3));
        this.movement.externalSpeedLimit = 0.8;
    }

    _applyDodge(threat) {
        if (!threat) return;
        const dodgeDir = new BABYLON.Vector3(-threat.direction.z, 0, threat.direction.x);
        this.movement.externalBias.addInPlace(dodgeDir.scale(0.4));
        this.movement.externalSpeedLimit = 0.85;
    }

    _applyBlock(threat) {
        if (!threat) return;
        const side = threat.direction.x < 0 ? 'r' : 'l';
        // Активируем позу блокирования головы
        this.krl.activatePose(`block_head_${side}`);
        // Вторую руку слегка поднимаем
        const otherSide = side === 'r' ? 'l' : 'r';
        this.krl.activatePose(`block_torso_${otherSide}`);
    }

    _applyNervousTwitch() {

    }

    _applySanctuaryPull(ctx) {
        if (!ctx.nearestThreat) return;
        const sanctuary = this.perception.snapshot?.sanctuaries?.[0];
        if (!sanctuary) return;
        const pullDir = sanctuary.direction;
        this.movement.externalBias.addInPlace(pullDir.scale(0.3));
        this.movement.externalSpeedLimit = 0.9;
    }

    recordDanger(hostileSubtype, attackPattern, damageZone, damage) {
        this.memory.addEvent('damage', {
            subtype: hostileSubtype,
            pattern: attackPattern,
            zone: damageZone,
            force: damage
        });
        const fearBonus = this.memory.getFearBonus(hostileSubtype);
        this.perceived.applyDamage(damage, damageZone, this.fearLevel, fearBonus);
        this.trust.recordDamage(damage);
    }

    dispose() {
        this.krl = null;
        this.perception = null;
    }
}

ФАЙЛ: collisionResponse.js

// js/collisionResponse.js
import { STATE_NAMES } from './stateMachine.js';

export class CollisionResponseHandler {
    constructor(stateMachine, movementSystem, staminaSystem) {
        this.stateMachine = stateMachine;
        this.movement = movementSystem;
        this.stamina = staminaSystem;
    }

    handleImpact(collisionData, speed, tier) {
        const force = collisionData.force || speed * 5;

        // Расход выносливости от удара о статику
        if (this.stamina) {
            this.stamina.consumeByImpact(force);
        }

        const effectiveTier = (tier === 'medium' && this.stamina?.isExhausted) ? 'high' : tier;

        if (effectiveTier === 'medium') {
            this.stateMachine.transitionTo(STATE_NAMES.STUMBLE, {
                direction: collisionData.normal,
                force: force,
                contactPoint: collisionData.point
            });
        } else if (effectiveTier === 'high') {
            this.stateMachine.transitionTo(STATE_NAMES.RAGDOLL_IMPACT, {
                direction: collisionData.normal,
                force: force * 1.5,
                contactPoint: collisionData.point
            });
        }
        this.movement.setInputLock(true);
    }
}

ФАЙЛ: dynamicInteraction.js

// js/dynamicInteraction.js
import { STATE_NAMES } from './stateMachine.js';

export class DynamicInteractionManager {
    constructor(scene, camera, upperRoot, meshUpper, skeleton, boneBodyMap,
                animManager, staminaSystem, movementSystem, weaponSystem, reactionSystem, stateMachine) {
        this.scene = scene;
        this.camera = camera;
        this.upperRoot = upperRoot;
        this.mesh = meshUpper;
        this.skeleton = skeleton;
        this.boneBodyMap = boneBodyMap;
        this.animManager = animManager;
        this.stamina = staminaSystem;
        this.movement = movementSystem;
        this.weaponSystem = weaponSystem;
        this.reactionSystem = reactionSystem;
        this.stateMachine = stateMachine;

        this.state = 'idle';
        this.targetObject = null;
        this.grabDistance = 2.0;
        this.cooldownTimer = 0;
        this.defendCooldown = 0.5;
        this.isRightMouseHeld = false;

        this.isGrabbing = false;
        this.grabMode = null;
        this.grabTargetBody = null;
        this.grabIKTargetR = null;
        this.grabIKTargetL = null;
        this.ikControllerR = null;
        this.ikControllerL = null;
        this.grabPointLocalR = null;
        this.grabPointLocalL = null;
        this.grabOffsetLocalR = null;
        this.grabOffsetLocalL = null;
        this.springActive = false;
        this.savedCollisionMask = null;
        this.savedMeshTag = null;

        this.springStiffness = 200.0;
        this.springDamping = 25.0;
        this.maxGrabSpeed = 5.0;

        this.ikSpeedBase = 8.0;
        this.handSensitivityBase = 0.05;
        this.handAngles = { theta: 0, phi: Math.PI / 2 };
        this.neutralTheta = 0;
        this.neutralPhi = Math.PI / 2;

        this.downForceStrength = 50.0;
        this.lightSizeMax = 0.6;
        this.heavySizeMin = 1.5;
        this.heavyMassMin = 10.0;

        this.savedMaxWalk = 2.2;
        this.savedMaxRun = 5.6;

        this.isHeavyGrabbing = false;

        this.lastMouseX = 0;
        this.lastMouseY = 0;
        this.mouseDelta = new BABYLON.Vector2(0, 0);

        this.handBoneR = this._findBone('hand_r');
        this.lowerarmBoneR = this._findBone('lowerarm_r') || this._findBone('forearm_r');
        this.upperarmBoneR = this._findBone('upperarm_r');
        this.handBoneL = this._findBone('hand_l');
        this.lowerarmBoneL = this._findBone('lowerarm_l') || this._findBone('forearm_l');
        this.upperarmBoneL = this._findBone('upperarm_l');
        this.hipsBone = this._findBone('hips') || this._findBone('pelvis');

        this._defendActive = false;
        this._throwAnimName = null;
        this._sideBlockTimer = 0;

        console.log('[DIM] Initialized');
    }

    _findBone(namePart) {
        return this.skeleton.bones.find(b => b.name.toLowerCase().includes(namePart.toLowerCase()));
    }

    _isRagdollActive() {
        if (!this.stateMachine || !this.stateMachine.currentState) return false;
        const name = this.stateMachine.currentState.name;
        return name === STATE_NAMES.RAGDOLL || name === STATE_NAMES.KNOCKDOWN || name === STATE_NAMES.GET_UP;
    }

    _distanceToAABBWorld(point, mesh) {
        if (!mesh.getBoundingInfo) return Infinity;
        const aabb = mesh.getBoundingInfo().boundingBox;
        const centerWorld = aabb.centerWorld;
        const extentsWorld = aabb.extendSize;
        const closest = new BABYLON.Vector3(
            Math.max(centerWorld.x - extentsWorld.x, Math.min(point.x, centerWorld.x + extentsWorld.x)),
            Math.max(centerWorld.y - extentsWorld.y, Math.min(point.y, centerWorld.y + extentsWorld.y)),
            Math.max(centerWorld.z - extentsWorld.z, Math.min(point.z, centerWorld.z + extentsWorld.z))
        );
        return BABYLON.Vector3.Distance(point, closest);
    }

    _getClosestDynamicObject() {
        const origin = this.upperRoot.getAbsolutePosition();
        let bestDist = Infinity;
        let bestMesh = null;
        for (const mesh of this.scene.meshes) {
            if (!mesh.isVisible || mesh.metadata?.tag !== 'dynamic') continue;
            if (!mesh.getBoundingInfo) continue;
            const dist = this._distanceToAABBWorld(origin, mesh);
            if (dist < this.grabDistance && dist < bestDist) {
                bestDist = dist;
                bestMesh = mesh;
            }
        }
        if (bestMesh) {
            const dir = bestMesh.getAbsolutePosition().subtract(origin).normalize();
            const ray = new BABYLON.Ray(origin, dir, this.grabDistance + 1);
            const pick = this.scene.pickWithRay(ray, mesh => mesh === bestMesh);
            const point = pick?.pickedPoint || bestMesh.getAbsolutePosition();
            const halfSize = bestMesh.getBoundingInfo().boundingBox.extendSize;
            const realSize = new BABYLON.Vector3(halfSize.x * 2, halfSize.y * 2, halfSize.z * 2);
            return {
                mesh: bestMesh,
                point,
                normal: (pick ? pick.getNormal(true) : new BABYLON.Vector3(0,1,0)),
                size: realSize,
                halfSize: halfSize,
                mass: bestMesh.physicsBody?.mass || 1
            };
        }
        return null;
    }

    _getAllNearby() {
        const origin = this.upperRoot.getAbsolutePosition();
        const result = [];
        for (const mesh of this.scene.meshes) {
            if (!mesh.isVisible || mesh.metadata?.tag !== 'dynamic') continue;
            if (!mesh.getBoundingInfo) continue;
            const dist = this._distanceToAABBWorld(origin, mesh);
            if (dist < this.grabDistance) {
                const halfSize = mesh.getBoundingInfo().boundingBox.extendSize;
                result.push({
                    mesh,
                    dist,
                    center: mesh.getBoundingInfo().boundingBox.centerWorld,
                    size: new BABYLON.Vector3(halfSize.x*2, halfSize.y*2, halfSize.z*2),
                    halfSize,
                    mass: mesh.physicsBody?.mass || 1
                });
            }
        }
        return result;
    }

    _isObjectNearby(mesh) {
        const origin = this.upperRoot.getAbsolutePosition();
        return this._distanceToAABBWorld(origin, mesh) < this.grabDistance;
    }

    isMovingTowardsDynamic(mesh) {
        if (!this.movement) return false;
        const moveDir = new BABYLON.Vector3(Math.sin(this.movement.worldRotation), 0, Math.cos(this.movement.worldRotation));
        const toObj = mesh.getAbsolutePosition().subtract(this.upperRoot.getAbsolutePosition()).normalize();
        return BABYLON.Vector3.Dot(moveDir, toObj) > 0.5;
    }

    _classifyObject(targetInfo) {
        const maxDim = Math.max(targetInfo.size.x, targetInfo.size.y, targetInfo.size.z);
        const mass = targetInfo.mass;
        if (maxDim >= this.heavySizeMin || mass >= this.heavyMassMin * 2) return 'heavy';
        if (maxDim > this.lightSizeMax   || mass >= this.heavyMassMin)   return 'medium';
        return 'light';
    }

    toggleGrab() {
        if (this._isRagdollActive()) return;
        if (this.isGrabbing) { this.releaseGrab(); return; }
        if (this.state === 'defending') this.stopDefend();
        const targetInfo = this._getClosestDynamicObject();
        if (!targetInfo) return;
        this.startGrabbing(targetInfo, this._classifyObject(targetInfo));
    }

    startGrabbing(targetInfo, mode) {
        if (this._isRagdollActive() || this.state !== 'idle') return;
        this.state = 'grabbing';
        this.isGrabbing = true;
        this.grabMode = mode;
        this.targetObject = targetInfo.mesh;
        this.grabTargetBody = targetInfo.mesh.physicsBody;
        if (!this.grabTargetBody) return;

        this.savedMeshTag = targetInfo.mesh.metadata?.tag || 'dynamic';
        targetInfo.mesh.metadata.tag = 'grabbed';

        const points = this._computeGrabPoints(targetInfo);
        this.grabPointLocalR = points.localR;
        this.grabPointLocalL = points.localL;
        const invUpper = this.upperRoot.getWorldMatrix().clone().invert();
        const localR = BABYLON.Vector3.TransformCoordinates(points.worldR, invUpper);
        const localL = BABYLON.Vector3.TransformCoordinates(points.worldL, invUpper);
        if (mode === 'light') {
            this._createIKTarget('R', localR);
        } else {
            this._createIKTarget('R', localR);
            this._createIKTarget('L', localL);
        }
        this.grabOffsetLocalR = (mode === 'medium' || mode === 'heavy') ? localR.clone() : null;
        this.grabOffsetLocalL = (mode === 'medium' || mode === 'heavy') ? localL.clone() : null;
        this.springActive = false;
        this._disableObjectCollisions();

        this.savedMaxWalk = this.movement.movement.maxWalkSpeed;
        this.savedMaxRun = this.movement.movement.maxRunSpeed;
        let mult = mode === 'light' ? 0.8 : (mode === 'medium' ? 0.6 : 0.5);
        this.movement.movement.maxWalkSpeed *= mult;
        this.movement.movement.maxRunSpeed *= mult;

        this.handAngles.theta = this.neutralTheta;
        this.handAngles.phi = this.neutralPhi;
        this.isHeavyGrabbing = (mode === 'heavy');

        if (this.animManager.upper['Interact']) {
            this.animManager.startAction('Interact', false, null, { disableBlending: true });
        }
    }

    _computeGrabPoints(targetInfo) {
        const halfSize = targetInfo.halfSize;
        const center = targetInfo.mesh.getAbsolutePosition();
        const toObj = center.subtract(this.upperRoot.getAbsolutePosition()).normalize();
        const right = BABYLON.Vector3.Cross(toObj, BABYLON.Axis.Y).normalize();
        const halfWidth = Math.max(halfSize.x, halfSize.z) * 0.9;
        const worldR = center.add(right.scale(halfWidth));
        const worldL = center.subtract(right.scale(halfWidth));
        const invObj = targetInfo.mesh.getWorldMatrix().clone().invert();
        return {
            worldR, worldL,
            localR: BABYLON.Vector3.TransformCoordinates(worldR, invObj),
            localL: BABYLON.Vector3.TransformCoordinates(worldL, invObj)
        };
    }

    _createIKTarget(side, localPos) {
        const ctrlKey = `ikController${side}`;
        if (this[ctrlKey]) { try { this[ctrlKey].dispose(); } catch(e){} this[ctrlKey] = null; }
        const targetKey = `grabIKTarget${side}`;
        let node = this[targetKey];
        if (!node) {
            node = new BABYLON.TransformNode(`grab_ik_target_${side.toLowerCase()}`, this.scene);
            node.parent = this.upperRoot;
            this[targetKey] = node;
        }
        node.position.copyFrom(localPos);
        const bone = side === 'R' ? this.lowerarmBoneR : this.lowerarmBoneL;
        const pole = side === 'R' ? this.upperarmBoneR : this.upperarmBoneL;
        this[ctrlKey] = new BABYLON.BoneIKController(this.mesh, bone, {
            targetMesh: node,
            poleTargetBone: pole,
            poleAngle: 0,
            bendAxis: new BABYLON.Vector3(0,0,1),
            slerpAmount: 0.95
        });
    }

    _disableObjectCollisions() {
        if (!this.grabTargetBody?.shape) return;
        this.savedCollisionMask = this.grabTargetBody.shape.filterCollideMask;
        this.grabTargetBody.shape.filterCollideMask &= ~(1 << 2);
    }

    _enableObjectCollisions() {
        if (!this.grabTargetBody?.shape || this.savedCollisionMask === null) return;
        this.grabTargetBody.shape.filterCollideMask = this.savedCollisionMask;
        this.savedCollisionMask = null;
    }

    releaseGrab() {
        if (!this.isGrabbing) return;
        this._cleanupGrab();
    }

    throwObject(targetWorldPoint) {
        if (!this.isGrabbing || !this.grabTargetBody) return false;
        const characterPos = this.upperRoot.getAbsolutePosition();
        const direction = targetWorldPoint.subtract(characterPos);
        if (direction.length() < 0.01) return false;
        direction.normalize();
        const body = this.grabTargetBody;
        const mass = body.mass || 1;
        const baseForce = 8.0;
        const force = baseForce * mass;
        if (this.grabMode === 'light') {
            const animName = mass < 5 ? 'Throw Object' : 'OverhandThrow';
            this.animManager.startAction(animName, false, () => {
                if (this.grabTargetBody) this._applyThrowImpulse(direction, force);
                this._cleanupGrab();
            });
            this.stamina.consume(3 + Math.min(mass, 5));
            return true;
        }
        this._applyThrowImpulse(direction, force);
        this._cleanupGrab();
        return true;
    }

    _applyThrowImpulse(direction, force) {
        if (!this.grabTargetBody) return;
        const body = this.grabTargetBody;
        body.applyImpulse(direction.scale(force), this.targetObject.getAbsolutePosition());
        const torque = BABYLON.Vector3.Cross(direction, BABYLON.Axis.Y).normalize().scale(force * 0.15);
        body.applyTorque(torque);
    }

    _cleanupGrab() {
        this._enableObjectCollisions();
        if (this.targetObject && this.savedMeshTag) {
            this.targetObject.metadata.tag = this.savedMeshTag;
        }
        this.savedMeshTag = null;

        if (this.grabIKTargetR) this.grabIKTargetR.position.set(0,0,0);
        if (this.grabIKTargetL) this.grabIKTargetL.position.set(0,0,0);

        this.movement.movement.maxWalkSpeed = this.savedMaxWalk;
        this.movement.movement.maxRunSpeed = this.savedMaxRun;
        this.isHeavyGrabbing = false;
        this.state = 'idle';
        this.isGrabbing = false;
        this.grabMode = null;
        this.springActive = false;
        this.grabOffsetLocalR = null;
        this.grabOffsetLocalL = null;
        this.targetObject = null;
        this.grabTargetBody = null;
    }

    _checkObjectProximity() {
        if (!this.targetObject || !this.movement) return;
        const charPos = this.upperRoot.getAbsolutePosition();
        const dist = this._distanceToAABBWorld(charPos, this.targetObject);
        const minDist = 0.3; // метры
        if (dist < minDist) {
            // Масштабируем скорость: при dist=0 -> 0.3, при dist>=minDist -> 1.0
            const factor = Math.max(0.3, dist / minDist);
            this.movement.movement.maxWalkSpeed = this.savedMaxWalk * factor;
            this.movement.movement.maxRunSpeed = this.savedMaxRun * factor;
        } else {
            this.movement.movement.maxWalkSpeed = this.savedMaxWalk;
            this.movement.movement.maxRunSpeed = this.savedMaxRun;
        }
    }

    _updateGrabbing(deltaTime) {
        if (!this.isGrabbing || !this.targetObject || !this.grabTargetBody) {
            this._silentRelease();
            return;
        }
        if (this.grabMode === 'heavy') {
            const toObj = this.targetObject.getAbsolutePosition().subtract(this.upperRoot.getAbsolutePosition()).normalize();
            const desiredAngle = Math.atan2(toObj.x, toObj.z);
            let diff = desiredAngle - this.movement.worldRotation;
            while (diff > Math.PI) diff -= 2*Math.PI;
            while (diff < -Math.PI) diff += 2*Math.PI;
            const turn = Math.max(-1.5 * deltaTime, Math.min(1.5 * deltaTime, diff));
            this.movement.worldRotation += turn;
            this.movement.targetWorldRotation = this.movement.worldRotation;

            // Адаптивное замедление при приближении объекта
            this._checkObjectProximity();
        } else if (this.grabMode === 'medium') {
            const objWorldPos = this.targetObject.getAbsolutePosition();
            const toObj = objWorldPos.subtract(this.upperRoot.getAbsolutePosition()).normalize();
            const forward = new BABYLON.Vector3(Math.sin(this.movement.worldRotation), 0, Math.cos(this.movement.worldRotation));
            const angle = Math.acos(BABYLON.Vector3.Dot(toObj, forward));
            if (angle > Math.PI / 3) {
                const desiredAngle = Math.atan2(toObj.x, toObj.z);
                let diff = desiredAngle - this.movement.worldRotation;
                while (diff > Math.PI) diff -= 2*Math.PI;
                while (diff < -Math.PI) diff += 2*Math.PI;
                const turn = Math.max(-2.0 * deltaTime, Math.min(2.0 * deltaTime, diff));
                this.movement.worldRotation += turn;
                this.movement.targetWorldRotation = this.movement.worldRotation;
            }
        }

        const ikSpeed = this.grabMode === 'medium' ? 12.0 : (this.grabMode === 'heavy' ? 15.0 : this.ikSpeedBase);
        const handSens = this.grabMode === 'medium' ? this.handSensitivityBase * 2.5 : this.handSensitivityBase;

        if (this.grabMode === 'light') {
            this.handAngles.theta += this.mouseDelta.x * handSens;
            this.handAngles.phi   += this.mouseDelta.y * handSens;
            this.handAngles.phi   = Math.max(0.2, Math.min(Math.PI - 0.2, this.handAngles.phi));
            this.handAngles.theta = Math.max(-Math.PI/2, Math.min(Math.PI/2, this.handAngles.theta));
            const center = new BABYLON.Vector3(0.15, 1.35, 0.20);
            const rx = 0.6, ry = 0.6, rz = 0.6;
            const localTarget = new BABYLON.Vector3(
                center.x + rx * Math.sin(this.handAngles.phi) * Math.sin(this.handAngles.theta),
                center.y + ry * Math.cos(this.handAngles.phi),
                center.z + rz * Math.sin(this.handAngles.phi) * Math.cos(this.handAngles.theta)
            );
            BABYLON.Vector3.SmoothToRef(this.grabIKTargetR.position, localTarget, ikSpeed, deltaTime, this.grabIKTargetR.position);
        } else if (this.grabMode === 'medium') {
            this.handAngles.theta += this.mouseDelta.x * handSens;
            this.handAngles.phi   += this.mouseDelta.y * handSens;
            this.handAngles.phi   = Math.max(0.2, Math.min(Math.PI - 0.2, this.handAngles.phi));
            this.handAngles.theta = Math.max(-1.5, Math.min(1.5, this.handAngles.theta));
            const center = new BABYLON.Vector3(0.15, 1.35, 0.20);
            const rx = 1.0, ry = 1.0, rz = 1.0;
            const mouseOffset = new BABYLON.Vector3(
                rx * Math.sin(this.handAngles.phi) * Math.sin(this.handAngles.theta),
                ry * Math.cos(this.handAngles.phi),
                rz * Math.sin(this.handAngles.phi) * Math.cos(this.handAngles.theta)
            ).subtract(center);
            const maxOffset = 1.2;
            if (mouseOffset.length() > maxOffset) mouseOffset.normalize().scaleInPlace(maxOffset);
            const targetR = this.grabOffsetLocalR.add(mouseOffset);
            const targetL = this.grabOffsetLocalL.add(mouseOffset);
            BABYLON.Vector3.SmoothToRef(this.grabIKTargetR.position, targetR, ikSpeed, deltaTime, this.grabIKTargetR.position);
            BABYLON.Vector3.SmoothToRef(this.grabIKTargetL.position, targetL, ikSpeed, deltaTime, this.grabIKTargetL.position);
        } else if (this.grabMode === 'heavy') {
            if (this.grabOffsetLocalR && this.grabOffsetLocalL) {
                BABYLON.Vector3.SmoothToRef(this.grabIKTargetR.position, this.grabOffsetLocalR, ikSpeed, deltaTime, this.grabIKTargetR.position);
                BABYLON.Vector3.SmoothToRef(this.grabIKTargetL.position, this.grabOffsetLocalL, ikSpeed, deltaTime, this.grabIKTargetL.position);
            }
        }

        if (this.ikControllerR) this.ikControllerR.update();
        if (this.ikControllerL) this.ikControllerL.update();

        if (!this.springActive && this.grabIKTargetR) {
            const handWorldR = this.grabIKTargetR.getAbsolutePosition();
            const objMatrix = this.targetObject.getWorldMatrix();
            const targetWorldR = BABYLON.Vector3.TransformCoordinates(this.grabPointLocalR, objMatrix);
            const distR = BABYLON.Vector3.Distance(handWorldR, targetWorldR);
            let activate = distR < 0.15;
            if (this.grabMode !== 'light' && this.grabIKTargetL) {
                const handWorldL = this.grabIKTargetL.getAbsolutePosition();
                const targetWorldL = BABYLON.Vector3.TransformCoordinates(this.grabPointLocalL, objMatrix);
                const distL = BABYLON.Vector3.Distance(handWorldL, targetWorldL);
                activate = activate && distL < 0.15;
            }
            if (activate) {
                this.springActive = true;
                if (this.grabMode === 'heavy') {
                    this.springStiffness = 800;
                    this.springDamping = 50;
                    this.maxGrabSpeed = 2.0;
                } else if (this.grabMode === 'medium') {
                    this.springStiffness = 400;
                    this.springDamping = 60;
                    this.maxGrabSpeed = 3.0;
                } else {
                    this.springStiffness = 200;
                    this.springDamping = 25;
                    this.maxGrabSpeed = 5.0;
                }
            }
        }

        if (this.springActive && this.grabTargetBody) this._applySpringForces(deltaTime);

        if (this.movement.movementState.isMoving) {
            let rate = this.grabMode === 'heavy' ? 4.0 : (this.grabMode === 'medium' ? 2.0 : 1.0);
            if (this.stamina) {
                this.stamina.consume(rate * deltaTime);
                if (this.stamina.currentStamina <= 0) this._silentRelease();
            }
        }

        if (this.grabMode === 'heavy') {
            const bodyPos = this.targetObject.getAbsolutePosition();
            const halfHeight = this.targetObject.getBoundingInfo().boundingBox.extendSize.y;
            const groundY = this.movement.groundY;
            const objectBottom = bodyPos.y - halfHeight;
            if (objectBottom > groundY + 0.05) {
                const vel = this.grabTargetBody.getLinearVelocity() || BABYLON.Vector3.Zero();
                this.grabTargetBody.applyForce(new BABYLON.Vector3(0, -this.downForceStrength * 0.8, 0).subtract(vel.scale(5)), this.targetObject.getAbsolutePosition());
            }
        }
    }

    _applySpringForces(deltaTime) {
        const body = this.grabTargetBody;
        const objMatrix = this.targetObject.getWorldMatrix();
        const worldGrabR = BABYLON.Vector3.TransformCoordinates(this.grabPointLocalR, objMatrix);
        const handWorldR = this.grabIKTargetR.getAbsolutePosition();
        let stiffness = this.springStiffness;
        let damping = this.springDamping;
        let maxSpeed = this.maxGrabSpeed;

        const errorR = handWorldR.subtract(worldGrabR);
        let totalForce = errorR.scale(stiffness);

        if (this.grabMode !== 'light' && this.grabIKTargetL) {
            const worldGrabL = BABYLON.Vector3.TransformCoordinates(this.grabPointLocalL, objMatrix);
            const handWorldL = this.grabIKTargetL.getAbsolutePosition();
            const errorL = handWorldL.subtract(worldGrabL);
            totalForce.addInPlace(errorL.scale(stiffness * 0.5));
        }

        const vel = body.getLinearVelocity() || BABYLON.Vector3.Zero();
        totalForce.subtractInPlace(vel.scale(damping));

        if (this.grabMode === 'medium') {
            totalForce.y += (body.mass || 1) * 9.81 * 1.1;
        }

        if (totalForce.length() > 0.01) {
            body.applyForce(totalForce, this.targetObject.getAbsolutePosition());
            const speed = vel.length();
            if (speed > maxSpeed) body.setLinearVelocity(vel.normalize().scale(maxSpeed));
        }

        const angVel = body.getAngularVelocity() || BABYLON.Vector3.Zero();
        const angDamping = this.grabMode === 'heavy' ? 0.3 : 0.9;
        body.setAngularVelocity(angVel.scale(angDamping));

        const halfHeight = this.targetObject.getBoundingInfo().boundingBox.extendSize.y;
        const groundY = this.movement.groundY;
        const pos = this.targetObject.position;
        if (pos.y - halfHeight < groundY) pos.y = groundY + halfHeight;

        if (this.grabMode === 'heavy') {
            const forward = new BABYLON.Vector3(Math.sin(this.movement.worldRotation), 0, Math.cos(this.movement.worldRotation));
            const sideVel = vel.subtract(forward.scale(BABYLON.Vector3.Dot(vel, forward)));
            if (sideVel.length() > 0.05) body.applyForce(sideVel.scale(-20), this.targetObject.getAbsolutePosition());
        }
    }

    _silentRelease() { this._cleanupGrab(); }

    _setDefend(active) {
        if (active && !this._defendActive) {
            this.animManager.setForcedUpper('Defend');
            this._defendActive = true;
        } else if (!active && this._defendActive) {
            this.animManager.setForcedUpper(null);
            this._defendActive = false;
        }
    }

    startDefend(objectMesh) {
        if (this._isRagdollActive() || this.state !== 'idle' || this.isGrabbing) return;
        this.state = 'defending';
        this.targetObject = objectMesh;
        this._setDefend(true);
    }

    stopDefend() {
        if (this.state !== 'defending') return;
        this.state = 'idle';
        this.targetObject = null;
        this._setDefend(false);
        this.cooldownTimer = this.defendCooldown;
    }

    update(deltaSec, mouseButtons, mouseX, mouseY, mouseDeltaX, mouseDeltaY) {
        if (this._isRagdollActive()) {
            if (this.isGrabbing) this._silentRelease();
            return;
        }
        if (this.weaponSystem?.isActive) {
            if (this.isGrabbing) this.releaseGrab();
            return;
        }
        if (this.cooldownTimer > 0) this.cooldownTimer -= deltaSec;
        this.mouseDelta.set(mouseDeltaX, mouseDeltaY);

        if (this.isGrabbing) {
            this._updateGrabbing(deltaSec);
            return;
        }

        if (this.state === 'idle') {
            const nearby = this._getAllNearby();
            for (const entry of nearby) {
                if (this.isMovingTowardsDynamic(entry.mesh)) {
                    this.startDefend(entry.mesh);
                    break;
                }
            }
        } else if (this.state === 'defending') {
            const still = this.targetObject && this.isMovingTowardsDynamic(this.targetObject) && this._isObjectNearby(this.targetObject);
            if (!still) this.stopDefend();
        }
    }

    getSpeedMultiplier() {
        if (this.state === 'defending') return 0.7;
        if (this.isGrabbing) {
            if (this.grabMode === 'light') return 0.8;
            if (this.grabMode === 'medium') return 0.6;
            return 0.5;
        }
        return 1.0;
    }

    dispose() {
        if (this.isGrabbing) this.releaseGrab();
        if (this.grabIKTargetR) this.grabIKTargetR.dispose();
        if (this.grabIKTargetL) this.grabIKTargetL.dispose();
        if (this.ikControllerR) try { this.ikControllerR.dispose(); } catch(e){}
        if (this.ikControllerL) try { this.ikControllerL.dispose(); } catch(e){}
    }
}


================================================================================
ФАЙЛ: episodicMemory.js
================================================================================

// js/episodicMemory.js
export class EpisodicMemory {
    constructor() {
        this.events = [];
        this.maxEvents = 100;
    }

    addEvent(type, data = {}) {
        const event = {
            type,
            ...data,
            timestamp: performance.now(),
            decayRate: type === 'damage' ? 0.005 : 0.1, // негатив затухает очень медленно (полужизнь ~2.5 мин)
            weight: 1.0
        };
        this.events.push(event);
        if (this.events.length > this.maxEvents) {
            this.events.shift();
        }
        console.log(`[EpisodicMemory] event: ${type}, zone: ${data.zone}, force: ${data.force?.toFixed(1)}`);
    }

    update(deltaSec) {
        const now = performance.now();
        for (let i = this.events.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            const ev = this.events[i];
            const age = (now - ev.timestamp) / 1000;
            ev.weight = Math.exp(-ev.decayRate * age);
            if (ev.weight < 0.05) {
                this.events.splice(i, 1);
            }
        }
    }

    getFearBonus(subtype = null) {
        let bonus = 0;
        for (const ev of this.events) {
            if (ev.type !== 'damage') continue;
            if (subtype && ev.subtype !== subtype) continue;
            bonus += ev.force * 0.1 * ev.weight;
        }
        return Math.min(1.0, bonus);
    }

    getRecentDamage(zone = null) {
        let total = 0;
        for (const ev of this.events) {
            if (ev.type !== 'damage') continue;
            if (zone && ev.zone !== zone) continue;
            total += ev.force * ev.weight;
        }
        return total;
    }
}


================================================================================
ФАЙЛ: fogEnemy.js
================================================================================

// js/fogEnemy.js
export class FogEnemy {
    constructor(scene, skeleton, mesh, animationGroups, initialPosition) {
        this.scene = scene;
        this.skeleton = skeleton;
        this.mesh = mesh;
        this.animGroups = animationGroups;
        this.position = initialPosition.clone();
        this.health = 100;
        this.maxHealth = 100;
        this.speed = 2.5;
        this.attackRange = 1.8;
        this.attackDamage = 15;
        this.attackCooldown = 1.2;
        this.lastAttackTime = 0;
        this.state = 'idle';

        // Полупрозрачный меш
        this.mesh.isVisible = true;           // теперь видим
        this.mesh.position = this.position;
        this.mesh.material = this.createPhantomMaterial();
        this.mesh.receiveShadows = false;

        // Корневой узел для перемещения
        this.rootNode = new BABYLON.TransformNode("enemyRoot", scene);
        this.rootNode.position = this.position;

        // Анимации
        this.idleAnim   = this._findAnim('idle');
        this.walkAnim   = this._findAnim('walk');
        this.attackAnim = this._findAnim('attack') || this._findAnim('sword') || this._findAnim('hit');
        this.hurtAnim   = this._findAnim('hit') || this._findAnim('damage');
        this.deathAnim  = this._findAnim('death');

        this.currentAnim = null;
        if (this.idleAnim) {
            this.idleAnim.start(true);
            this.currentAnim = this.idleAnim;
        }

        // Система частиц (три типа)
        this.particlesData = [];
        this.initAdvancedParticles();
    }

    _findAnim(keyword) {
        return this.animGroups.find(ag => ag.name.toLowerCase().includes(keyword));
    }

    createPhantomMaterial() {
        const mat = new BABYLON.StandardMaterial("phantomMat", this.scene);
        mat.diffuseColor  = new BABYLON.Color3(0.05, 0.05, 0.08);
        mat.specularColor = BABYLON.Color3.Black();
        mat.alpha = 0.25;
        mat.emissiveColor = new BABYLON.Color3(0.02, 0.02, 0.04);
        mat.backFaceCulling = false;
        return mat;
    }

    initAdvancedParticles() {
        const bones = this.skeleton.bones;
        const particlesPerBone = 20; // базовое количество частиц каждого типа на кость

        // Создаём шаблоны для SPS
        const templateLarge = BABYLON.MeshBuilder.CreateSphere("tplLarge", { diameter: 0.2 }, this.scene);
        const templateMedium = BABYLON.MeshBuilder.CreateSphere("tplMedium", { diameter: 0.1 }, this.scene);
        const templateSmall = BABYLON.MeshBuilder.CreateBox("tplSmall", { size: 0.06 }, this.scene);
        templateLarge.isVisible = false;
        templateMedium.isVisible = false;
        templateSmall.isVisible = false;

        this.sps = new BABYLON.SolidParticleSystem("fogEnemySPS", this.scene, { updatable: true });

        // Добавляем частицы разных типов для каждой кости
        for (let i = 0; i < bones.length; i++) {
            const bone = bones[i];
            // Пропускаем концевые кости (пальцы и т.п.), если их много
            const name = bone.name.toLowerCase();
            const isImportant = !name.includes('finger') && !name.includes('thumb') && !name.includes('toe');

            const countMult = isImportant ? 1.0 : 0.3;
            const numLarge  = Math.floor(particlesPerBone * 0.15 * countMult);
            const numMedium = Math.floor(particlesPerBone * 0.35 * countMult);
            const numSmall  = Math.floor(particlesPerBone * 0.5  * countMult);

            for (let j = 0; j < numLarge; j++) {
                this.sps.addShape(templateLarge, 1);
                this.particlesData.push({ boneIndex: i, type: 'large', offset: this._randomOffset(0.25), oscSpeed: 0.5 + Math.random() * 1.5, oscAmp: 0.02 + Math.random() * 0.04 });
            }
            for (let j = 0; j < numMedium; j++) {
                this.sps.addShape(templateMedium, 1);
                this.particlesData.push({ boneIndex: i, type: 'medium', offset: this._randomOffset(0.2), oscSpeed: 1.0 + Math.random() * 2.5, oscAmp: 0.03 + Math.random() * 0.06 });
            }
            for (let j = 0; j < numSmall; j++) {
                this.sps.addShape(templateSmall, 1);
                this.particlesData.push({ boneIndex: i, type: 'small', offset: this._randomOffset(0.15), oscSpeed: 2.0 + Math.random() * 4.0, oscAmp: 0.01 + Math.random() * 0.03 });
            }
        }

        templateLarge.dispose();
        templateMedium.dispose();
        templateSmall.dispose();

        this.sps.buildMesh();
        this.spsMesh = this.sps.mesh;
        this.spsMesh.material = this.createParticleMaterial();

        this.updateParticles(0);
        this.sps.setParticles();
    }

    _randomOffset(maxRadius) {
        return new BABYLON.Vector3(
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * maxRadius,
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * maxRadius,
            (Math.random() - 0.5) * maxRadius
        );
    }

    createParticleMaterial() {
        // Используем текстуру с мягким пятном для всех частиц
        const mat = new BABYLON.StandardMaterial("particleMat", this.scene);
        mat.diffuseTexture = new BABYLON.Texture("https://assets.babylonjs.com/textures/flare.png", this.scene);
        mat.emissiveColor = new BABYLON.Color3(0.2, 0.25, 0.4);
        mat.alpha = 0.4;
        mat.disableLighting = true;
        mat.backFaceCulling = false;
        mat.needDepthPrePass = false;
        return mat;
    }

    updateParticles(deltaTime) {
        const time = performance.now() * 0.001;
        const particles = this.sps.particles;
        const bones = this.skeleton.bones;
        const data = this.particlesData;

        for (let i = 0; i < particles.length; i++) {
            const p = particles[i];
            const d = data[i];
            const bone = bones[d.boneIndex];
            if (!bone) continue;

            const boneWorld = bone.getAbsolutePosition(this.mesh);
            const osc = Math.sin(time * d.oscSpeed + i) * d.oscAmp;
            const offset = d.offset.clone().scale(1 + osc * 5);
            p.position.copyFrom(boneWorld).addInPlace(offset);

            // Разные цвета/размеры в зависимости от типа
            if (d.type === 'large') {
                p.color = new BABYLON.Color4(0.2, 0.25, 0.4, 0.3);
                p.scale.setAll(0.8 + Math.random() * 0.3);
            } else if (d.type === 'medium') {
                p.color = new BABYLON.Color4(0.3, 0.4, 0.6, 0.5);
                p.scale.setAll(0.5 + Math.random() * 0.4);
            } else {
                // small spark
                p.color = new BABYLON.Color4(0.5, 0.6, 0.8, 0.7);
                p.scale.setAll(0.3 + Math.random() * 0.4);
            }
        }
        this.sps.setParticles();
    }

    setState(newState) {
        if (this.state === newState) return;
        this.state = newState;

        if (this.currentAnim) {
            this.currentAnim.stop();
        }

        switch (newState) {
            case 'idle': this.currentAnim = this.idleAnim; break;
            case 'walk': this.currentAnim = this.walkAnim; break;
            case 'attack':
                this.currentAnim = this.attackAnim;
                if (this.attackAnim) {
                    this.attackAnim.start(false);
                    this.attackAnim.onAnimationGroupEndObservable.addOnce(() => {
                        this.setState('walk');
                    });
                }
                break;
            case 'hurt':
                this.currentAnim = this.hurtAnim;
                if (this.hurtAnim) {
                    this.hurtAnim.start(false);
                    this.hurtAnim.onAnimationGroupEndObservable.addOnce(() => {
                        this.setState('walk');
                    });
                }
                break;
            case 'dead':
                this.currentAnim = this.deathAnim;
                if (this.deathAnim) {
                    this.deathAnim.start(false);
                    this.deathAnim.onAnimationGroupEndObservable.addOnce(() => {
                        this.dispose();
                    });
                } else {
                    // Нет анимации смерти — просто исчезаем через 1 сек
                    setTimeout(() => this.dispose(), 1000);
                }
                break;
        }

        if (this.currentAnim && !['attack', 'hurt', 'dead'].includes(newState)) {
            this.currentAnim.start(true);
        }
    }

    updateBehavior(playerPosition, deltaTime) {
        if (this.health <= 0) {
            if (this.state !== 'dead') this.setState('dead');
            return;
        }

        const dist = BABYLON.Vector3.Distance(this.position, playerPosition);
        const now = performance.now() / 1000;

        if (dist > 15) {
            if (this.state !== 'idle') this.setState('idle');
            return;
        }

        if (dist <= this.attackRange) {
            if (this.state !== 'attack' && this.state !== 'hurt') {
                if (now - this.lastAttackTime > this.attackCooldown) {
                    this.lastAttackTime = now;
                    this.setState('attack');
                    if (this.onAttack) this.onAttack(this.attackDamage);
                }
            }
        } else if (dist <= 15) {
            if (this.state !== 'walk') this.setState('walk');
            const direction = playerPosition.subtract(this.position).normalize();
            this.position.addInPlace(direction.scale(this.speed * deltaTime));
            this.rootNode.position = this.position;
            this.mesh.position = this.position;
            const angle = Math.atan2(direction.x, direction.z);
            this.rootNode.rotation.y = angle;
            this.mesh.rotation.y = angle;
        }
    }

    takeDamage(amount) {
        this.health -= amount;
        if (this.health <= 0) {
            this.setState('dead');
        } else {
            this.setState('hurt');
        }
        // Визуальная вспышка
        if (this.spsMesh?.material) {
            this.spsMesh.material.emissiveColor = new BABYLON.Color3(0.8, 0.2, 0.2);
            setTimeout(() => {
                if (this.spsMesh?.material) {
                    this.spsMesh.material.emissiveColor = new BABYLON.Color3(0.2, 0.25, 0.4);
                }
            }, 200);
        }
    }

    getPosition() {
        return this.rootNode.position;
    }

    dispose() {
        if (this.sps) this.sps.dispose();
        if (this.spsMesh) this.spsMesh.dispose();
        if (this.mesh) this.mesh.dispose();
        if (this.rootNode) this.rootNode.dispose();
    }
}

ФАЙЛ: healthSystem.js

// js/healthSystem.js
export class HealthSystem {
    constructor(staminaSystem, options = {}) {
        this.stamina = staminaSystem;
        this.maxHealth = options.maxHealth ?? 100;
        this.currentHealth = options.currentHealth ?? this.maxHealth;
        this.healthRegenRate = options.healthRegenRate ?? 2;
        this.healthRegenStaminaThreshold = 0.8;
        this.damageResistanceByZone = {
            head: 1.5,
            torso: 1.0,
            arm: 0.8,
            leg: 0.7,
            pelvis: 1.2,
            other: 0.9
        };
    }

    takeDamage(rawDamage, zone = 'other') {
        const multiplier = this.damageResistanceByZone[zone] || 1.0;
        const damage = rawDamage * multiplier;
        const staminaBefore = this.stamina.currentStamina;
        const staminaDamage = Math.min(staminaBefore, damage);
        this.stamina.consume(staminaDamage);
        const remaining = damage - staminaDamage;
        const healthDamage = Math.min(this.currentHealth, remaining);
        this.currentHealth = Math.max(0, this.currentHealth - healthDamage);
        console.log(`[Health] zone: ${zone}, raw: ${rawDamage.toFixed(1)}, mult: ${multiplier.toFixed(1)}, stamina dmg: ${staminaDamage.toFixed(1)}, health dmg: ${healthDamage.toFixed(1)}`);
        return { staminaDamage, healthDamage };
    }

    update(deltaSec, isInSanctuary = false) {
        const staminaRatio = this.stamina.getNormalized();
        if (staminaRatio >= this.healthRegenStaminaThreshold && this.currentHealth < this.maxHealth) {
            const rate = this.healthRegenRate * (isInSanctuary ? 3.0 : 1.0);
            this.currentHealth = Math.min(this.maxHealth, this.currentHealth + rate * deltaSec);
        }
    }

    getMaxStamina() {
        const healthRatio = Math.max(0.1, this.currentHealth / this.maxHealth);
        return this.stamina.maxStamina * healthRatio;
    }

    getHealthNormalized() {
        return this.currentHealth / this.maxHealth;
    }

    isDead() {
        return this.currentHealth <= 0;
    }
}

// js/hostileAI.js
export class HostileCubeAI {
    constructor(mesh, scene, characterRoot) {
        this.mesh = mesh;
        this.scene = scene;
        this.characterRoot = characterRoot;
        this.state = 'idle';
        this.attackRange = 2.5;
        this.chaseRange = 8.0;
        this.speed = 2.0;
        this.attackDamage = 15;
        this.telegraphTime = 0.6;
        this.attackCooldown = 1.2;
        this._timer = 0;
        this._telegraphProgress = 0;
        this._attackDealt = false;

        if (!mesh.physicsBody) {
            new BABYLON.PhysicsAggregate(mesh, BABYLON.PhysicsShapeType.BOX, { mass: 20, restitution: 0.2, friction: 0.8 }, scene);
        }

        this.mesh.metadata = this.mesh.metadata || {};
        this.mesh.metadata.subtype = this.mesh.metadata.subtype || 'angular';
        this.mesh.metadata.attackPattern = this.mesh.metadata.attackPattern || 'swipe_left';
        this.mesh.metadata.damageZone = this.mesh.metadata.damageZone || 'head';
        this.mesh.metadata.fearIntensity = this.mesh.metadata.fearIntensity || 6;
        this.mesh.metadata.isAttacking = false;
        this.mesh.metadata.telegraphProgress = 0;
    }

    update(deltaSec) {
        if (!this.characterRoot) return;
        const charPos = this.characterRoot.getAbsolutePosition();
        const myPos = this.mesh.getAbsolutePosition();
        const dist = BABYLON.Vector3.Distance(charPos, myPos);

        switch (this.state) {
            case 'idle':
                if (dist <= this.chaseRange) {
                    this.state = 'chase';
                    console.log(`[HostileAI] ${this.mesh.name} entering chase`);
                }
                break;
            case 'chase':
                if (dist > this.chaseRange * 1.2) {
                    this.state = 'idle';
                    this._stopMove();
                    console.log(`[HostileAI] ${this.mesh.name} returning to idle`);
                } else if (dist <= this.attackRange) {
                    this.state = 'attack';
                    this._timer = 0;
                    this._telegraphProgress = 0;
                    this._attackDealt = false;
                    this._stopMove();
                    console.log(`[HostileAI] ${this.mesh.name} beginning attack telegraph`);
                } else {
                    const dir = charPos.subtract(myPos).normalize();
                    if (this.mesh.physicsBody) {
                        this.mesh.physicsBody.setLinearVelocity(dir.scale(this.speed));
                    } else {
                        this.mesh.position.addInPlace(dir.scale(this.speed * deltaSec));
                    }
                    this.mesh.lookAt(charPos);
                }
                break;
            case 'attack':
                this._timer += deltaSec;
                if (!this._attackDealt) {
                    this._telegraphProgress = Math.min(1.0, this._timer / this.telegraphTime);
                    this.mesh.metadata.isAttacking = true;
                    this.mesh.metadata.telegraphProgress = this._telegraphProgress;
                    if (!this._telegraphVisual) {
                        this._telegraphVisual = true;
                        this.mesh.scaling.setAll(1.3);
                    }
                    if (this._timer >= this.telegraphTime) {
                        this._dealDamage();
                        this._attackDealt = true;
                        this._timer = 0;
                        this.mesh.scaling.setAll(0.7);
                        console.log(`[HostileAI] ${this.mesh.name} attack hit!`);
                    }
                } else {
                    this._timer += deltaSec;
                    if (this._timer >= 0.2) {
                        this.state = 'cooldown';
                        this._timer = 0;
                        this.mesh.metadata.isAttacking = false;
                        this.mesh.metadata.telegraphProgress = 0;
                        this.mesh.scaling.setAll(1.0);
                        this._telegraphVisual = false;
                    }
                }
                break;
            case 'cooldown':
                this._timer += deltaSec;
                if (this._timer >= this.attackCooldown) {
                    this.state = 'idle';
                    this._timer = 0;
                    console.log(`[HostileAI] ${this.mesh.name} cooldown finished`);
                }
                break;
        }
    }

    _dealDamage() {
        const myPos = this.mesh.getAbsolutePosition();
        const damageZone = this.mesh.metadata.damageZone || 'head';
        const bodies = this.scene.getPhysicsEngine()?.getBodies();
        if (!bodies) return;

        // Собираем подходящие по тэгу тела, отсортированные по расстоянию до врага
        const candidates = [];
        for (const body of bodies) {
            if (body.tag !== 'ragdollPart') continue;
            const bodyPos = body.transformNode.getAbsolutePosition();
            const dist = BABYLON.Vector3.Distance(myPos, bodyPos);
            if (dist < this.attackRange + 1.0) {
                // Определяем зону тела по имени кости (как в ReactionSystem)
                const boneName = body.transformNode.name.toLowerCase();
                let zone = 'other';
                if (boneName.includes('head')) zone = 'head';
                else if (boneName.includes('neck')) zone = 'neck';
                else if (boneName.includes('spine') || boneName.includes('chest') || boneName.includes('pelvis')) zone = 'torso';
                else if (boneName.includes('thigh') || boneName.includes('calf') || boneName.includes('foot')) zone = 'leg';
                else if (boneName.includes('upperarm') || boneName.includes('lowerarm') || boneName.includes('hand')) zone = 'arm';
                candidates.push({ body, bodyPos, dist, zone });
            }
        }
        if (candidates.length === 0) return;

        // Предпочитаем кости, чья зона совпадает с damageZone врага
        const preferred = candidates.filter(c => c.zone === damageZone);
        const target = preferred.length > 0 ? preferred[0] : candidates.reduce((a, b) => a.dist < b.dist ? a : b);

        const dir = target.bodyPos.subtract(myPos).normalize();
        const impulse = dir.scale(this.attackDamage * 0.8);
        target.body.applyImpulse(impulse, target.bodyPos);

        if (this.scene.reactionSystem) {
            this.scene.reactionSystem.registerHit({
                force: this.attackDamage,
                point: target.bodyPos,
                normal: dir,
                bodyPart: target.body.transformNode.name,
                body: target.body
            });
        }
        console.log(`[HostileAI] dealt ${this.attackDamage} damage to ${target.body.transformNode.name} (zone: ${target.zone})`);
    }

    _stopMove() {
        if (this.mesh.physicsBody) {
            this.mesh.physicsBody.setLinearVelocity(BABYLON.Vector3.Zero());
        }
    }

    dispose() {}
}
