// js/movement.js
export class MovementSystem {
    constructor(root, capsuleRadius, capsuleHeight, scene, globalScene) {
        this.root = root;
        this.scene = scene;
        this.globalScene = globalScene || scene;
        this.position = new BABYLON.Vector3(0, 0, 0);
        this.inputLocked = false;
        this.movement = {
            forward: false, backward: false, left: false, right: false,
            speed: 0, targetSpeed: 0,
            maxWalkSpeed: 2.2, maxRunSpeed: 5.6,
            acceleration: 8.0, deceleration: 6.0,
            isRunning: false, isCrouching: false
        };
        this.worldRotation = 0;
        this.targetWorldRotation = 0;
        this.movementState = {
            isMoving: false,
            hasVelocity: false,
            moveX: 0, moveY: 0,
            isRunning: false, isCrouching: false
        };
        this.capsuleRadius = capsuleRadius;
        this.capsuleHeight = capsuleHeight;

        this.lowSpeedThreshold = 4.5;
        this.mediumSpeedThreshold = 10.0;

        this.lockedDirection = null;
        this.collisionResponseHandler = null;
        this.isReactingToCollision = false;
        this.collisionCapsuleMesh = null;
        this.slideRotationSpeed = 5.0;

        this.groundY = 0;
        this.currentYOffset = 0;
        this.targetYOffset = 0;
        this.stepHeightMin = 0.15;
        this.stepHeightMax = 0.5;
        this.stepCheckDistance = 0.8;
        this.stepSpeed = 3.0;
        this.isSteppingUp = false;
        this.onLargeStep = null;

        // Поля для внешнего воздействия (автономное поведение)
        this.externalBias = new BABYLON.Vector3(0, 0, 0);
        this.externalSpeedLimit = 1.0;

        // Флаг, что персонаж выполняет уклонение/отступление
        this.isEvading = false;
    }

    setInputLock(locked) {
        this.inputLocked = locked;
        if (locked) {
            this.movement.forward = false;
            this.movement.backward = false;
            this.movement.left = false;
            this.movement.right = false;
            this.movement.isRunning = false;
            this.movement.isCrouching = false;
            this.updateTargetMovement();
        }
    }

    handleMovementInput(inputMap) {
        if (this.inputLocked) return;
        this.movement.forward = !!(inputMap['w'] || inputMap['W'] || inputMap['ArrowUp']);
        this.movement.backward = !!(inputMap['s'] || inputMap['S'] || inputMap['ArrowDown']);
        this.movement.left = !!(inputMap['a'] || inputMap['A'] || inputMap['ArrowLeft']);
        this.movement.right = !!(inputMap['d'] || inputMap['D'] || inputMap['ArrowRight']);
        this.movement.isRunning = !!(inputMap['Shift'] || inputMap['ShiftLeft'] || inputMap['ShiftRight']);
        this.movement.isCrouching = !!(inputMap['Control'] || inputMap['Ctrl'] || inputMap['ControlLeft']);

        // Ограничение движения при тяжёлом захвате
        if (this.globalScene?.dim?.isHeavyGrabbing && this.globalScene.dim.targetObject) {
            const dim = this.globalScene.dim;
            const objPos = dim.targetObject.getAbsolutePosition();
            const toObj = objPos.subtract(this.position).normalize();

            let inputX = 0, inputY = 0;
            if (this.movement.forward) inputY += 1;
            if (this.movement.backward) inputY -= 1;
            if (this.movement.left) inputX -= 1;
            if (this.movement.right) inputX += 1;

            if (inputX !== 0 || inputY !== 0) {
                const inputDir = new BABYLON.Vector3(inputX, 0, inputY).normalize();
                const dot = BABYLON.Vector3.Dot(inputDir, toObj);
                if (dot <= 0.15) {
                    this.movement.forward = false;
                    this.movement.backward = false;
                    this.movement.left = false;
                    this.movement.right = false;
                }
            }
        }

        this.updateTargetMovement();
    }

    updateTargetMovement() {
        let dx = 0, dy = 0;
        if (this.movement.forward) dy += 1;
        if (this.movement.backward) dy -= 1;
        if (this.movement.left) dx -= 1;
        if (this.movement.right) dx += 1;
        const len = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
        if (len > 0) {
            dx /= len;
            dy /= len;
            this.targetWorldRotation = Math.atan2(dx, dy);
            this.movement.targetSpeed = this.movement.isCrouching
                ? this.movement.maxWalkSpeed * 0.5
                : (this.movement.isRunning ? this.movement.maxRunSpeed : this.movement.maxWalkSpeed);
            this.movementState.isMoving = true;
            this.movementState.moveX = dx;
            this.movementState.moveY = dy;
        } else {
            this.movement.targetSpeed = 0;
            this.movementState.isMoving = false;
            this.movementState.moveX = 0;
            this.movementState.moveY = 0;
        }
        this.movementState.isRunning = this.movement.isRunning;
        this.movementState.isCrouching = this.movement.isCrouching;
    }

    _getHorizontalCheckPosition() {
        const checkPos = this.position.clone();
        checkPos.y = this.groundY + this.capsuleHeight * 0.5;
        return checkPos;
    }

    raycastObstacle(moveDelta) {
        const checkPos = this._getHorizontalCheckPosition();
        const direction = moveDelta.clone().normalize();
        const distance = moveDelta.length();
        const radius = this.capsuleRadius;

        const ray = new BABYLON.Ray(checkPos, direction, distance + radius);
        const pickInfo = this.scene.pickWithRay(ray, mesh => {
            if (!mesh.isVisible || !mesh.getBoundingInfo) return false;
            const tag = mesh.metadata?.tag;
            return tag === "static";
        });

        if (pickInfo.hit && pickInfo.pickedPoint) {
            const normal = pickInfo.getNormal(true);
            const upDot = BABYLON.Vector3.Dot(normal, BABYLON.Axis.Y);
            if (upDot > 0.7) {
                return { hit: false };
            }
            const hitDistance = BABYLON.Vector3.Distance(checkPos, pickInfo.pickedPoint) - radius;
            return {
                hit: true,
                distance: Math.max(0, hitDistance),
                point: pickInfo.pickedPoint,
                normal: normal
            };
        }
        return { hit: false };
    }

    raycastStep(moveDelta) {
        if (!this.movementState.isMoving || this.movement.speed < 0.1) return null;
        const dir = moveDelta.clone().normalize();
        const start = this._getHorizontalCheckPosition().add(new BABYLON.Vector3(0, 0.2, 0));
        const end = start.add(dir.scale(this.stepCheckDistance));
        end.y -= this.stepHeightMax;
        const ray = new BABYLON.Ray(start, end.subtract(start).normalize(), this.stepCheckDistance);
        const pickInfo = this.scene.pickWithRay(ray, mesh => {
            if (!mesh.isVisible) return false;
            const tag = mesh.metadata?.tag;
            return tag === "static" || tag === "dynamic";
        });
        if (pickInfo.hit && pickInfo.pickedPoint) {
            const normal = pickInfo.getNormal(true);
            const upDot = BABYLON.Vector3.Dot(normal, BABYLON.Axis.Y);
            if (upDot < 0.6) return null;
            const deltaY = pickInfo.pickedPoint.y - this.groundY;
            return { point: pickInfo.pickedPoint, deltaY, normal };
        }
        return null;
    }

    getSlideDirection(moveDir, normal) {
        const dot = BABYLON.Vector3.Dot(moveDir, normal);
        if (dot >= 0) return moveDir.clone().normalize();
        const slide = moveDir.subtract(normal.scale(dot));
        const len = slide.length();
        return len > 0.001 ? slide.scale(1 / len) : BABYLON.Vector3.Zero();
    }

    _checkSideClearance(direction, angleOffsetDeg) {
        const angleRad = angleOffsetDeg * Math.PI / 180;
        const cos = Math.cos(angleRad);
        const sin = Math.sin(angleRad);
        const leftDir = new BABYLON.Vector3(
            direction.x * cos - direction.z * sin,
            0,
            direction.x * sin + direction.z * cos
        ).normalize();
        const rightDir = new BABYLON.Vector3(
            direction.x * cos + direction.z * sin,
            0,
            -direction.x * sin + direction.z * cos
        ).normalize();
        const checkDist = 0.3;
        const leftHit = this.raycastObstacle(leftDir.scale(checkDist));
        const rightHit = this.raycastObstacle(rightDir.scale(checkDist));
        return !(leftHit.hit || rightHit.hit);
    }

    applyMovementWithCollisionAvoidance(moveDelta, deltaSec) {
        let collision = this.raycastObstacle(moveDelta);
        if (!collision.hit) {
            this.position.addInPlace(moveDelta);
            this.lockedDirection = null;
            return { impacted: false };
        }

        const speed = this.movement.speed;
        const tier = this.getSpeedTier(speed);
        const safeDistance = Math.max(0, collision.distance - 0.15);

        if (tier === 'low') {
            const safeMove = moveDelta.clone().normalize().scale(safeDistance);
            this.position.addInPlace(safeMove);

            let remaining = moveDelta.length() - safeDistance;
            let slideDirection = null;
            if (remaining > 0.01) {
                slideDirection = this.getSlideDirection(moveDelta, collision.normal);
                let slideDelta = slideDirection.scale(remaining);
                let iterations = 3;
                while (iterations-- > 0 && slideDelta.length() > 0.001) {
                    const slideCollision = this.raycastObstacle(slideDelta);
                    if (!slideCollision.hit) {
                        this.position.addInPlace(slideDelta);
                        break;
                    }
                    const slideSafeDist = Math.max(0, slideCollision.distance - 0.15);
                    const slideSafeMove = slideDelta.clone().normalize().scale(slideSafeDist);
                    this.position.addInPlace(slideSafeMove);
                    remaining = slideDelta.length() - slideSafeDist;
                    if (remaining <= 0.01) break;
                    const newSlideDir = this.getSlideDirection(slideDelta, slideCollision.normal);
                    slideDelta = newSlideDir.scale(remaining);
                    slideDirection = newSlideDir;
                }
            }
            if (slideDirection && slideDirection.length() > 0.1) {
                this.alignRotationToSlideDirection(slideDirection, deltaSec);
            }
            this.lockedDirection = collision.normal.clone();
            return { impacted: false };
        }
        else if (tier === 'medium') {
            const safeMove = moveDelta.clone().normalize().scale(safeDistance);
            this.position.addInPlace(safeMove);
            return {
                impacted: true,
                collisionData: collision,
                remainingDelta: moveDelta.length() - safeDistance
            };
        }
        else {
            this.position.addInPlace(moveDelta);
            return {
                impacted: true,
                collisionData: collision,
                remainingDelta: 0
            };
        }
    }

    alignRotationToSlideDirection(slideDir, deltaSec) {
        const targetAngle = Math.atan2(slideDir.x, slideDir.z);
        let diff = targetAngle - this.worldRotation;
        while (diff > Math.PI) diff -= 2 * Math.PI;
        while (diff < -Math.PI) diff += 2 * Math.PI;
        const maxRotation = this.slideRotationSpeed * deltaSec;
        const rotationDelta = Math.max(-maxRotation, Math.min(maxRotation, diff));
        this.worldRotation += rotationDelta;
        this.targetWorldRotation = this.worldRotation;
    }

    getSpeedTier(speed) {
        if (speed <= this.lowSpeedThreshold) return 'low';
        if (speed <= this.mediumSpeedThreshold) return 'medium';
        return 'high';
    }

    applyInputLock(inputVector) {
        if (!this.lockedDirection) return inputVector;
        const dot = BABYLON.Vector3.Dot(inputVector, this.lockedDirection);
        if (dot < 0) {
            inputVector.subtractInPlace(this.lockedDirection.scale(dot));
        }
        return inputVector;
    }

    setKinematicColliderActive(active) {
        if (this.collisionCapsuleMesh) {
            this.collisionCapsuleMesh.isVisible = active;
            if (this.collisionCapsuleMesh.physicsBody) {
                this.collisionCapsuleMesh.physicsBody.setEnabled(active);
            }
        }
    }

    updateGroundY() {
        const ray = new BABYLON.Ray(
            this.position.clone().add(new BABYLON.Vector3(0, 0.5, 0)),
            BABYLON.Axis.Y.negate(),
            2.0
        );
        const pick = this.scene.pickWithRay(ray, mesh => {
            if (!mesh.isVisible) return false;
            const tag = mesh.metadata?.tag;
            return tag === "static" || tag === "dynamic" || mesh.name === "ground";
        });
        this.groundY = pick.hit ? pick.pickedPoint.y : 0;
    }

    checkForStep(moveDelta) {
        if (!this.movementState.isMoving || this.movement.speed < 0.1) {
            this.targetYOffset = 0;
            return;
        }

        const stepInfo = this.raycastStep(moveDelta);
        if (stepInfo) {
            const { deltaY, point } = stepInfo;
            if (deltaY > this.stepHeightMin && deltaY <= this.stepHeightMax) {
                if (!this.isSteppingUp && this.onLargeStep) {
                    this.isSteppingUp = true;
                    const foot = this.getLeadingFoot();
                    this.onLargeStep(point, deltaY, foot);
                    setTimeout(() => { this.isSteppingUp = false; }, 800);
                }
            } else if (deltaY > 0.05 && deltaY <= this.stepHeightMin) {
                this.targetYOffset = deltaY;
            } else {
                this.targetYOffset = 0;
            }
        } else {
            this.targetYOffset = 0;
        }
    }

    getLeadingFoot() {
        const moveX = this.movementState.moveX;
        const moveY = this.movementState.moveY;
        return (moveY > 0 || moveX > 0) ? 'right' : 'left';
    }

    update(deltaTimeMs, speedFactor = 1.0, collisionCapsuleMesh = null) {
        const deltaSec = deltaTimeMs / 1000;
        const effectiveTargetSpeed = this.movement.targetSpeed * speedFactor * this.externalSpeedLimit;
        if (Math.abs(effectiveTargetSpeed - this.movement.speed) > 0.01) {
            const accel = effectiveTargetSpeed > this.movement.speed ? this.movement.acceleration : this.movement.deceleration;
            this.movement.speed = BABYLON.Scalar.Lerp(this.movement.speed, effectiveTargetSpeed, accel * deltaSec);
        } else {
            this.movement.speed = effectiveTargetSpeed;
        }
        this.movementState.hasVelocity = Math.abs(this.movement.speed) > 0.01;

        if (!this.lockedDirection) {
            if (Math.abs(this.targetWorldRotation - this.worldRotation) > 0.01) {
                let diff = this.targetWorldRotation - this.worldRotation;
                while (diff > Math.PI) diff -= 2 * Math.PI;
                while (diff < -Math.PI) diff += 2 * Math.PI;
                this.worldRotation += diff * 4.0 * deltaSec;
            }
        }

        const moveDist = this.movement.speed * deltaSec;
        let moveDelta = new BABYLON.Vector3(
            Math.sin(this.worldRotation) * moveDist,
            0,
            Math.cos(this.worldRotation) * moveDist
        );

        // Применяем внешнее смещение (автономное поведение)
        if (this.externalBias.length() > 0.001) {
            moveDelta.addInPlace(this.externalBias.scale(deltaSec * 3.0));
            this.externalBias.scaleInPlace(0.95); // затухание
        }

        if (this.lockedDirection) {
            const testCollision = this.raycastObstacle(moveDelta.clone().normalize().scale(0.3));
            const sideClear = this._checkSideClearance(moveDelta.clone().normalize(), 30);
            if (!testCollision.hit && sideClear) {
                this.lockedDirection = null;
            } else {
                const adjusted = this.applyInputLock(moveDelta.clone());
                moveDelta = adjusted;
            }
        }

        const result = this.applyMovementWithCollisionAvoidance(moveDelta, deltaSec);

        if (result.impacted && this.collisionResponseHandler && !this.isReactingToCollision) {
            const tier = this.getSpeedTier(this.movement.speed);
            if (tier === 'high') {
                this.setKinematicColliderActive(false);
            }
            this.collisionResponseHandler.handleImpact(result.collisionData, this.movement.speed, tier);
            this.inputLocked = true;
            this.isReactingToCollision = true;
        }

        this.updateGroundY();
        this.checkForStep(moveDelta);

        if (Math.abs(this.currentYOffset - this.targetYOffset) > 0.01) {
            const step = Math.sign(this.targetYOffset - this.currentYOffset) * this.stepSpeed * deltaSec;
            this.currentYOffset += step;
            if (Math.abs(this.currentYOffset - this.targetYOffset) < 0.01) {
                this.currentYOffset = this.targetYOffset;
            }
        }

        const newY = this.groundY + this.currentYOffset;
        if (newY > this.position.y) {
            const upRay = new BABYLON.Ray(this.position, BABYLON.Axis.Y, newY - this.position.y + 0.1);
            const pick = this.scene.pickWithRay(upRay, mesh => {
                if (!mesh.isVisible) return false;
                const tag = mesh.metadata?.tag;
                return tag === "static" || tag === "dynamic";
            });
            if (pick.hit) {
                this.currentYOffset = pick.pickedPoint.y - this.groundY - this.capsuleHeight;
                if (this.currentYOffset < 0) this.currentYOffset = 0;
            }
        }
        this.position.y = this.groundY + this.currentYOffset;

        if (this.root) {
            this.root.position.copyFrom(this.position);
            this.root.rotation.y = this.worldRotation;
        }
    }

    getMovementState() {
        return this.movementState;
    }

    reset() {
        this.position.set(0, 0, 0);
        this.worldRotation = 0;
        this.targetWorldRotation = 0;
        this.movement.speed = 0;
        this.movement.targetSpeed = 0;
        this.setInputLock(false);
        this.resetCollisionState();
        this.setKinematicColliderActive(true);
        this.currentYOffset = 0;
        this.targetYOffset = 0;
        this.externalBias.setAll(0);
        this.externalSpeedLimit = 1.0;
        this.isEvading = false;
    }

    resetCollisionState() {
        this.lockedDirection = null;
        this.inputLocked = false;
        this.isReactingToCollision = false;
    }
}

// js/perceivedCondition.js
export class PerceivedCondition {
    constructor(healthSystem, staminaSystem) {
        this.health = healthSystem;
        this.stamina = staminaSystem;
        this.perceivedHealth = healthSystem.currentHealth;
        this.perceivedStamina = staminaSystem.currentStamina;
        this._driftSpeed = 3.0; // ускорено
        this._psychFactors = {
            head: 1.8,
            torso: 1.2,
            arm: 0.9,
            leg: 0.8,
            pelvis: 1.3
        };
    }

    applyDamage(realDamage, zone, fearLevel, memoryBonus) {
        const factor = this._psychFactors[zone] || 1.0;
        const perceivedDamage = realDamage * (1 + fearLevel * 0.5 + memoryBonus * 0.3) * factor; // чуть снижено
        this.perceivedHealth = Math.max(0, this.perceivedHealth - perceivedDamage);
        this.perceivedStamina = Math.max(0, this.perceivedStamina - perceivedDamage);
        console.log(`[Perceived] real dmg ${realDamage.toFixed(1)} (${zone}) -> felt ${perceivedDamage.toFixed(1)}, health felt ${this.perceivedHealth.toFixed(1)}`);
    }

    update(deltaSec) {
        // Основной дрейф к реальному здоровью
        this.perceivedHealth += (this.health.currentHealth - this.perceivedHealth) * this._driftSpeed * deltaSec;
        this.perceivedStamina += (this.stamina.currentStamina - this.perceivedStamina) * this._driftSpeed * deltaSec;

        // Психологическая адаптация: ускоренный рост ощущаемого здоровья, если реальное выше
        if (this.perceivedHealth < this.health.currentHealth - 0.05 * this.health.maxHealth) {
            this.perceivedHealth += 1.5 * deltaSec; // усилено
        }
    }

    getHealthRatio() {
        return this.perceivedHealth / this.health.maxHealth;
    }

    getStaminaRatio() {
        const maxStamina = this.health.getMaxStamina();
        return this.perceivedStamina / maxStamina;
    }

    isLowHealth() {
        return this.getHealthRatio() < 0.25;
    }

    isLowStamina() {
        return this.getStaminaRatio() < 0.25;
    }
}

// js/perceptionSystem.js
// Сканирует окружение и собирает данные об объектах с метаданными.
// Добавлена категория "projectile" для быстрых угроз.

export class PerceptionSystem {
    constructor(scene, characterRoot) {
        this.scene = scene;
        this.characterRoot = characterRoot;
        this.nearRadius = 3.0;
        this.mediumRadius = 10.0;
        this.farRadius = 20.0; // для projectiles (летят быстро, нужно замечать заранее)
        this.updateInterval = 0.1; // секунды
        this._timer = 0;
        this.snapshot = null; // PerceptionSnapshot
    }

    /**
     * @param {number} deltaSec
     * @returns {PerceptionSnapshot|undefined} новый снапшот, если был обновлён
     */
    update(deltaSec) {
        this._timer += deltaSec;
        if (this._timer < this.updateInterval) return undefined;
        this._timer = 0;

        const charPos = this.characterRoot.getAbsolutePosition();
        const threats = [];
        const projectiles = [];
        const sanctuaries = [];
        const relics = [];
        const mirrors = [];
        const voids = [];
        const fogCovers = [];

        for (const mesh of this.scene.meshes) {
            if (!mesh.isVisible || !mesh.metadata) continue;
            const tag = mesh.metadata.tag;
            if (!tag) continue;

            const pos = mesh.getAbsolutePosition();
            const dist = BABYLON.Vector3.Distance(charPos, pos);
            const dir = pos.subtract(charPos).normalize();

            switch (tag) {
                case 'hostile':
                    if (dist <= this.mediumRadius) {
                        threats.push({
                            mesh,
                            distance: dist,
                            direction: dir,
                            subtype: mesh.metadata.subtype || 'angular',
                            attackPattern: mesh.metadata.attackPattern || 'any',
                            damageZone: mesh.metadata.damageZone || 'any',
                            fearIntensity: mesh.metadata.fearIntensity || 5,
                            telegraphProgress: mesh.metadata.telegraphProgress ?? 0,
                            isAttacking: mesh.metadata.isAttacking || false
                        });
                    }
                    break;

                case 'projectile':
                    if (dist <= this.farRadius) {
                        const vel = mesh.physicsBody ? mesh.physicsBody.getLinearVelocity() : BABYLON.Vector3.Zero();
                        const speed = vel.length();
                        const threatDir = speed > 0.1 ? vel.normalize() : dir;
                        projectiles.push({
                            mesh,
                            distance: dist,
                            direction: threatDir,
                            speed,
                            radius: mesh.getBoundingInfo ? mesh.getBoundingInfo().boundingBox.extendSize.x : 0.3
                        });
                    }
                    break;

                case 'sanctuary':
                    if (dist <= this.mediumRadius) {
                        sanctuaries.push({
                            mesh,
                            distance: dist,
                            direction: dir,
                            comfortLevel: mesh.metadata.comfortLevel || 0.5
                        });
                    }
                    break;

                case 'relic':
                    if (dist <= this.mediumRadius) {
                        relics.push({ mesh, distance: dist, direction: dir, memoryType: mesh.metadata.memoryType });
                    }
                    break;

                case 'mirror':
                    if (dist <= this.mediumRadius) {
                        mirrors.push({
                            mesh,
                            distance: dist,
                            direction: dir,
                            selfAwarenessBoost: mesh.metadata.selfAwarenessBoost || 0.2
                        });
                    }
                    break;

                case 'void':
                    if (dist <= this.mediumRadius) {
                        voids.push({
                            mesh,
                            distance: dist,
                            direction: dir,
                            edgeDirection: mesh.metadata.edgeDirection || dir.scale(-1)
                        });
                    }
                    break;

                case 'fog_cover':
                    if (dist <= this.mediumRadius) {
                        fogCovers.push({ mesh, distance: dist, direction: dir, density: mesh.metadata.density || 0.5 });
                    }
                    break;
            }
        }

        this.snapshot = {
            threats,
            projectiles,
            sanctuaries,
            relics,
            mirrors,
            voids,
            fogCovers,
            timestamp: performance.now()
        };
        return this.snapshot;
    }

    dispose() {
        this.snapshot = null;
    }
}


================================================================================
ФАЙЛ: projectileSpawner.js

// js/projectileSpawner.js
export class ProjectileSpawner {
    constructor(scene, targetRoot, options = {}) {
        this.scene = scene;
        this.targetRoot = targetRoot;
        this.spawnInterval = options.spawnInterval ?? 2.0;
        this.projectileSpeed = options.projectileSpeed ?? 15;  // увеличена скорость
        this.projectileMass = options.projectileMass ?? 2.0;
        this.projectileRadius = options.projectileRadius ?? 0.3;
        this.spawnDistance = options.spawnDistance ?? 6.0;      // уменьшена дистанция, чтобы быстрее долетать
        this._timer = 0;
        this.projectiles = [];
    }

    update(deltaSec) {
        this._timer += deltaSec;
        if (this._timer >= this.spawnInterval) {
            this._timer -= this.spawnInterval;
            this.spawnProjectile();
        }
        for (let i = this.projectiles.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            const p = this.projectiles[i];
            if (!p.mesh || p.mesh.isDisposed()) {
                this.projectiles.splice(i, 1);
                continue;
            }
            if (BABYLON.Vector3.Distance(p.mesh.position, this.targetRoot.getAbsolutePosition()) > 40) {
                p.mesh.dispose();
                this.projectiles.splice(i, 1);
            }
        }
    }

    spawnProjectile() {
        if (!this.targetRoot) return;
        const targetPos = this.targetRoot.getAbsolutePosition();
        const angle = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
        // Спавним чуть выше и вокруг персонажа
        const spawnPos = targetPos.add(new BABYLON.Vector3(
            Math.cos(angle) * this.spawnDistance,
            3 + Math.random() * 2,   // высота 3-5 метров
            Math.sin(angle) * this.spawnDistance
        ));

        const mesh = BABYLON.MeshBuilder.CreateSphere("projectile", { diameter: this.projectileRadius * 2 }, this.scene);
        mesh.position = spawnPos;
        const mat = new BABYLON.StandardMaterial("projMat", this.scene);
        mat.diffuseColor = new BABYLON.Color3(1, 1, 0);
        mat.emissiveColor = new BABYLON.Color3(0.8, 0.4, 0);
        mesh.material = mat;
        mesh.metadata = { tag: "projectile" };

        const aggregate = new BABYLON.PhysicsAggregate(mesh, BABYLON.PhysicsShapeType.SPHERE, { mass: this.projectileMass, restitution: 0.3, friction: 0.2 }, this.scene);
        aggregate.body.tag = "projectile";

        const toTarget = targetPos.subtract(spawnPos);
        const dir = toTarget.normalize();
        aggregate.body.setLinearVelocity(dir.scale(this.projectileSpeed));



        aggregate.body.setCollisionCallbackEnabled(true);
        aggregate.body.getCollisionObservable().add((collisionEvent) => {
            const other = collisionEvent.collidedAgainst;
            if (other?.tag === "ragdollPart" && this.scene.reactionSystem) {
                this.scene.reactionSystem.registerHit({
                    force: collisionEvent.impulse ?? 5,
                    point: collisionEvent.point,
                    normal: collisionEvent.normal || BABYLON.Vector3.Zero(),
                    bodyPart: other.transformNode?.name || 'unknown',
                    body: other
                });
                console.log(`[Projectile] hit ${other.transformNode.name}`);
            }
            setTimeout(() => {
                mesh.dispose();
            }, 100);
        });

        this.projectiles.push({ mesh, aggregate });
    }

    dispose() {
        this.projectiles.forEach(p => p.mesh.dispose());
        this.projectiles = [];
    }
}

// js/ragdollPhysics.js
import { STATE_NAMES } from './stateMachine.js';

/**
 * Возвращает угловые ограничения для кости по её имени.
 * @param {string} boneName - Имя кости.
 * @returns {Array} Массив ограничений { axis, min, max } в радианах.
 */
function getAngularLimits(boneName) {
    const name = boneName.toLowerCase();

    if (name.includes('root') || name.includes('pelvis')) {
        return [
            { axis: 0, min: -3 * Math.PI / 180, max: 3 * Math.PI / 180 },
            { axis: 1, min: -8 * Math.PI / 180, max: 8 * Math.PI / 180 },
            { axis: 2, min: -3 * Math.PI / 180, max: 3 * Math.PI / 180 }
        ];
    }
    if (name.includes('spine_01')) {
        return [
            { axis: 0, min: -6 * Math.PI / 180, max: 6 * Math.PI / 180 },
            { axis: 1, min: -10 * Math.PI / 180, max: 10 * Math.PI / 180 },
            { axis: 2, min: -6 * Math.PI / 180, max: 6 * Math.PI / 180 }
        ];
    }
    if (name.includes('spine_02')) {
        return [
            { axis: 0, min: -8 * Math.PI / 180, max: 8 * Math.PI / 180 },
            { axis: 1, min: -12 * Math.PI / 180, max: 12 * Math.PI / 180 },
            { axis: 2, min: -8 * Math.PI / 180, max: 8 * Math.PI / 180 }
        ];
    }
    if (name.includes('spine_03')) {
        return [
            { axis: 0, min: -10 * Math.PI / 180, max: 10 * Math.PI / 180 },
            { axis: 1, min: -15 * Math.PI / 180, max: 15 * Math.PI / 180 },
            { axis: 2, min: -10 * Math.PI / 180, max: 10 * Math.PI / 180 }
        ];
    }
    if (name.includes('neck_01')) {
        return [
            { axis: 0, min: -15 * Math.PI / 180, max: 15 * Math.PI / 180 },
            { axis: 1, min: -30 * Math.PI / 180, max: 30 * Math.PI / 180 },
            { axis: 2, min: -20 * Math.PI / 180, max: 20 * Math.PI / 180 }
        ];
    }
    if (name.includes('head')) {
        return [
            { axis: 0, min: -10 * Math.PI / 180, max: 10 * Math.PI / 180 },
            { axis: 1, min: -20 * Math.PI / 180, max: 20 * Math.PI / 180 },
            { axis: 2, min: -15 * Math.PI / 180, max: 15 * Math.PI / 180 }
        ];
    }
    if (name.includes('clavicle')) {
        return [
            { axis: 0, min: -8 * Math.PI / 180, max: 8 * Math.PI / 180 },
            { axis: 1, min: -15 * Math.PI / 180, max: 15 * Math.PI / 180 },
            { axis: 2, min: -10 * Math.PI / 180, max: 10 * Math.PI / 180 }
        ];
    }
    // Плечо – большой диапазон, но с ограничением вращения вокруг продольной оси
    if (name.includes('upperarm')) {
        return [
            { axis: 0, min: -70 * Math.PI / 180, max: 70 * Math.PI / 180 },
            { axis: 1, min: -50 * Math.PI / 180, max: 50 * Math.PI / 180 },
            { axis: 2, min: -40 * Math.PI / 180, max: 40 * Math.PI / 180 }
        ];
    }
    // Локоть – только сгибание (ось Z) и очень маленькие остальные движения
    if (name.includes('lowerarm')) {
        return [
            { axis: 0, min: 0, max: 0 },
            { axis: 1, min: 0, max: 0 },
            { axis: 2, min: 0, max: 130 * Math.PI / 180 } // сгибание от 0 до 130°
        ];
    }
    // Запястье – небольшие углы
    if (name.includes('hand')) {
        return [
            { axis: 0, min: -15 * Math.PI / 180, max: 15 * Math.PI / 180 },
            { axis: 1, min: -20 * Math.PI / 180, max: 20 * Math.PI / 180 },
            { axis: 2, min: -15 * Math.PI / 180, max: 15 * Math.PI / 180 }
        ];
    }
    // Пальцы – минимальная подвижность
    if (name.includes('middle_01') || name.includes('index_01') || name.includes('pinky_01') || name.includes('ring_01') || name.includes('thumb_01')) {
        return [
            { axis: 0, min: -5 * Math.PI / 180, max: 5 * Math.PI / 180 },
            { axis: 1, min: -5 * Math.PI / 180, max: 5 * Math.PI / 180 },
            { axis: 2, min: 0, max: 30 * Math.PI / 180 }
        ];
    }
    // Бедро – хорошая подвижность, но без вывихов
    if (name.includes('thigh')) {
        return [
            { axis: 0, min: -70 * Math.PI / 180, max: 70 * Math.PI / 180 },
            { axis: 1, min: -35 * Math.PI / 180, max: 35 * Math.PI / 180 },
            { axis: 2, min: -25 * Math.PI / 180, max: 25 * Math.PI / 180 }
        ];
    }
    // Колено – только сгибание
    if (name.includes('calf')) {
        return [
            { axis: 0, min: 0, max: 120 * Math.PI / 180 },
            { axis: 1, min: 0, max: 0 },
            { axis: 2, min: 0, max: 0 }
        ];
    }
    // Стопа – небольшие углы
    if (name.includes('foot')) {
        return [
            { axis: 0, min: -20 * Math.PI / 180, max: 20 * Math.PI / 180 },
            { axis: 1, min: -15 * Math.PI / 180, max: 15 * Math.PI / 180 },
            { axis: 2, min: -10 * Math.PI / 180, max: 10 * Math.PI / 180 }
        ];
    }
    // Пальцы ног – минимальные
    if (name.includes('ball')) {
        return [
            { axis: 0, min: -10 * Math.PI / 180, max: 10 * Math.PI / 180 },
            { axis: 1, min: -5 * Math.PI / 180, max: 5 * Math.PI / 180 },
            { axis: 2, min: -5 * Math.PI / 180, max: 5 * Math.PI / 180 }
        ];
    }

    // Для остальных костей – почти полная блокировка
    return [
        { axis: 0, min: -1 * Math.PI / 180, max: 1 * Math.PI / 180 },
        { axis: 1, min: -1 * Math.PI / 180, max: 1 * Math.PI / 180 },
        { axis: 2, min: -1 * Math.PI / 180, max: 1 * Math.PI / 180 }
    ];
}

export function buildRagdollConfig(skeleton, skinnedMesh) {
    const positions = skinnedMesh.getVerticesData(BABYLON.VertexBuffer.PositionKind);
    const weights = skinnedMesh.getVerticesData(BABYLON.VertexBuffer.MatricesWeightsKind);
    const indices = skinnedMesh.getVerticesData(BABYLON.VertexBuffer.MatricesIndicesKind);
    if (!positions || !weights || !indices) throw new Error("Нет данных весов");

    const vertices = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < positions.length; i += 3) {
        vertices.push(new BABYLON.Vector3(positions[i], positions[i + 1], positions[i + 2]));
    }

    const boneVerts = new Array(skeleton.bones.length).fill().map(() => []);
    for (let i = 0; i < weights.length; i += 4) {
        let maxW = 0, maxIdx = -1;
        for (let j = 0; j < 4; j++) {
            const w = weights[i + j];
            const idx = indices[i + j];
            if (w > maxW && idx < skeleton.bones.length) {
                maxW = w;
                maxIdx = idx;
            }
        }
        if (maxIdx >= 0) boneVerts[maxIdx].push(i / 4);
    }

    const rootBone = skeleton.bones.find(b => !b.parent) || skeleton.bones[0];
    const configs = [];

    skeleton.bones.forEach((bone, idx) => {
        const verts = boneVerts[idx] || [];
        const invMatrix = bone.getAbsoluteMatrix().clone().invert();
        let min = new BABYLON.Vector3(Infinity, Infinity, Infinity);
        let max = new BABYLON.Vector3(-Infinity, -Infinity, -Infinity);

        if (verts.length === 0) {
            min = new BABYLON.Vector3(-0.08, -0.08, -0.08);
            max = new BABYLON.Vector3(0.08, 0.08, 0.08);
        } else {
            verts.forEach(vIdx => {
                const worldPos = vertices[vIdx];
                const localPos = BABYLON.Vector3.TransformCoordinates(worldPos, invMatrix);
                min = BABYLON.Vector3.Minimize(min, localPos);
                max = BABYLON.Vector3.Maximize(max, localPos);
            });
        }

        const size = max.subtract(min);
        const SCALE = 0.45;
        let w = Math.max(size.x * SCALE, 0.12);
        let h = Math.max(size.y * SCALE, 0.12);
        let d = Math.max(size.z * SCALE, 0.12);

        const boneName = bone.name.toLowerCase();
        if (boneName.includes('head')) w = h = d = 0.18;
        if (boneName.includes('hand')) { w = 0.1; h = 0.1; d = 0.08; }
        if (boneName.includes('foot')) { w = 0.15; h = 0.08; d = 0.2; }
        if (boneName.includes('clavicle')) { w = 0.15; h = 0.08; d = 0.15; }

        // Массы подобраны для устойчивости: таз самый тяжёлый, конечности легче
        let mass;
        if (bone === rootBone) mass = 60;    // таз/корень
        else if (boneName.includes('pelvis')) mass = 50;
        else if (boneName.includes('spine_01') || boneName.includes('spine_02') || boneName.includes('spine_03')) mass = 15;
        else if (boneName.includes('thigh')) mass = 8;
        else if (boneName.includes('upperarm')) mass = 6;
        else if (boneName.includes('calf')) mass = 4;
        else if (boneName.includes('lowerarm')) mass = 3;
        else if (boneName.includes('hand')) mass = 1;
        else if (boneName.includes('foot')) mass = 1.5;
        else if (boneName.includes('head')) mass = 3;
        else mass = Math.min(10, Math.max(1, w * h * d * 600));

        const angularLimits = getAngularLimits(bone.name);

        configs.push({
            bones: [bone.name],
            mass,
            width: w,
            height: h,
            depth: d,
            angularLimits
        });
    });

    if (!configs.some(cfg => cfg.bones.includes(rootBone.name))) {
        configs.push({
            bones: [rootBone.name],
            mass: 60,
            width: 0.35,
            height: 0.35,
            depth: 0.35,
            angularLimits: getAngularLimits(rootBone.name)
        });
    }

    return { configs, rootBone };
}

export function stabilizeBodies(boneBodyMap) {
    boneBodyMap.forEach(body => {
        body.setLinearDamping?.(0.9);
        body.setAngularDamping?.(0.9);
        body.setSleepThreshold?.(0.2);

        const shape = body.shape;
        if (shape) {
            shape.filterMembershipMask = 1 << 2;
            shape.filterCollideMask = -1;
        }

        body.tag = "ragdollPart";
        body.setCollisionCallbackEnabled(true);
    });
}

export function createClavicleStabilizer(boneBodyMap, scene) {
    const leftClav = boneBodyMap.get('clavicle_l');
    const rightClav = boneBodyMap.get('clavicle_r');
    if (!leftClav || !rightClav) {
        console.warn("Ключицы не найдены, стабилизатор не создан");
        return;
    }
    if (typeof leftClav.addConstraint !== 'function') {
        console.warn("Метод addConstraint не найден. Возможно, используется устаревшая версия физики.");
        return;
    }

    const posL = leftClav.transformNode.getAbsolutePosition();
    const posR = rightClav.transformNode.getAbsolutePosition();
    const restLength = BABYLON.Vector3.Distance(posL, posR);
    const minDistance = restLength * 0.9;
    const maxDistance = restLength * 1.1;
    const stiffness = 200;
    const damping = 15;

    try {
        const constraint = new BABYLON.Physics6DoFConstraint(
            { pivotA: BABYLON.Vector3.Zero(), pivotB: BABYLON.Vector3.Zero() },
            [{
                axis: BABYLON.PhysicsConstraintAxis.LINEAR_DISTANCE,
                minLimit: minDistance,
                maxLimit: maxDistance,
                stiffness: stiffness,
                damping: damping
            }],
            scene
        );
        try { constraint.isCollisionsEnabled = false; } catch (e) {}
        leftClav.addConstraint(rightClav, constraint);
        console.log(`Стабилизатор ключиц создан, длина = ${restLength.toFixed(3)}`);
    } catch (e) {
        console.error("Ошибка при создании стабилизатора ключиц:", e);
    }
}

export function createSpringConstraint(bodyA, bodyB, pivotA, pivotB, config, scene) {
    if (!bodyA || !bodyB || typeof bodyA.addConstraint !== 'function') {
        console.warn("createSpringConstraint: addConstraint не доступен");
        return null;
    }
    const { stiffness = 400, damping = 50, restLength = null, minDistance = 0, maxDistance = Infinity, collisionEnabled = false } = config;
    try {
        if (restLength === null) {
            const posA = bodyA.transformNode.getAbsolutePosition();
            const posB = bodyB.transformNode.getAbsolutePosition();
            restLength = BABYLON.Vector3.Distance(posA, posB);
        }
        const constraint = new BABYLON.Physics6DoFConstraint(
            { pivotA: pivotA || BABYLON.Vector3.Zero(), pivotB: pivotB || BABYLON.Vector3.Zero() },
            [
                { axis: BABYLON.PhysicsConstraintAxis.LINEAR_X, minLimit: 0, maxLimit: 0, stiffness, damping },
                { axis: BABYLON.PhysicsConstraintAxis.LINEAR_Y, minLimit: 0, maxLimit: 0, stiffness, damping },
                { axis: BABYLON.PhysicsConstraintAxis.LINEAR_Z, minLimit: 0, maxLimit: 0, stiffness, damping }
            ],
            scene
        );
        constraint.isCollisionsEnabled = collisionEnabled;
        bodyA.addConstraint(bodyB, constraint);
        return constraint;
    } catch (e) {
        console.error("Ошибка при создании пружины:", e);
        return null;
    }
}

export function createDistanceSpring(bodyA, bodyB, config, scene) {
    if (!bodyA || !bodyB || typeof bodyA.addConstraint !== 'function') return null;
    const { stiffness = 500, damping = 30, restLength = null, minDistanceRatio = 0.8, maxDistanceRatio = 1.2 } = config;
    try {
        const posA = bodyA.transformNode.getAbsolutePosition();
        const posB = bodyB.transformNode.getAbsolutePosition();
        const actualRestLength = restLength || BABYLON.Vector3.Distance(posA, posB);
        const minDistance = actualRestLength * minDistanceRatio;
        const maxDistance = actualRestLength * maxDistanceRatio;
        const constraint = new BABYLON.Physics6DoFConstraint(
            { pivotA: BABYLON.Vector3.Zero(), pivotB: BABYLON.Vector3.Zero() },
            [{
                axis: BABYLON.PhysicsConstraintAxis.LINEAR_DISTANCE,
                minLimit: minDistance,
                maxLimit: maxDistance,
                stiffness,
                damping
            }],
            scene
        );
        constraint.isCollisionsEnabled = false;
        bodyA.addConstraint(bodyB, constraint);
        return constraint;
    } catch (e) {
        console.error("Ошибка при создании distance spring:", e);
        return null;
    }
}

// ==================== ReactionSystem ====================
const MIN_FORCE_THRESHOLD = 2.0; // порог силы, ниже которого удар игнорируется

export class ReactionSystem {
    constructor(markers, boneBodyMap, upTargets, root, movementSystem, stateMachine, animManager, staminaSystem, autonomousBehaviour = null, healthSystem = null) {
        this.markers = markers;
        this.boneBodyMap = boneBodyMap;
        this.upTargets = upTargets;
        this.root = root;
        this.movement = movementSystem;
        this.stateMachine = stateMachine;
        this.animManager = animManager;
        this.stamina = staminaSystem;
        this.autonomous = autonomousBehaviour;
        this.healthSystem = healthSystem;
        this.hitQueue = [];
    }

    getHitZone(boneName) {
        const name = boneName.toLowerCase();
        if (name.includes('head')) return 'head';
        if (name.includes('neck')) return 'neck';
        if (name.includes('spine') || name.includes('chest')) return 'torso';
        if (name.includes('pelvis')) return 'pelvis';
        if (name.includes('thigh') || name.includes('calf') || name.includes('foot')) return 'leg';
        if (name.includes('upperarm') || name.includes('lowerarm') || name.includes('hand')) return 'arm';
        return 'other';
    }

    registerHit(hitData) {
        this.hitQueue.push(hitData);
    }

    processHits() {
        if (this.hitQueue.length === 0) return;

        let maxForceHit = null;
        let maxForce = 0;
        for (let hit of this.hitQueue) {
            if (hit.force > maxForce) {
                maxForce = hit.force;
                maxForceHit = hit;
            }
        }
        this.hitQueue = [];

        if (!maxForceHit) return;
        const { force, point, normal, bodyPart, body } = maxForceHit;

        // Игнорируем слабые касания
        if (force < MIN_FORCE_THRESHOLD) {
            return;
        }

        const zone = this.getHitZone(bodyPart);
        console.log(`[Reaction] hit ${zone} (${bodyPart}) force ${force.toFixed(1)}`);

        if (this.healthSystem) {
            this.healthSystem.takeDamage(force, zone);
        } else if (this.stamina) {
            this.stamina.consumeByImpact(force);
        }

        this.markers.forEach(m => { if (m.material) m.material.emissiveColor = new BABYLON.Color3(1, 0, 0); });
        setTimeout(() => {
            this.markers.forEach(m => { if (m.material) m.material.emissiveColor = new BABYLON.Color3(0, 1, 0); });
        }, 100);

        const curState = this.stateMachine?.currentState?.name;
        if (curState === STATE_NAMES.KNOCKDOWN || curState === STATE_NAMES.RAGDOLL || curState === STATE_NAMES.GET_UP) return;

        // Импульс физическому телу (уменьшен)
        if (body) {
            body.applyImpulse(normal.scale(force * 0.3), point);
        }

        // Запись в автономное поведение только для значительных ударов
        if (this.autonomous && force > MIN_FORCE_THRESHOLD) {
            this.autonomous.recordDanger('generic', 'impact', zone, force);
        }

        const isLow = this.stamina?.isLow || false;
        const isExhausted = this.stamina?.isExhausted || false;

        if (isExhausted && force > 15) {
            this.stateMachine.transitionTo(STATE_NAMES.RAGDOLL_IMPACT, { direction: normal, force, contactPoint: point });
        } else if (isLow && force > 10) {
            this.stateMachine.transitionTo(STATE_NAMES.STUMBLE, { direction: normal, force, contactPoint: point });
        } else if (force > 20) {
            const hitZone = this.getHitZone(bodyPart);
            const level = force > 50 ? 3 : (force > 30 ? 2 : 1);
            this.stateMachine.transitionTo(STATE_NAMES.IMPACT, { level, hitZone, force });
        }
    }

    // --- Проверка баланса (расхождение кинематики и физики) ---
    checkBalance(markers, boneBodyMap, pdStrength) {
        // Не проверяем баланс, если персонаж выполняет уклонение/отступление
        if (this.movement?.isEvading) {
            return;
        }

        const curState = this.stateMachine?.currentState?.name;
        if (curState === STATE_NAMES.RAGDOLL ||
            curState === STATE_NAMES.RAGDOLL_IMPACT ||
            curState === STATE_NAMES.KNOCKDOWN ||
            curState === STATE_NAMES.GET_UP) {
            return;
        }

        const staminaMultiplier = this.stamina ? this.stamina.getPdStrengthMultiplier() : 1.0;
        const baseThreshold = 1.2;
        const threshold = baseThreshold * staminaMultiplier;

        const bonesToCheck = ['pelvis', 'spine_03', 'thigh_l', 'thigh_r', 'upperarm_l', 'upperarm_r'];
        let maxError = 0;
        let maxErrorDirection = new BABYLON.Vector3(0, 0, 0);
        let maxErrorBody = null;

        for (const marker of markers) {
            const boneName = marker.name.replace('marker_', '');
            if (!bonesToCheck.includes(boneName)) continue;
            const physBody = boneBodyMap.get(boneName);
            if (!physBody) continue;

            const targetPos = marker.getAbsolutePosition();
            const currentPos = physBody.transformNode.getAbsolutePosition();
            const error = targetPos.subtract(currentPos);
            const dist = error.length();

            if (dist > maxError) {
                maxError = dist;
                maxErrorDirection = error.normalize();
                maxErrorBody = physBody;
            }
        }

        if (maxError > threshold && maxErrorBody) {
            const force = maxError * 30;
            if (this.stamina) {
                this.stamina.consumeByImpact(force);
            }
            this.stateMachine.transitionTo(STATE_NAMES.RAGDOLL_IMPACT, {
                direction: maxErrorDirection,
                force: force,
                contactPoint: maxErrorBody.transformNode.getAbsolutePosition()
            });
        }
    }

    update(deltaSec) {
        this.processHits();
    }
}

ФАЙЛ: rlEnvironment.js
================================================================================

ФАЙЛ: staminaSystem.js

// js/staminaSystem.js
export class StaminaSystem {
    constructor(options = {}) {
        this.maxStamina = options.maxStamina ?? 100;
        this.currentStamina = options.initialStamina ?? this.maxStamina;

        // Расход в секунду
        this.runCost = options.runCost ?? 12;
        this.walkCost = options.walkCost ?? 0;
        this.crouchCost = options.crouchCost ?? 0;

        // Единоразовые затраты
        this.jumpCost = options.jumpCost ?? 20;
        this.attackCost = options.attackCost ?? 8;
        this.blockCost = options.blockCost ?? 6;
        this.turnCost = options.turnCost ?? 5;
        this.stumbleCost = options.stumbleCost ?? 15;

        // Восстановление в секунду
        this.regenRateIdle = options.regenRateIdle ?? 15;
        this.regenRateMoving = options.regenRateMoving ?? 5;

        // Пороги эффектов
        this.lowStaminaThreshold = options.lowStaminaThreshold ?? 25;
        this.exhaustedThreshold = options.exhaustedThreshold ?? 10;

        // Множители при низкой стамине
        this.speedMultiplierLow = 0.7;
        this.damageMultiplierLow = 0.7;
        this.pdStrengthLow = 0.7;
        this.pdStrengthExhausted = 0.5;

        // Флаги
        this.isExhausted = false;
        this.isLow = false;
    }

    update(deltaSec, isMoving, isRunning, isCrouching) {
        if (isMoving) {
            let cost = this.walkCost;
            if (isRunning) cost = this.runCost;
            else if (isCrouching) cost = this.crouchCost;
            this.currentStamina = Math.max(0, this.currentStamina - cost * deltaSec);
        }

        if (!isMoving && this.currentStamina < this.maxStamina) {
            this.currentStamina = Math.min(this.maxStamina, this.currentStamina + this.regenRateIdle * deltaSec);
        } else if (isMoving && !isRunning && this.currentStamina < this.maxStamina) {
            this.currentStamina = Math.min(this.maxStamina, this.currentStamina + this.regenRateMoving * deltaSec);
        }

        this.isLow = this.currentStamina <= this.lowStaminaThreshold;
        this.isExhausted = this.currentStamina <= this.exhaustedThreshold;
    }

    consume(amount) {
        this.currentStamina = Math.max(0, this.currentStamina - amount);
        this.isLow = this.currentStamina <= this.lowStaminaThreshold;
        this.isExhausted = this.currentStamina <= this.exhaustedThreshold;
    }

    consumeByImpact(force) {
        const impactCost = Math.min(30, force * 0.5);
        this.consume(impactCost);
    }

    canPerformAction(cost = 1) {
        return this.currentStamina >= cost;
    }

    getSpeedMultiplier() {
        if (this.isExhausted) return 0.5;
        if (this.isLow) return this.speedMultiplierLow;
        return 1.0;
    }

    getDamageMultiplier() {
        if (this.isExhausted) return 0.4;
        if (this.isLow) return this.damageMultiplierLow;
        return 1.0;
    }

    getPdStrengthMultiplier() {
        if (this.isExhausted) return this.pdStrengthExhausted;
        if (this.isLow) return this.pdStrengthLow;
        return 1.0;
    }

    getNormalized() {
        return this.currentStamina / this.maxStamina;
    }
}

ФАЙЛ: stateMachine.js

// js/stateMachine.js
export const STATE_NAMES = {
    IDLE: 'idle',
    MOVE: 'move',
    ACTION: 'action',
    IMPACT: 'impact',
    STRUGGLE: 'struggle',
    KNOCKDOWN: 'knockdown',
    GET_UP: 'getUp',
    RAGDOLL: 'ragdoll',
    DEFEND: 'defend',
    STUMBLE: 'stumble',
    RAGDOLL_IMPACT: 'ragdollImpact'
};

class State {
    constructor(name) {
        this.name = name;
        this.machine = null;
        this.lockUpper = false;
        this.lockLower = false;
        this.statePdFactor = null;
    }
    onEnter(prevState, ...args) {}
    onExit(nextState) {}
    update(deltaTime) {}
    transitionTo(newStateName, ...args) {
        if (this.machine) this.machine.transitionTo(newStateName, ...args);
    }
    getDesiredUpper() { return null; }
    getDesiredLower() { return null; }
}

export class IdleState extends State {
    onEnter(prevState) {
        if (this.machine.movementSystem) this.machine.movementSystem.setInputLock(false);
        this.statePdFactor = 1.0;
        this.machine.pdStrengthTarget = this.machine.getBasePdTarget() * this.statePdFactor;
    }
    update(deltaTime) {
        if (this.machine.movementSystem && this.machine.movementSystem.getMovementState().isMoving) {
            this.transitionTo(STATE_NAMES.MOVE);
        }
    }
    getDesiredUpper() {
        // При низкой выносливости показываем Dizzy
        if (this.machine.staminaSystem?.isLow) return 'Dizzy';
        return null;
    }
}

export class MoveState extends State {
    getDesiredLower() {
        const moveState = this.machine.movementSystem.getMovementState();
        const isLow = this.machine.staminaSystem?.isLow;
        // Передаём в менеджер анимаций флаг усталости через stressLevel (временно переиспользуем аргумент)
        return this.machine.animManager.getDesiredLowerFromMovement(moveState, isLow ? 60 : 0);
    }
    getDesiredUpper() {
        const isLow = this.machine.staminaSystem?.isLow;
        const weaponDrawn = false; // здесь оружие не учитываем
        return this.machine.animManager.getDesiredUpperFromState(this.name, isLow ? 60 : 0, weaponDrawn);
    }
    onEnter(prevState) {
        if (this.machine.movementSystem) this.machine.movementSystem.setInputLock(false);
        this.statePdFactor = 1.0;
        this.machine.pdStrengthTarget = this.machine.getBasePdTarget() * this.statePdFactor;
    }
    update(deltaTime) {
        if (!this.machine.movementSystem.getMovementState().isMoving) {
            this.transitionTo(STATE_NAMES.IDLE);
        }
    }
}

export class ActionState extends State {
    onEnter(prevState, animName, fullBody) {
        this.lockUpper = true;
        this.lockLower = fullBody;
        if (this.machine.movementSystem) this.machine.movementSystem.setInputLock(fullBody);
        this.statePdFactor = 1.0;
        this.machine.pdStrengthTarget = this.machine.getBasePdTarget() * this.statePdFactor;
        this.machine.animManager.startAction(animName, fullBody, () => {
            if (this.machine.movementSystem) this.machine.movementSystem.setInputLock(false);
            this.transitionTo(STATE_NAMES.IDLE);
        });
    }
    onExit(nextState) {
        if (this.machine.animManager) this.machine.animManager.resetActions();
        if (this.machine.movementSystem) this.machine.movementSystem.setInputLock(false);
        this.statePdFactor = null;
    }
    getDesiredUpper() { return null; }
    getDesiredLower() { return null; }
}

export class ImpactState extends State {
    onEnter(prevState, impactData) {
        const { level, hitZone, force } = impactData;
        let animName = null;
        if (level === 1) animName = 'Shivering';
        else if (level === 2) animName = hitZone === 'head' ? 'Hit_Head' : 'Hit_Chest';
        else if (level === 3) animName = 'Hit_Knockback';

        if (animName && (this.machine.animManager.upper[animName] || this.machine.animManager.lower[animName])) {
            const fullBody = level >= 3 || animName === 'Hit_Knockback';
            this.lockUpper = true;
            this.lockLower = fullBody;
            if (this.machine.movementSystem) this.machine.movementSystem.setInputLock(fullBody);
            this.statePdFactor = 0.7;
            this.machine.pdStrengthTarget = this.machine.getBasePdTarget() * this.statePdFactor;
            this.machine.animManager.startAction(animName, fullBody, () => {
                if (this.machine.movementSystem) this.machine.movementSystem.setInputLock(false);
                this.transitionTo(STATE_NAMES.IDLE);
            });
        } else {
            this.waitTimer = 0.2;
        }
    }
    onExit(nextState) {
        if (this.machine.animManager) this.machine.animManager.resetActions();
        if (this.machine.movementSystem) this.machine.movementSystem.setInputLock(false);
        this.statePdFactor = null;
    }
    update(deltaTime) {
        if (this.waitTimer !== undefined) {
            this.waitTimer -= deltaTime;
            if (this.waitTimer <= 0) {
                delete this.waitTimer;
                this.transitionTo(STATE_NAMES.IDLE);
            }
        }
    }
    getDesiredUpper() { return null; }
    getDesiredLower() { return null; }
}

export class StruggleState extends State {
    onEnter(prevState) {
        if (this.machine.movementSystem) this.machine.movementSystem.setInputLock(false);
        this.statePdFactor = 0.7;
        this.machine.pdStrengthTarget = this.machine.getBasePdTarget() * this.statePdFactor;
    }
    getDesiredUpper() {
        return 'Dizzy';
    }
    getDesiredLower() {
        const moveState = this.machine.movementSystem.getMovementState();
        if (moveState.hasVelocity) {
            return 'Zombie_Walk_Fwd_Loop';
        } else {
            return 'Tired_Hunched';
        }
    }
    update(deltaTime) {
        // Выходим из Struggle, когда выносливость восстановилась
        if (this.machine.staminaSystem && !this.machine.staminaSystem.isLow) {
            this.transitionTo(STATE_NAMES.IDLE);
        }
    }
    onExit(nextState) {
        this.statePdFactor = null;
    }
}

export class KnockdownState extends State {
    onEnter(prevState) {
        this.lockUpper = true;
        this.lockLower = true;
        if (this.machine.movementSystem) this.machine.movementSystem.setInputLock(true);
        this.statePdFactor = 0.0;
        this.machine.pdStrengthTarget = 0.0;

        if (this.machine.animManager.upper['Death01']) {
            const deathAnim = this.machine.animManager.upper['Death01'];
            const duration = deathAnim.length;
            this.machine.animManager.startAction('Death01', true, null);
            this.ragdollTimer = Math.max(0, duration - 0.3);
        } else {
            this.transitionTo(STATE_NAMES.RAGDOLL, true);
        }
    }
    onExit(nextState) {
        if (this.machine.animManager) this.machine.animManager.resetActions();
        this.statePdFactor = null;
    }
    update(deltaTime) {
        if (this.ragdollTimer !== undefined) {
            this.ragdollTimer -= deltaTime;
            if (this.ragdollTimer <= 0) {
                this.transitionTo(STATE_NAMES.RAGDOLL, true);
            }
        }
    }
    getDesiredUpper() { return null; }
    getDesiredLower() { return null; }
}

export class GetUpState extends State {
    onEnter(prevState) {
        this.lockUpper = true;
        this.lockLower = true;
        if (this.machine.movementSystem) this.machine.movementSystem.setInputLock(true);
        this.machine.pdStrength = 1.0;
        this.statePdFactor = 1.0;
        this.machine.pdStrengthTarget = this.machine.getBasePdTarget() * this.statePdFactor;

        const animName = 'LayToIdle';
        if (this.machine.animManager.upper[animName] || this.machine.animManager.lower[animName]) {
            this.machine.animManager.startAction(animName, true, () => {
                if (this.machine.movementSystem) this.machine.movementSystem.setInputLock(false);
                this.transitionTo(STATE_NAMES.IDLE);
            }, { disableBlending: true, lockBase: true });
        } else {
            console.warn("GetUpState: анимация LayToIdle не найдена, переход в IDLE");
            if (this.machine.movementSystem) this.machine.movementSystem.setInputLock(false);
            this.transitionTo(STATE_NAMES.IDLE);
        }
    }
    getDesiredUpper() { return null; }
    getDesiredLower() { return null; }
}

export class RagdollState extends State {
    onEnter(prevState, autoGetUp = false) {
        this.lockUpper = true;
        this.lockLower = true;
        if (this.machine.movementSystem) this.machine.movementSystem.setInputLock(true);
        this.statePdFactor = 0.0;
        this.machine.pdStrength = 0.0;
        this.machine.pdStrengthTarget = 0.0;
        this.machine.animManager.stopAll();
        this.machine.animManager.resetActions();
        if (this.machine.dynamicMesh) this.machine.dynamicMesh.alwaysSelectAsActiveMesh = true;

        const pelvisBody = this.machine.boneBodyMap?.get('pelvis') ||
                           (this.machine.boneBodyMap ? Array.from(this.machine.boneBodyMap.values())[0] : null);
        if (pelvisBody) {
            const randomDir = new BABYLON.Vector3(
                (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5,
                Math.random() * 0.3,
                (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5
            ).normalize();
            const force = randomDir.scale(5);
            const worldPos = pelvisBody.transformNode.getAbsolutePosition();
            pelvisBody.applyImpulse(force, worldPos);
        }
        if (autoGetUp) {
            this.getUpTimer = 2.5;
        } else {
            this.getUpTimer = null;
        }
    }
    update(deltaTime) {
        if (this.getUpTimer !== null) {
            this.getUpTimer -= deltaTime;
            if (this.getUpTimer <= 0) {
                this.transitionTo(STATE_NAMES.GET_UP);
            }
        }
    }
    onExit() {
        if (this.machine.dynamicMesh) this.machine.dynamicMesh.alwaysSelectAsActiveMesh = false;
        if (this.machine.movementSystem) this.machine.movementSystem.setInputLock(false);
        this.statePdFactor = null;
    }
    getDesiredUpper() { return null; }
    getDesiredLower() { return null; }
}

export class DefendState extends State {
    onEnter(prevState, hitDirection, collisionSpeed = 2.0) {
        this.lockUpper = true;
        this.lockLower = false;
        this.hitDirection = hitDirection ? hitDirection.clone() : new BABYLON.Vector3(1, 0, 0);
        if (this.machine.movementSystem) this.machine.movementSystem.setInputLock(true);
        this.statePdFactor = 1.0;
        this.machine.pdStrengthTarget = this.machine.getBasePdTarget() * this.statePdFactor;
        this.backwardDistance = Math.min(2.0, 0.5 + collisionSpeed * 0.3);
        this.moveDuration = Math.min(0.5, 0.2 + collisionSpeed * 0.1);
        this.moveTimer = this.moveDuration;
        this.startPos = this.machine.movementSystem.position.clone();
        this.targetPos = this.startPos.add(this.hitDirection.scale(this.backwardDistance));
        this.isMoving = true;
    }
    getDesiredUpper() { return 'Defend'; }
    getDesiredLower() {
        const moveState = this.machine.movementSystem.getMovementState();
        const isLow = this.machine.staminaSystem?.isLow;
        return this.machine.animManager.getDesiredLowerFromMovement(moveState, isLow ? 60 : 0);
    }
    update(deltaTime) {
        if (this.isMoving) {
            this.moveTimer -= deltaTime;
            let t = 1.0 - Math.max(0, this.moveTimer / this.moveDuration);
            t = 1 - Math.pow(1 - t, 3);
            const newPos = BABYLON.Vector3.Lerp(this.startPos, this.targetPos, t);
            this.machine.movementSystem.position.copyFrom(newPos);
            this.machine.movementSystem.root.position.copyFrom(newPos);
            if (this.moveTimer <= 0) {
                this.isMoving = false;
                this.idleTimer = 0.2;
            }
        } else if (this.idleTimer !== undefined) {
            this.idleTimer -= deltaTime;
            if (this.idleTimer <= 0) {
                this.transitionTo(STATE_NAMES.IDLE);
            }
        }
    }
    onExit() {
        if (this.machine.movementSystem) this.machine.movementSystem.setInputLock(false);
        this.statePdFactor = null;
    }
}

export class StumbleState extends State {
    onEnter(prevState, impactData) {
        this.lockUpper = true;
        this.lockLower = true;
        if (this.machine.movementSystem) {
            this.machine.movementSystem.setInputLock(true);
            this.machine.movementSystem.isReactingToCollision = true;
        }
        const basePd = this.machine.getBasePdTarget();
        this.statePdFactor = 0.3;
        this.machine.pdStrengthTarget = basePd * this.statePdFactor;

        if (this.machine.staminaSystem) this.machine.staminaSystem.consume(15);

        let animName = 'Hit_Chest';
        const force = impactData.force || 20;
        const direction = impactData.direction || new BABYLON.Vector3(0, 0, 1);
        const dotForward = BABYLON.Vector3.Dot(direction, new BABYLON.Vector3(0, 0, 1));
        if (force > 40) animName = 'Hit_Knockback';
        else if (Math.abs(dotForward) < 0.5) animName = 'Hit_Head';
        else if (dotForward > 0.7) animName = 'Hit_Chest';
        else animName = 'Dizzy';

        this.machine.animManager.startAction(animName, true, () => {
            this.transitionTo(STATE_NAMES.IDLE);
        }, { disableBlending: true });

        if (this.machine.reactionSystem && direction) {
            const pelvisBody = this.machine.boneBodyMap?.get('pelvis');
            if (pelvisBody) {
                const impulse = direction.scale(-force * 0.5);
                pelvisBody.applyImpulse(impulse, pelvisBody.transformNode.getAbsolutePosition());
            }
        }
        if (this.machine.camera) {
            const originalPos = this.machine.camera.position.clone();
            const shakeAmount = 0.15;
            this.machine.camera.position.addInPlace(new BABYLON.Vector3(
                (Math.random() - 0.5) * shakeAmount,
                (Math.random() - 0.5) * shakeAmount,
                (Math.random() - 0.5) * shakeAmount
            ));
            setTimeout(() => this.machine.camera.position.copyFrom(originalPos), 150);
        }
    }
    onExit(nextState) {
        if (this.machine.movementSystem) {
            this.machine.movementSystem.resetCollisionState();
            this.machine.movementSystem.isReactingToCollision = false;
        }
        this.statePdFactor = null;
    }
    getDesiredUpper() { return null; }
    getDesiredLower() { return null; }
}

export class RagdollImpactState extends State {
    onEnter(prevState, impactData) {
        this.lockUpper = true;
        this.lockLower = true;
        if (this.machine.movementSystem) {
            this.machine.movementSystem.setInputLock(true);
            this.machine.movementSystem.isReactingToCollision = true;
            this.machine.movementSystem.setKinematicColliderActive(false);
        }
        this.statePdFactor = 0.0;
        this.machine.pdStrengthTarget = 0.0;
        this.machine.animManager.stopAll();
        this.machine.animManager.resetActions();

        if (this.machine.staminaSystem) this.machine.staminaSystem.consume(35);

        const force = impactData.force || 50;
        const direction = impactData.direction || new BABYLON.Vector3(0, 0, 1);
        const pelvisBody = this.machine.boneBodyMap?.get('pelvis');
        const spineBody = this.machine.boneBodyMap?.get('spine_03');
        const headBody = this.machine.boneBodyMap?.get('head');
        if (pelvisBody && direction) {
            const impulse = direction.scale(-force * 0.8);
            pelvisBody.applyImpulse(impulse, pelvisBody.transformNode.getAbsolutePosition());
        }
        if (spineBody && direction) {
            const impulse = direction.scale(-force * 0.4);
            spineBody.applyImpulse(impulse, spineBody.transformNode.getAbsolutePosition());
        }
        if (headBody && direction) {
            const randomDir = new BABYLON.Vector3(
                (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5, Math.random() * 0.3, (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5
            ).normalize();
            const impulse = direction.scale(-force * 0.2).add(randomDir.scale(force * 0.1));
            headBody.applyImpulse(impulse, headBody.transformNode.getAbsolutePosition());
        }
        if (this.machine.camera) {
            const originalPos = this.machine.camera.position.clone();
            const shakeAmount = 0.3;
            this.machine.camera.position.addInPlace(new BABYLON.Vector3(
                (Math.random() - 0.5) * shakeAmount,
                (Math.random() - 0.5) * shakeAmount,
                (Math.random() - 0.5) * shakeAmount
            ));
            setTimeout(() => this.machine.camera.position.copyFrom(originalPos), 200);
        }
        this.getUpTimer = 2.5;
    }
    update(deltaTime) {
        const pelvisBody = this.machine.boneBodyMap?.get('pelvis');
        if (pelvisBody && this.machine.movementSystem) {
            const worldPos = pelvisBody.transformNode.getAbsolutePosition();
            worldPos.y = Math.max(worldPos.y, this.machine.movementSystem.groundY);
            this.machine.movementSystem.position.copyFrom(worldPos);
        }
        if (this.getUpTimer) {
            this.getUpTimer -= deltaTime;
            if (this.getUpTimer <= 0) {
                this.transitionTo(STATE_NAMES.GET_UP);
            }
        }
    }
    onExit(nextState) {
        if (this.machine.movementSystem) {
            this.machine.movementSystem.resetCollisionState();
            this.machine.movementSystem.isReactingToCollision = false;
            this.machine.movementSystem.setKinematicColliderActive(true);
        }
        this.statePdFactor = null;
        if (this.machine.staminaSystem) {
            this.machine.movementSystem.movement.maxWalkSpeed *= 0.7;
            this.machine.movementSystem.movement.maxRunSpeed *= 0.7;
            setTimeout(() => {
                this.machine.movementSystem.movement.maxWalkSpeed /= 0.7;
                this.machine.movementSystem.movement.maxRunSpeed /= 0.7;
            }, 5000);
        }
    }
    getDesiredUpper() { return null; }
    getDesiredLower() { return null; }
}

export class StateMachine {
    constructor() {
        this.states = {};
        this.currentState = null;
        this.pdStrength = 1.0;
        this.pdStrengthTarget = 1.0;
        this.staminaSystem = null;
    }
    getBasePdTarget() {
        return this.staminaSystem ? this.staminaSystem.getPdStrengthMultiplier() : 1.0;
    }
    addState(state) {
        this.states[state.name] = state;
        state.machine = this;
    }
    start(initialStateName) {
        this.currentState = this.states[initialStateName];
        if (this.currentState) this.currentState.onEnter(null);
    }
    transitionTo(newStateName, ...args) {
        const newState = this.states[newStateName];
        if (!newState) {
            console.warn(`State ${newStateName} not found`);
            return;
        }
        if (this.currentState && this.currentState.name === newStateName) {
            return;
        }
        const oldState = this.currentState;
        if (oldState) oldState.onExit(newState);
        this.currentState = newState;
        newState.onEnter(oldState, ...args);
        console.log(`Transition: ${oldState?.name} -> ${newState.name}`);
    }
    update(deltaTime) {
        if (this.currentState) this.currentState.update(deltaTime);
    }
}

// js/trustModel.js
export class TrustModel {
    constructor(initialTrust = 0.6) {
        this.trust = initialTrust;
        this._recoveryRate = 0.08; // ускорено
        this._decayRate = 0.1;
        this._damageImpact = 0.005; // уменьшено вдвое
        this._minTrust = 0.0;
        this._maxTrust = 1.0;
    }

    update(deltaSec, isSafe, playerPushingTowardDanger) {
        if (!playerPushingTowardDanger) {
            this.trust = Math.min(this._maxTrust, this.trust + this._recoveryRate * 0.5 * deltaSec);
        }
        if (isSafe) {
            this.trust = Math.min(this._maxTrust, this.trust + this._recoveryRate * 1.5 * deltaSec);
        }
        if (playerPushingTowardDanger) {
            this.trust = Math.max(this._minTrust, this.trust - this._decayRate * 2.0 * deltaSec);
        }
    }

    recordDamage(force) {
        if (force > 2.0) { // порог из физики
            this.trust = Math.max(this._minTrust, this.trust - force * this._damageImpact);
            console.log(`[Trust] dropped to ${this.trust.toFixed(2)} after damage (force: ${force.toFixed(1)})`);
        }
    }

    getTrust() {
        return this.trust;
    }
}
