<script type="module">
    // v26: НОКДАУН ПО МОМЕНТУМ-БЮДЖЕТУ
    //  D = (Δv * sens * heightFactor * horizontal) / KnockImpulse
    //  - sens: торс/голова 1.0, ноги 0.7, плечо 0.45, предплечье 0.25
    //  - heightFactor: 0.55 (лодыжка) .. 1.25 (голова)
    //  - horizontal: доля горизонтальной составляющей направления
    //  - бюджет накапливается и восстанавливается (recoveryTau); >=100% → нокдаун
    //  По умолчанию KnockImpulse=8: грудь/таз ~8 м/с, голова ~6.5, бедро ~11, рука не валит

    const boneGroups = {
        'pelvis_grp': ['pelvis', 'hip', 'Hips'],
        'lower_back': ['spine_01'],
        'chest': ['spine_02', 'spine_03'],
        'neck': ['neck_01'],
        'head': ['head', 'head_leaf'],
    };
    const kinDefs = [
        { name: 'hand_l', bones: ['hand_l'] },
        { name: 'hand_r', bones: ['hand_r'] },
        { name: 'foot_l', bones: ['foot_l', 'ball_l'] },
        { name: 'foot_r', bones: ['foot_r', 'ball_r'] },
    ];
    const excludeBones = ['root', '__root__', 'Root'];
    const SKIP_RE = /thumb|index|middle_|pinky|ring|finger|palm|toe|ball|foot|hand|wrist/i;

    const KP = 5, KD = 5.5, KR = 6, KDR = 5;
    const MAX_FORCE = 60, MAX_TORQUE = 35;
    const W_TAU_SMOOTH = 0.06;
    const W_SNAP = 0.02;
    const VIS_TAU = 0.06;
    const PD_DEAD_POS = 0.005, PD_DEAD_VEL = 0.06, PD_DEAD_ANG = 0.12;
    const PULSE_DUR = 0.18;
    const INSTANT_FRACTION = 0.2;
    const BRACE_TAU = 0.18;
    const BRACE_GAIN = 1.0;
    const HEAD_STAB = 2.2;
    const SYNC_GRACE = 0.5;
    const BALL_LIFE = 8;
    const BALL_HIT_CD = 0.25;
    const BALL_BOUNCE = 0.45;
    const MAX_BALLS = 10;
    const GRAVITY = new BABYLON.Vector3(0, -9.81, 0);

    const COL_GROUP = { RAGDOLL: 1<<0, GROUND: 1<<1, BALL: 1<<2 };
    const COL_MASK = {
        RAGDOLL: COL_GROUP.GROUND,
        GROUND: ~0,
        BALL: COL_GROUP.GROUND | COL_GROUP.BALL
    };

    function massFor(name) {
        const n = name.toLowerCase();
        if (n.includes('pelvis') || n.includes('hip')) return 12;
        if (n.includes('lower_back') || n.includes('spine_01')) return 8;
        if (n.includes('spine') || n.includes('chest') || n.includes('back')) return 6;
        if (n.includes('neck')) return 3;
        if (n.includes('head')) return 5;
        if (n.includes('clavicle')) return 2;
        if (n.includes('upperarm') || n.includes('upper_arm')) return 4;
        if (n.includes('lowerarm') || n.includes('lower_arm')) return 3;
        if (n.includes('thigh') || n.includes('upper_leg')) return 6;
        if (n.includes('calf') || n.includes('lower_leg')) return 5;
        return 3;
    }

    // чувствительность к нокдауну по типу тела
    function fallSensitivity(name) {
        const n = name.toLowerCase();
        if (n.includes('pelvis') || n.includes('hip') || n.includes('spine') || n.includes('back') || n.includes('chest') || n.includes('head') || n.includes('neck')) return 1.0;
        if (n.includes('thigh') || n.includes('calf') || n.includes('leg')) return 0.7;
        if (n.includes('clavicle') || n.includes('upperarm') || n.includes('upper_arm')) return 0.45;
        return 0.25;
    }

    const HINGE_RE = /calf|lower_leg|knee|lowerarm|lower_arm|elbow/;
    const STIFF_RE = /spine|chest|back|pelvis|hip|clavicle/;
    function jointSpec(name) {
        const n = name.toLowerCase();
        const toRad = d => d * Math.PI / 180;
        let type = 'ball', D = [-45, 45, -45, 45, -30, 30];
        if (HINGE_RE.test(n)) {
            type = 'hinge';
            D = (n.includes('lowerarm') || n.includes('elbow')) ? [-5, 145, -8, 8, -8, 8] : [-5, 140, -8, 8, -8, 8];
        } else if (STIFF_RE.test(n)) {
            type = 'stiff';
            D = [-10, 20, -12, 12, -8, 8];
        } else if (n.includes('upperarm') || n.includes('upper_arm')) {
            D = [-70, 120, -60, 70, -60, 60];
        } else if (n.includes('thigh') || n.includes('upper_leg')) {
            D = [-110, 45, -45, 30, -35, 35];
        } else if (n.includes('neck') || n.includes('head')) {
            D = [-40, 50, -60, 60, -30, 30];
        }
        return {
            type, D,
            limits: [
                { axis: BABYLON.PhysicsConstraintAxis.LINEAR_X, minLimit: 0, maxLimit: 0 },
                { axis: BABYLON.PhysicsConstraintAxis.LINEAR_Y, minLimit: 0, maxLimit: 0 },
                { axis: BABYLON.PhysicsConstraintAxis.LINEAR_Z, minLimit: 0, maxLimit: 0 },
                { axis: BABYLON.PhysicsConstraintAxis.ANGULAR_X, minLimit: toRad(D[0]), maxLimit: toRad(D[1]) },
                { axis: BABYLON.PhysicsConstraintAxis.ANGULAR_Y, minLimit: toRad(D[2]), maxLimit: toRad(D[3]) },
                { axis: BABYLON.PhysicsConstraintAxis.ANGULAR_Z, minLimit: toRad(D[4]), maxLimit: toRad(D[5]) },
            ]
        };
    }

    const canvas = document.getElementById('renderCanvas');
    const engine = new BABYLON.Engine(canvas, true, { preserveDrawingBuffer: true, stencil: true });
    const scene = new BABYLON.Scene(engine);
    scene.useRightHandedSystem = true;
    scene.clearColor = new BABYLON.Color4(0.1, 0.1, 0.15, 1);

    const camera = new BABYLON.ArcRotateCamera('cam', -Math.PI / 2, Math.PI / 3, 8, new BABYLON.Vector3(0, 1.2, 0), scene);
    camera.attachControl(canvas, true);
    camera.lowerRadiusLimit = 2; camera.upperRadiusLimit = 20;

    const light = new BABYLON.HemisphericLight('l1', new BABYLON.Vector3(0, 1, 0), scene);
    light.intensity = 0.6;
    const light2 = new BABYLON.DirectionalLight('l2', new BABYLON.Vector3(-1, -1, -1), scene);
    light2.intensity = 0.8;

    const havok = await HavokPhysics();
    const havokPlugin = new BABYLON.HavokPlugin(true, havok);
    scene.enablePhysics(GRAVITY, havokPlugin);
    const physicsEngine = scene.getPhysicsEngine();
    if (physicsEngine && physicsEngine.setTimeStep) { try { physicsEngine.setTimeStep(1 / 60, 8); } catch (_) {} }

    const ground = BABYLON.MeshBuilder.CreateGround('ground', { width: 30, height: 30 }, scene);
    ground.position.y = -0.05;
    const gm = new BABYLON.StandardMaterial('gm', scene);
    gm.diffuseColor = new BABYLON.Color3(0.15, 0.15, 0.2);
    ground.material = gm;
    new BABYLON.PhysicsAggregate(ground, BABYLON.PhysicsShapeType.BOX, { mass: 0, restitution: 0.3, friction: 1.0, collisionGroup: COL_GROUP.GROUND, collisionMask: ~0 }, scene);

    const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
    const fileInput = $('fileInput'), logEl = $('log'), statusEl = $('status'), infoEl = $('info');
    const angleLogEl = $('angleLog'), angleCountEl = $('angleCount'), toggleBtn = $('toggleAngles');
    const colliderSelect = $('colliderSelect'), impulseBtn = $('impulseBtn'), randomBtn = $('randomBtn');
    const fallBtn = $('fallBtn'), standBtn = $('standBtn'), resetBtn = $('resetBtn');
    const throwBtn = $('throwBtn'), throwSpeed = $('throwSpeed'), throwSpeedVal = $('throwSpeedVal');
    const throwMass = $('throwMass'), throwMassVal = $('throwMassVal');
    const ballRadius = $('ballRadius'), ballRadiusVal = $('ballRadiusVal');
    const showBalls = $('showBalls');
    const impulseSpeedInput = $('impulseSpeed'), showRag = $('showRag');
    const selectedCenterEl = $('selectedCenter'), selectedBBoxEl = $('selectedBBox');
    const ragDom = $('ragDom'), ragDomVal = $('ragDomVal');
    const chainMul = $('chainMul'), chainMulVal = $('chainMulVal');
    const waveDelay = $('waveDelay'), waveDelayVal = $('waveDelayVal');
    const reflexK = $('reflexK'), reflexKVal = $('reflexKVal');
    const holdK = $('holdK'), holdKVal = $('holdKVal');
    const iSat = $('iSat'), iSatVal = $('iSatVal');
    const lerpTime = $('lerpTime'), lerpTimeVal = $('lerpTimeVal');
    const ragGravity = $('ragGravity'), ragGravityVal = $('ragGravityVal');
    const knockImpulse = $('knockImpulse'), knockImpulseVal = $('knockImpulseVal');
    const recoveryTau = $('recoveryTau'), recoveryTauVal = $('recoveryTauVal');
    const ragPctEl = $('ragPct');
    const modeIndicator = $('modeIndicator'), blendFill = $('blendFill');

    ragDom.addEventListener('input', () => ragDomVal.textContent = parseFloat(ragDom.value).toFixed(2));
    chainMul.addEventListener('input', () => chainMulVal.textContent = parseFloat(chainMul.value).toFixed(2));
    waveDelay.addEventListener('input', () => waveDelayVal.textContent = parseFloat(waveDelay.value).toFixed(0));
    reflexK.addEventListener('input', () => reflexKVal.textContent = parseFloat(reflexK.value).toFixed(1));
    holdK.addEventListener('input', () => holdKVal.textContent = parseFloat(holdK.value).toFixed(2));
    iSat.addEventListener('input', () => iSatVal.textContent = parseFloat(iSat.value).toFixed(1));
    lerpTime.addEventListener('input', () => lerpTimeVal.textContent = parseFloat(lerpTime.value).toFixed(2));
    ragGravity.addEventListener('input', () => ragGravityVal.textContent = parseFloat(ragGravity.value).toFixed(2));
    knockImpulse.addEventListener('input', () => knockImpulseVal.textContent = parseFloat(knockImpulse.value).toFixed(1));
    recoveryTau.addEventListener('input', () => recoveryTauVal.textContent = parseFloat(recoveryTau.value).toFixed(1));
    throwSpeed.addEventListener('input', () => throwSpeedVal.textContent = parseFloat(throwSpeed.value).toFixed(1));
    throwMass.addEventListener('input', () => throwMassVal.textContent = parseFloat(throwMass.value).toFixed(1));
    ballRadius.addEventListener('input', () => ballRadiusVal.textContent = parseFloat(ballRadius.value).toFixed(2));
    showRag.addEventListener('change', () => { for (const r of colliderRefs) if (r.physMesh) r.physMesh.visibility = showRag.checked ? 0.12 : 0; });
    showBalls.addEventListener('change', () => { for (const b of balls) b.mesh.visibility = showBalls.checked ? 1 : 0; });

    let logPaused = false;
    toggleBtn.addEventListener('click', () => { logPaused = !logPaused; toggleBtn.textContent = logPaused ? '▶ Логи' : '⏸ Пауза'; });

    function logToUI(msg, type = 'info') {
        const colors = { info: '#88ddff', warn: '#ffaa00', error: '#ff4444', success: '#44ff44' };
        const p = { info: 'ℹ️', warn: '⚠️', error: '❌', success: '✅' }[type] || 'ℹ️';
        logEl.innerHTML += `<div style="color:${colors[type] || '#88ddff'}">${p} ${msg}</div>`;
        logEl.scrollTop = logEl.scrollHeight;
    }

    function bodySetGravityFactor(body, f) { try { if (typeof body.setGravityFactor === 'function') { body.setGravityFactor(f); return true; } } catch (_) {} return false; }
    function bodyWake(body) { try { if (typeof body.wakeUp === 'function') body.wakeUp(); } catch (_) {} }
    function bodyZeroVel(body) {
        try { if (typeof body.setLinearVelocity === 'function') body.setLinearVelocity(BABYLON.Vector3.Zero()); } catch (_) {}
        try { if (typeof body.setAngularVelocity === 'function') body.setAngularVelocity(BABYLON.Vector3.Zero()); } catch (_) {}
    }
    function bodyGetLinVel(body) { try { if (typeof body.getLinearVelocity === 'function') return body.getLinearVelocity().clone(); } catch (_) {} return new BABYLON.Vector3(); }
    function bodyGetAngVel(body) { try { if (typeof body.getAngularVelocity === 'function') return body.getAngularVelocity().clone(); } catch (_) {} return new BABYLON.Vector3(); }
    function bodySetLinVel(body, v) { try { if (typeof body.setLinearVelocity === 'function') body.setLinearVelocity(v); } catch (_) {} }
    function bodyApplyForce(body, force) { try { if (typeof body.applyForce === 'function') { body.applyForce(force, BABYLON.Vector3.Zero()); return true; } } catch (_) {} return false; }
    function bodyApplyForceAt(body, force, point) {
        try { if (typeof body.applyForce === 'function') { body.applyForce(force, point); return true; } } catch (_) {}
        return bodyApplyForce(body, force);
    }
    function bodyApplyTorque(body, torque) { try { if (typeof body.applyTorque === 'function') { body.applyTorque(torque); return true; } } catch (_) {} return false; }
    function bodySetTransform(body, pos, quat) {
        try { if (typeof body.setTransformation === 'function') { body.setTransformation(pos, quat); return true; } } catch (_) {}
        return false;
    }

    function decomposeM(M) {
        const sc = new BABYLON.Vector3(); const ro = new BABYLON.Quaternion(); const po = new BABYLON.Vector3();
        M.decompose(sc, ro, po);
        return { pos: po, rot: ro };
    }
    function quatToMatrix(q) {
        const m = new BABYLON.Matrix();
        q.toRotationMatrix(m);
        return m;
    }
    const formatAngles = d => `X: ${d.x.toFixed(1)}° Y: ${d.y.toFixed(1)}° Z: ${d.z.toFixed(1)}°`;
    const formatPos = p => `X: ${p.x.toFixed(3)} Y: ${p.y.toFixed(3)} Z: ${p.z.toFixed(3)}`;
    const formatVec3 = v => `(${v.x.toFixed(3)}, ${v.y.toFixed(3)}, ${v.z.toFixed(3)})`;

    const COLOR_KIN = new BABYLON.Color3(1.0, 0.6, 0.2);
    const COLOR_DYN = new BABYLON.Color3(0.2, 0.8, 1.0);

    let colliderRefs = [];
    let constraints = [];
    let syncObserver = null;
    let selectedIndex = -1;
    let skinnedMesh = null, skelRef = null;
    let refMeshHidden = null, refSkel = null;
    let refBoneMap = new Map();
    let rootRef = null;
    let pelvisOffset = new BABYLON.Vector3();
    let boneToRef = new Map();
    let buildVis = null;
    let falling = false;
    let instability = 0;   // моментум-бюджет неустойчивости 0..1+
    let activePulses = [];
    let balls = [];
    const ANGLE_LOG_FRAMES = 20;
    const nowSec = () => performance.now() / 1000;

    function boneDepth(bone) { let d = 0, p = bone.getParent(); while (p) { d++; p = p.getParent(); } return d; }

    function dynamicRefs() { return colliderRefs.filter(r => !r.kinematic); }

    function maxWeight() { let m = 0; for (const r of colliderRefs) if (r.wS > m) m = r.wS; return m; }

    function updateModeIndicator() {
        const mw = maxWeight();
        if (falling) {
            modeIndicator.textContent = '💀 ПАДЕНИЕ (гравитация)';
            modeIndicator.className = 'fallen';
        } else if (mw > 0.05) {
            modeIndicator.textContent = `🔄 BLEND maxW=${mw.toFixed(2)}`;
            modeIndicator.className = 'blend';
        } else {
            modeIndicator.textContent = '🟢 СТОЙКА (слой2=слой1)';
            modeIndicator.className = '';
        }
        blendFill.style.width = `${(mw * 100).toFixed(0)}%`;
    }

    function triggerFall(reason) {
        if (falling) return;
        falling = true;
        instability = Math.max(instability, 1);
        for (const r of colliderRefs) { r.pendingW = null; if (!r.kinematic) bodyWake(r.agg.body); }
        updateModeIndicator();
        logToUI(`💀 Нокдаун: ${reason} → гравитация ВКЛ`, 'warn');
    }

    // добавление дестабилизации от удара
    function addInstability(ref, speed, dir) {
        const K = parseFloat(knockImpulse.value);
        const sens = fallSensitivity(ref.name);
        const restY = decomposeM(ref.restWorld).pos.y;
        const hFactor = 0.5 + 0.75 * Math.min(1, restY / 1.4);
        const horiz = Math.sqrt(dir.x * dir.x + dir.z * dir.z);
        const D = (speed * sens * hFactor * horiz) / K;
        if (D <= 0.01) return D;
        instability = Math.min(1.5, instability + D);
        if (!falling && instability >= 1) {
            triggerFall(`неустойчивость ${(instability * 100).toFixed(0)}% (удар Δv=${speed.toFixed(1)} в ${ref.name})`);
        }
        return D;
    }

    function computeLayer1() {
        for (const ref of colliderRefs) {
            let L1 = ref.restWorld;
            if (ref.refBone) {
                try {
                    const animW = ref.refBone.getWorldMatrix ? ref.refBone.getWorldMatrix() : null;
                    if (animW) {
                        L1 = ref.restWorld.clone().multiply(BABYLON.Matrix.Invert(ref.boneRestWorld)).multiply(animW);
                    }
                } catch (_) {}
            }
            ref.l1World = L1;
        }
    }

    function applyPD(ref, strength, time) {
        const depthMul = 1 / (1 + 0.22 * (ref.depth || 0));
        let st = strength * depthMul;

        const rk = parseFloat(reflexK.value);
        const bt = time - ref.braceAt;
        if (rk > 0 && bt >= 0 && bt < 1.0) {
            st *= (1 + rk * BRACE_GAIN * (ref.braceAmp || 0) * Math.exp(-bt / BRACE_TAU));
        }
        if (st <= 0.01) return;

        ref.physMesh.computeWorldMatrix(true);
        const cur = decomposeM(ref.physMesh.getWorldMatrix());
        const tgt = decomposeM(ref.l1World);

        const errV = tgt.pos.subtract(cur.pos);
        const err = errV.length();
        if (err > 2.0) return;

        const vel = bodyGetLinVel(ref.agg.body);
        const av0 = bodyGetAngVel(ref.agg.body);

        if (err < PD_DEAD_POS && vel.length() < PD_DEAD_VEL && av0.length() < PD_DEAD_ANG) {
            bodyZeroVel(ref.agg.body);
            return;
        }

        let F = errV.scale(ref.mass * KP * st).subtract(vel.scale(ref.mass * KD * st));
        const fLen = F.length();
        if (fLen > MAX_FORCE * st) F = F.normalize().scale(MAX_FORCE * st);
        bodyApplyForce(ref.agg.body, F);

        const qe = cur.rot.conjugate().multiply(tgt.rot);
        const s = Math.sqrt(qe.x * qe.x + qe.y * qe.y + qe.z * qe.z);
        let ang = 0; const axis = new BABYLON.Vector3(0, 0, 0);
        if (s > 1e-6) { axis.set(qe.x / s, qe.y / s, qe.z / s); ang = 2 * Math.atan2(s, qe.w); }
        if (ang > Math.PI) ang -= 2 * Math.PI;
        let T = axis.scale(ang * ref.mass * KR * st).subtract(av0.scale(ref.mass * KDR * st));

        if (/head|neck/.test(ref.name)) {
            const R = ref.physMesh.getWorldMatrix().getRotationMatrix();
            const up = BABYLON.Vector3.TransformNormal(new BABYLON.Vector3(0, 1, 0), R);
            const axisH = BABYLON.Vector3.Cross(up, new BABYLON.Vector3(0, 1, 0));
            const angH = Math.asin(Math.min(1, axisH.length()));
            if (angH > 0.01 && axisH.length() > 1e-6) {
                T = T.add(axisH.normalize().scale(angH * ref.mass * KR * st * HEAD_STAB));
            }
        }

        const tLen = T.length();
        if (tLen > MAX_TORQUE * st * (1 + HEAD_STAB)) T = T.normalize().scale(MAX_TORQUE * st * (1 + HEAD_STAB));
        bodyApplyTorque(ref.agg.body, T);
    }

    function queueWeight(ref, a, level, time) {
        if (ref.kinematic) return;
        if (a <= 0.001) return;
        a = Math.min(1, a);
        const at = time + level * (parseFloat(waveDelay.value) / 1000);
        if (!ref.pendingW || a > ref.pendingW.a) {
            ref.pendingW = { a, at };
        } else if (Math.abs(a - ref.pendingW.a) < 1e-6 && at < ref.pendingW.at) {
            ref.pendingW.at = at;
        }
    }

    function alignFactor(ref, dir, rel) {
        if (ref.spec.type === 'hinge') {
            ref.visMesh.computeWorldMatrix(true);
            const R = ref.visMesh.getWorldMatrix().getRotationMatrix();
            const h = BABYLON.Vector3.TransformNormal(new BABYLON.Vector3(1, 0, 0), R).normalize();
            const c = Math.abs(dir.dot(h));
            let f = 0.35 + 0.65 * (1 - c);
            if (rel) {
                const torqueAxis = rel.cross(dir);
                const proj = torqueAxis.dot(h);
                const minA = ref.spec.D[0], maxA = ref.spec.D[1];
                if ((proj < 0 && minA >= -5) || (proj > 0 && maxA <= 5)) f *= 0.35;
            }
            return f;
        }
        if (ref.spec.type === 'stiff') return 0.8;
        return 1;
    }

    function contactRel(ref, dir, jitter = 0) {
        ref.visMesh.computeWorldMatrix(true);
        const rot = ref.visMesh.rotationQuaternion || BABYLON.Quaternion.Identity();
        const inv = rot.conjugate();
        const dirLocal = BABYLON.Vector3.TransformNormal(dir, quatToMatrix(inv));
        const relLocal = dirLocal.multiply(ref.halfExtents).scale(-0.9);
        if (jitter > 0) {
            relLocal.x += (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2 * jitter * ref.halfExtents.x;
            relLocal.y += (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2 * jitter * ref.halfExtents.y;
            relLocal.z += (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2 * jitter * ref.halfExtents.z;
            relLocal.x = Math.max(-ref.halfExtents.x, Math.min(ref.halfExtents.x, relLocal.x));
            relLocal.y = Math.max(-ref.halfExtents.y, Math.min(ref.halfExtents.y, relLocal.y));
            relLocal.z = Math.max(-ref.halfExtents.z, Math.min(ref.halfExtents.z, relLocal.z));
        }
        return BABYLON.Vector3.TransformNormal(relLocal, quatToMatrix(rot));
    }

    function applyImpulseTo(ref, speed, dir, quiet = false, contactOverride = null) {
        if (ref.kinematic) ref = ref.parentRef;
        if (!ref || ref.kinematic) return;
        const time = nowSec();
        const IS = parseFloat(iSat.value);
        const dom = parseFloat(ragDom.value);
        const chain = parseFloat(chainMul.value);
        const base = Math.min(1, speed / IS) * dom;

        const rel = contactOverride ? contactOverride.clone() : contactRel(ref, dir, 0.35);

        queueWeight(ref, base * alignFactor(ref, dir, rel), 0, time);
        ref.braceAt = time;
        ref.braceAmp = Math.min(1, base * 1.2 + 0.2);
        const walk = (r, depth) => {
            for (const c of r.children) {
                if (!c.kinematic) {
                    queueWeight(c, base * chain * Math.pow(0.9, depth) * alignFactor(c, dir, null), depth, time);
                    if (depth <= 2) c.braceAt = time;
                }
                walk(c, depth + 1);
            }
        };
        walk(ref, 1);
        let p = ref.parentRef, d = 1;
        while (p && d <= 4) {
            queueWeight(p, base * chain * 0.55 * Math.pow(0.75, d - 1) * alignFactor(p, dir, null), d, time);
            p.braceAt = time;
            p = p.parentRef; d++;
        }

        // дестабилизация для нокдауна
        const D = addInstability(ref, speed, dir);

        const impulseVec = dir.scale(speed * ref.mass);
        try { ref.agg.body.applyImpulse(impulseVec.scale(INSTANT_FRACTION), rel); } catch (_) {}
        activePulses.push({ ref, impulse: impulseVec.scale(1 - INSTANT_FRACTION), t0: time, dur: PULSE_DUR, rel });
        bodyWake(ref.agg.body);
        if (!quiet) logToUI(`💥 ${ref.name}: w=${base.toFixed(2)}, D=${D.toFixed(2)}`, 'warn');
    }

    function randomPushDir() {
        const ang = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
        return new BABYLON.Vector3(Math.cos(ang), (Math.random() - 0.35) * 0.6, Math.sin(ang)).normalize();
    }

    function spawnBall() {
        if (!rootRef) { logToUI('Сначала загрузите модель', 'warn'); return; }
        const speed = parseFloat(throwSpeed.value);
        const mass = parseFloat(throwMass.value);
        const radius = parseFloat(ballRadius.value);

        const start = camera.position.clone();
        const rootPos = rootRef.visMesh.getAbsolutePosition();
        const target = rootPos.clone();
        target.y += 0.6;
        target.x += (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5;
        target.z += (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5;
        const dist = BABYLON.Vector3.Distance(start, target);
        target.y += dist * 0.06;

        const dir = target.subtract(start).normalize();

        const mesh = BABYLON.MeshBuilder.CreateSphere('throw_ball', { diameter: radius * 2, segments: 14 }, scene);
        mesh.position.copyFrom(start.add(dir.scale(radius + 0.2)));
        mesh.visibility = showBalls.checked ? 1 : 0;
        const mat = new BABYLON.StandardMaterial('ballMat', scene);
        mat.diffuseColor = new BABYLON.Color3(0.9, 0.5, 0.15);
        mat.emissiveColor = new BABYLON.Color3(0.35, 0.15, 0.02);
        mesh.material = mat;

        const agg = new BABYLON.PhysicsAggregate(mesh, BABYLON.PhysicsShapeType.SPHERE, {
            mass, restitution: 0.4, friction: 0.6,
            collisionGroup: COL_GROUP.BALL,
            collisionMask: COL_MASK.BALL
        }, scene);
        bodySetLinVel(agg.body, dir.scale(speed));
        bodyWake(agg.body);

        balls.push({ mesh, agg, mass, radius, born: nowSec(), lastHits: new Map(), prevPos: null });

        while (balls.length > MAX_BALLS) {
            const old = balls.shift();
            try { old.agg.dispose(); old.mesh.dispose(); } catch (_) {}
        }
        logToUI(`🎾 Бросок: v=${speed.toFixed(1)} м/с, m=${mass.toFixed(1)} кг, r=${radius.toFixed(2)}`, 'info');
    }

    function disposeBall(idx) {
        const b = balls[idx];
        try { b.agg.dispose(); b.mesh.dispose(); } catch (_) {}
        balls.splice(idx, 1);
    }

    function updateBalls(time) {
        const IS = parseFloat(iSat.value);
        const dom = parseFloat(ragDom.value);

        for (let bi = balls.length - 1; bi >= 0; bi--) {
            const b = balls[bi];
            if (time - b.born > BALL_LIFE) { disposeBall(bi); continue; }

            const v = bodyGetLinVel(b.agg.body);
            const cur = b.mesh.absolutePosition.clone();
            const prev = b.prevPos ? b.prevPos.clone() : cur.clone();

            for (let i = 0; i < colliderRefs.length; i++) {
                const ref = colliderRefs[i];
                const last = b.lastHits.get(i) || -10;
                if (time - last < BALL_HIT_CD) continue;

                ref.visMesh.computeWorldMatrix(true);
                const worldM = ref.visMesh.getWorldMatrix();
                const inv = worldM.clone(); inv.invert();
                const e = ref.halfExtents.add(new BABYLON.Vector3(b.radius, b.radius, b.radius));
                const p0 = BABYLON.Vector3.TransformCoordinates(prev, inv);
                const p1 = BABYLON.Vector3.TransformCoordinates(cur, inv);

                const d = p1.subtract(p0);
                let tEnter = 0, tExit = 1, hit = true;
                for (const ax of ['x', 'y', 'z']) {
                    if (Math.abs(d[ax]) < 1e-9) {
                        if (p0[ax] < -e[ax] || p0[ax] > e[ax]) { hit = false; break; }
                    } else {
                        let t1 = (-e[ax] - p0[ax]) / d[ax];
                        let t2 = (e[ax] - p0[ax]) / d[ax];
                        if (t1 > t2) { const t = t1; t1 = t2; t2 = t; }
                        tEnter = Math.max(tEnter, t1);
                        tExit = Math.min(tExit, t2);
                        if (tEnter > tExit) { hit = false; break; }
                    }
                }
                if (!hit) continue;

                const vBody = ref.kinematic ? new BABYLON.Vector3() : bodyGetLinVel(ref.agg.body);
                const vRel = v.subtract(vBody);
                const vAlong = vRel.length();
                if (vAlong < 0.6) continue;
                const dirW = vRel.clone().normalize();

                const contactLocal = BABYLON.Vector3.Lerp(p0, p1, Math.min(1, Math.max(0, tEnter)));
                const contactWorld = BABYLON.Vector3.TransformCoordinates(contactLocal, worldM);
                const rx = Math.abs(contactLocal.x) / e.x, ry = Math.abs(contactLocal.y) / e.y, rz = Math.abs(contactLocal.z) / e.z;
                const nL = new BABYLON.Vector3(0, 0, 0);
                if (rx >= ry && rx >= rz) nL.x = Math.sign(contactLocal.x) || 1;
                else if (ry >= rz) nL.y = Math.sign(contactLocal.y) || 1;
                else nL.z = Math.sign(contactLocal.z) || 1;
                const nW = BABYLON.Vector3.TransformNormal(nL, worldM).normalize();

                let target = ref;
                if (target.kinematic) target = target.parentRef;
                if (!target || target.kinematic) continue;

                const comWorld = target.visMesh.getAbsolutePosition();
                const rel = contactWorld.subtract(comWorld);

                b.lastHits.set(i, time);

                const massRatio = b.mass / (b.mass + target.mass);
                const effSpeed = Math.min(20, (1 + 0.5) * vAlong * massRatio);
                const plow = vAlong > 7 && massRatio > 0.3;

                applyImpulseTo(target, effSpeed * (plow ? 1.15 : 1.0), dirW, true, rel);
                logToUI(`🎾→${ref.name}${ref.kinematic ? '⇒' + target.name : ''}: v=${vAlong.toFixed(1)} → импульс ${(effSpeed).toFixed(1)} ${plow ? '[ПРОБИЛ]' : '[отскок]'}`, 'warn');

                const vDotN = v.dot(nW);
                if (plow) {
                    const keep = 0.45 + 0.4 * Math.min(1, massRatio);
                    const vNew = v.scale(keep);
                    if (vDotN < 0) vNew.addInPlace(nW.scale(-vDotN * 0.15));
                    bodySetLinVel(b.agg.body, vNew);
                } else if (vDotN < 0) {
                    const vNew = v.subtract(nW.scale((1 + BALL_BOUNCE) * vDotN));
                    bodySetLinVel(b.agg.body, vNew);
                }
                bodyWake(b.agg.body);
                break;
            }

            b.prevPos = cur;
        }
    }

    function syncInactiveToBlend(time) {
        if (falling) return;
        for (const ref of colliderRefs) {
            if (ref.kinematic) continue;
            const recentlyHit = (time - ref.braceAt) < SYNC_GRACE || ref.pendingW;
            if (ref.wS < W_SNAP && !recentlyHit) {
                ref.visMesh.computeWorldMatrix(true);
                const vp = ref.visMesh.getAbsolutePosition();
                const vr = ref.visMesh.absoluteRotationQuaternion || ref.visMesh.rotationQuaternion;
                bodySetTransform(ref.agg.body, vp, vr);
                bodyZeroVel(ref.agg.body);
                ref.physMesh.position.copyFrom(vp);
                ref.physMesh.rotationQuaternion = vr.clone();
                ref.physMesh.computeWorldMatrix(true);
            }
        }
    }

    function createRagdoll(skeleton, mesh, refSkeleton, refMesh) {
        if (syncObserver) { scene.onBeforeRenderObservable.remove(syncObserver); syncObserver = null; }
        for (const c of constraints) { try { c.dispose(); } catch (_) {} }
        constraints = [];
        for (const ref of colliderRefs) { if (ref.savedLinkedNode) { try { ref.bone.linkTransformNode(ref.savedLinkedNode); } catch (_) {} } }
        for (const ref of colliderRefs) { try { if (ref.agg) ref.agg.dispose(); } catch (_) {} try { if (ref.physMesh) ref.physMesh.dispose(); } catch (_) {} try { ref.visMesh.dispose(); } catch (_) {} }
        for (const b of balls) { try { b.agg.dispose(); b.mesh.dispose(); } catch (_) {} }
        balls = [];
        colliderRefs = []; boneToRef = new Map(); rootRef = null;
        activePulses = [];
        falling = false;
        instability = 0;
        scene.meshes.forEach(m => { if (m.name.startsWith('col_') || m.name.startsWith('vis_') || m.name.startsWith('throw_ball')) m.dispose(); });
        scene.stopAllAnimations();

        skinnedMesh = mesh; skelRef = skeleton;
        refSkel = refSkeleton; refMeshHidden = refMesh;
        refBoneMap = new Map();
        if (refSkel) for (const b of refSkel.bones) refBoneMap.set(b.name, b);
        mesh.computeWorldMatrix(true);

        const positions = mesh.getVerticesData(BABYLON.VertexBuffer.PositionKind);
        const matricesIndices = mesh.getVerticesData(BABYLON.VertexBuffer.MatricesIndicesKind);
        const matricesWeights = mesh.getVerticesData(BABYLON.VertexBuffer.MatricesWeightsKind);
        if (!positions || !matricesIndices || !matricesWeights) { logToUI('Нет скиннинга', 'error'); return; }

        const numVertices = positions.length / 3;
        const boneNameToIndex = {};
        skeleton.bones.forEach((b, i) => boneNameToIndex[b.name] = i);
        const boneVertexIndices = Array.from({ length: skeleton.bones.length }, () => []);
        for (let i = 0; i < numVertices; i += 2) {
            let maxW = matricesWeights[i * 4], maxIdx = matricesIndices[i * 4];
            for (let k = 1; k < 4; k++) if (matricesWeights[i * 4 + k] > maxW) { maxW = matricesWeights[i * 4 + k]; maxIdx = matricesIndices[i * 4 + k]; }
            if (maxW > 0.5 && maxIdx >= 0 && maxIdx < skeleton.bones.length) boneVertexIndices[maxIdx].push(i);
        }

        const tmpVec = new BABYLON.Vector3();

        const groups = [];
        const usedBones = new Set();
        for (const [groupName, boneNames] of Object.entries(boneGroups)) {
            const indices = []; const boneRefs = []; let valid = false;
            for (const name of boneNames) {
                const idx = boneNameToIndex[name];
                if (idx === undefined) continue;
                valid = true;
                usedBones.add(idx); boneRefs.push(skeleton.bones[idx]); indices.push(...boneVertexIndices[idx]);
            }
            if (!valid || indices.length < 4) continue;
            groups.push({ name: groupName, vertexIndices: [...new Set(indices)], primaryBone: boneRefs[0], boneNames: boneRefs.map(b => b.name).join('+') });
        }
        for (let i = 0; i < skeleton.bones.length; i++) {
            const bn = skeleton.bones[i].name;
            if (usedBones.has(i) || excludeBones.includes(bn) || SKIP_RE.test(bn)) continue;
            if (boneVertexIndices[i].length < 4) continue;
            groups.push({ name: bn, vertexIndices: boneVertexIndices[i], primaryBone: skeleton.bones[i], boneNames: bn });
        }
        logToUI(`Групп динамических: ${groups.length}`, 'info');

        for (const group of groups) {
            const bone = group.primaryBone;
            const invBind = bone.getAbsoluteInverseBindMatrix();
            let minX = Infinity, minY = Infinity, minZ = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity, maxY = -Infinity, maxZ = -Infinity;
            for (const vi of group.vertexIndices) {
                tmpVec.set(positions[vi * 3], positions[vi * 3 + 1], positions[vi * 3 + 2]);
                BABYLON.Vector3.TransformCoordinatesToRef(tmpVec, invBind, tmpVec);
                if (tmpVec.x < minX) minX = tmpVec.x; if (tmpVec.y < minY) minY = tmpVec.y; if (tmpVec.z < minZ) minZ = tmpVec.z;
                if (tmpVec.x > maxX) maxX = tmpVec.x; if (tmpVec.y > maxY) maxY = tmpVec.y; if (tmpVec.z > maxZ) maxZ = tmpVec.z;
            }
            const size = new BABYLON.Vector3(maxX - minX, maxY - minY, maxZ - minZ);
            const center = new BABYLON.Vector3((minX + maxX) / 2, (minY + maxY) / 2, (minZ + maxZ) / 2);
            if (size.lengthSquared() < 0.001) continue;

            const boneWorld = BABYLON.Matrix.Invert(invBind);
            const centerWorld = BABYLON.Vector3.TransformCoordinates(center, boneWorld);
            let boneWorldRot = null;
            try { boneWorldRot = bone.getRotationQuaternion(BABYLON.Space.WORLD, mesh); } catch (_) {}
            if (!boneWorldRot) boneWorldRot = BABYLON.Quaternion.FromRotationMatrix(boneWorld.getRotationMatrix());
            const restWorld = BABYLON.Matrix.ComposeToRef(BABYLON.Vector3.One(), boneWorldRot.clone(), centerWorld, new BABYLON.Matrix());

            const physMesh = BABYLON.MeshBuilder.CreateBox(`col_${group.name}`, { width: size.x, height: size.y, depth: size.z }, scene);
            physMesh.position.copyFrom(centerWorld);
            physMesh.rotationQuaternion = boneWorldRot.clone();
            physMesh.computeWorldMatrix(true);
            physMesh.visibility = showRag.checked ? 0.12 : 0;
            const pmat = new BABYLON.StandardMaterial('col_mat', scene);
            pmat.diffuseColor = COLOR_DYN; pmat.alpha = 0.25;
            physMesh.material = pmat;

            const agg = new BABYLON.PhysicsAggregate(physMesh, BABYLON.PhysicsShapeType.BOX, {
                mass: massFor(group.name), restitution: 0.1, friction: 0.95,
                linearDamping: 0.9, angularDamping: 1.5,
                collisionGroup: COL_GROUP.RAGDOLL,
                collisionMask: COL_MASK.RAGDOLL
            }, scene);
            bodySetGravityFactor(agg.body, 0);
            bodyWake(agg.body);

            const visMesh = BABYLON.MeshBuilder.CreateBox(`vis_${group.name}`, { width: size.x, height: size.y, depth: size.z }, scene);
            visMesh.position.copyFrom(centerWorld);
            visMesh.rotationQuaternion = boneWorldRot.clone();
            visMesh.computeWorldMatrix(true);
            visMesh.visibility = 0.4;
            const vmat = new BABYLON.StandardMaterial('vis_mat', scene);
            vmat.diffuseColor = COLOR_KIN; vmat.alpha = 0.5;
            visMesh.material = vmat;

            const refBone = refBoneMap.get(bone.name) || null;
            const boneRestWorld = bone.getWorldMatrix ? bone.getWorldMatrix().clone() : restWorld.clone();

            const ref = {
                visMesh, physMesh, agg, bone, name: group.name, boneNames: group.boneNames,
                bindCenter: center, isRoot: false, parentRef: null, children: [],
                savedLinkedNode: null, kinematic: false,
                mass: massFor(group.name),
                halfExtents: size.scale(0.5),
                restWorld,
                boneRestWorld,
                refBone,
                l1World: restWorld.clone(),
                w: 0, wS: 0,
                pendingW: null,
                braceAt: -10, braceAmp: 0,
                depth: 0,
                smPos: null, smRot: null,
                devDeg: { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 },
                spec: jointSpec(group.name),
            };
            colliderRefs.push(ref);
            boneToRef.set(bone, ref);
        }

        for (const ref of colliderRefs) {
            const tn = ref.bone.getTransformNode();
            if (tn) { ref.savedLinkedNode = tn; ref.bone.linkTransformNode(null); }
        }

        for (const r of colliderRefs) if (!rootRef || boneDepth(r.bone) < boneDepth(rootRef.bone)) rootRef = r;
        if (rootRef) {
            rootRef.isRoot = true;
            pelvisOffset = rootRef.bone.getAbsolutePosition(mesh).subtract(mesh.position.clone());
            logToUI(`Верх цепи: ${rootRef.name}`, 'info');
        }

        for (const ref of colliderRefs) {
            let parentBone = ref.bone.getParent();
            let parentRef = null;
            while (parentBone) {
                parentRef = boneToRef.get(parentBone) || colliderRefs.find(r => r.boneNames && r.boneNames.split('+').includes(parentBone.name));
                if (parentRef) break;
                parentBone = parentBone.getParent();
            }
            if (!parentRef) parentRef = rootRef;
            if (!parentRef || parentRef === ref) continue;
            ref.parentRef = parentRef;
            parentRef.children.push(ref);

            const jointWorld = ref.bone.getAbsolutePosition(mesh);
            const invParent = BABYLON.Matrix.Invert(parentRef.physMesh.getWorldMatrix());
            const invChild = BABYLON.Matrix.Invert(ref.physMesh.getWorldMatrix());
            const pivotA = BABYLON.Vector3.TransformCoordinates(jointWorld, invParent);
            const pivotB = BABYLON.Vector3.TransformCoordinates(jointWorld, invChild);
            const isHinge = ref.spec.type === 'hinge';
            let dirAxis;
            try { dirAxis = ref.bone.getDirection(isHinge ? BABYLON.Axis.X : BABYLON.Axis.Y, mesh).normalize(); }
            catch (_) { dirAxis = new BABYLON.Vector3(0, 1, 0); }
            const axisA = BABYLON.Vector3.TransformNormal(dirAxis, invParent).normalize();
            const axisB = BABYLON.Vector3.TransformNormal(dirAxis, invChild).normalize();

            const con = new BABYLON.Physics6DoFConstraint({ pivotA, pivotB, axisA, axisB }, ref.spec.limits, scene);
            parentRef.agg.body.addConstraint(ref.agg.body, con);
            try { con.isCollisionsEnabled = false; } catch (_) {}
            constraints.push(con);
        }

        if (rootRef) {
            rootRef.depth = 0;
            const queue = [rootRef];
            while (queue.length) {
                const r = queue.shift();
                for (const c of r.children) { c.depth = r.depth + 1; queue.push(c); }
            }
        }
        for (const ref of colliderRefs) {
            const d = ref.depth || 0;
            try { if (ref.agg.body.setAngularDamping) ref.agg.body.setAngularDamping(1.5 + 0.35 * d); } catch (_) {}
            try { if (ref.agg.body.setLinearDamping) ref.agg.body.setLinearDamping(0.9 + 0.1 * d); } catch (_) {}
        }

        let kinCount = 0;
        for (const kd of kinDefs) {
            const boneRefs = kd.bones.map(n => skeleton.bones[boneNameToIndex[n]]).filter(Boolean);
            if (!boneRefs.length) continue;
            const indices = [];
            for (const bn of boneRefs) indices.push(...boneVertexIndices[boneNameToIndex[bn.name]]);
            if (indices.length < 4) continue;

            const primary = boneRefs[0];
            const invBind = primary.getAbsoluteInverseBindMatrix();
            let minX = Infinity, minY = Infinity, minZ = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity, maxY = -Infinity, maxZ = -Infinity;
            for (const vi of indices) {
                tmpVec.set(positions[vi * 3], positions[vi * 3 + 1], positions[vi * 3 + 2]);
                BABYLON.Vector3.TransformCoordinatesToRef(tmpVec, invBind, tmpVec);
                if (tmpVec.x < minX) minX = tmpVec.x; if (tmpVec.y < minY) minY = tmpVec.y; if (tmpVec.z < minZ) minZ = tmpVec.z;
                if (tmpVec.x > maxX) maxX = tmpVec.x; if (tmpVec.y > maxY) maxY = tmpVec.y; if (tmpVec.z > maxZ) maxZ = tmpVec.z;
            }
            const size = new BABYLON.Vector3(maxX - minX, maxY - minY, maxZ - minZ);
            const center = new BABYLON.Vector3((minX + maxX) / 2, (minY + maxY) / 2, (minZ + maxZ) / 2);
            if (size.lengthSquared() < 0.001) continue;

            const boneWorld = BABYLON.Matrix.Invert(invBind);
            const centerWorld = BABYLON.Vector3.TransformCoordinates(center, boneWorld);
            let boneWorldRot = null;
            try { boneWorldRot = primary.getRotationQuaternion(BABYLON.Space.WORLD, mesh); } catch (_) {}
            if (!boneWorldRot) boneWorldRot = BABYLON.Quaternion.FromRotationMatrix(boneWorld.getRotationMatrix());
            const restWorld = BABYLON.Matrix.ComposeToRef(BABYLON.Vector3.One(), boneWorldRot.clone(), centerWorld, new BABYLON.Matrix());

            const visMesh = BABYLON.MeshBuilder.CreateBox(`vis_${kd.name}`, { width: size.x, height: size.y, depth: size.z }, scene);
            visMesh.position.copyFrom(centerWorld);
            visMesh.rotationQuaternion = boneWorldRot.clone();
            visMesh.computeWorldMatrix(true);
            visMesh.visibility = 0.4;
            const vmat = new BABYLON.StandardMaterial('vis_mat', scene);
            vmat.diffuseColor = COLOR_KIN; vmat.alpha = 0.5;
            visMesh.material = vmat;

            let parentBone = primary.getParent();
            let parentRef = null;
            while (parentBone) {
                parentRef = boneToRef.get(parentBone);
                if (parentRef && !parentRef.kinematic) break;
                parentRef = null;
                parentBone = parentBone.getParent();
            }
            if (!parentRef) parentRef = rootRef;

            const ref = {
                visMesh, physMesh: null, agg: null, bone: primary, name: kd.name, boneNames: boneRefs.map(b => b.name).join('+'),
                bindCenter: center, isRoot: false, parentRef, children: [],
                savedLinkedNode: null, kinematic: true,
                mass: 0,
                halfExtents: size.scale(0.5),
                restWorld,
                boneRestWorld: primary.getWorldMatrix ? primary.getWorldMatrix().clone() : restWorld.clone(),
                refBone: refBoneMap.get(primary.name) || null,
                l1World: restWorld.clone(),
                w: 0, wS: 0,
                pendingW: null,
                braceAt: -10, braceAmp: 0,
                depth: (parentRef ? parentRef.depth : 0) + 1,
                smPos: null, smRot: null,
                devDeg: { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 },
                spec: jointSpec(kd.name),
            };
            colliderRefs.push(ref);
            boneToRef.set(primary, ref);
            parentRef.children.push(ref);
            kinCount++;
        }
        logToUI(`Кинематических (кисти/стопы): ${kinCount}`, 'info');

        buildVis = function (dt) {
            const kSm = 1 - Math.exp(-Math.min(0.1, dt) / VIS_TAU);
            const memo = new Map();
            const compute = (ref) => {
                if (memo.has(ref)) return memo.get(ref);
                const p = ref.parentRef;
                const parentL2 = p ? compute(p) : BABYLON.Matrix.Identity();
                const parentL1 = p ? p.l1World : BABYLON.Matrix.Identity();

                let L2;
                if (ref.kinematic) {
                    const L1local = ref.l1World.clone().multiply(BABYLON.Matrix.Invert(parentL1));
                    L2 = L1local.multiply(parentL2);
                } else {
                    const L1local = ref.l1World.clone().multiply(BABYLON.Matrix.Invert(parentL1));
                    ref.physMesh.computeWorldMatrix(true);
                    const L3local = ref.physMesh.getWorldMatrix().clone().multiply(BABYLON.Matrix.Invert(p ? p.physMesh.getWorldMatrix() : BABYLON.Matrix.Identity()));

                    const d1 = decomposeM(L1local);
                    const d3 = decomposeM(L3local);
                    const w = ref.wS < W_SNAP ? 0 : ref.wS;

                    const pos = BABYLON.Vector3.Lerp(d1.pos, d3.pos, w);
                    const rot = BABYLON.Quaternion.Slerp(d1.rot, d3.rot, w);
                    const blendedLocal = BABYLON.Matrix.ComposeToRef(BABYLON.Vector3.One(), rot, pos, new BABYLON.Matrix());
                    L2 = blendedLocal.multiply(parentL2);
                }

                const d2 = decomposeM(L2);
                if (!ref.smPos || !ref.smRot) {
                    ref.smPos = d2.pos.clone();
                    ref.smRot = d2.rot.clone();
                } else {
                    ref.smPos = BABYLON.Vector3.Lerp(ref.smPos, d2.pos, kSm);
                    ref.smRot = BABYLON.Quaternion.Slerp(ref.smRot, d2.rot, kSm);
                }

                ref.visMesh.position.copyFrom(ref.smPos);
                ref.visMesh.rotationQuaternion = ref.smRot.clone();
                ref.visMesh.computeWorldMatrix(true);

                const pureL1 = ref.l1World.clone().multiply(BABYLON.Matrix.Invert(parentL1));
                const q2 = decomposeM(pureL1).rot.conjugate().multiply(d2.rot);
                const eu = new BABYLON.Vector3();
                q2.toEulerAnglesToRef(eu);
                ref.devDeg = { x: BABYLON.Tools.ToDegrees(eu.x), y: BABYLON.Tools.ToDegrees(eu.y), z: BABYLON.Tools.ToDegrees(eu.z) };

                if (ref.visMesh.material) ref.visMesh.material.diffuseColor = BABYLON.Color3.Lerp(COLOR_KIN, COLOR_DYN, ref.kinematic ? 0 : ref.wS);

                memo.set(ref, L2);
                return L2;
            };
            for (const r of colliderRefs) compute(r);
        };

        computeLayer1();
        buildVis(1);

        logToUI(`Готово: v26 (моментум-бюджет нокдауна)`, 'success');
        populateSelect();
        logAllAngles();
        updateSelectedLocalPos();
        updateModeIndicator();

        let frameCounter = 0;
        syncObserver = scene.onBeforeRenderObservable.add(() => {
            if (!skinnedMesh || !rootRef || !skelRef) return;
            const time = nowSec();
            const dt = Math.min(0.1, engine.getDeltaTime() / 1000);
            const meshPos0 = skinnedMesh.position;
            const s = skinnedMesh.scaling.x || 1;
            const oneOverS = 1 / s;
            const hk = parseFloat(holdK.value);
            const decayTau = parseFloat(lerpTime.value);
            const gFall = parseFloat(ragGravity.value);
            const recTau = parseFloat(recoveryTau.value);

            if (refSkel) { try { refMeshHidden.computeWorldMatrix(true); if (refSkel.computeAbsoluteMatrices) refSkel.computeAbsoluteMatrices(true); } catch (_) {} }

            computeLayer1();

            updateBalls(time);

            for (let i = activePulses.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
                const p = activePulses[i];
                const t = time - p.t0;
                if (t < 0 || t > p.dur) { activePulses.splice(i, 1); continue; }
                const a = p.dur / 3;
                const env = (t / a) * Math.exp(1 - t / a);
                const k = 1 / (a * Math.E);
                const F = p.impulse.scale(k * env);
                bodyApplyForceAt(p.ref.agg.body, F, p.rel);
                bodyWake(p.ref.agg.body);
            }

            for (const ref of colliderRefs) {
                if (ref.kinematic) continue;
                if (ref.pendingW && time >= ref.pendingW.at) {
                    if (ref.pendingW.a > ref.w) ref.w = ref.pendingW.a;
                    ref.pendingW = null;
                }
                if (falling) ref.w = Math.min(1, ref.w + dt * 3);
                else if (ref.w > 0) {
                    ref.w *= Math.exp(-dt / decayTau);
                    if (ref.w < 0.001) ref.w = 0;
                }
                const k = 1 - Math.exp(-dt / W_TAU_SMOOTH);
                ref.wS += (ref.w - ref.wS) * k;
                if (ref.w === 0 && Math.abs(ref.wS) < 0.001) ref.wS = 0;
            }

            // восстановление устойчивости
            if (!falling && instability > 0) {
                instability *= Math.exp(-dt / recTau);
                if (instability < 0.01) instability = 0;
            }
            ragPctEl.textContent = `${Math.min(100, instability * 100).toFixed(0)}%`;

            for (const ref of colliderRefs) {
                if (ref.kinematic) continue;
                bodySetGravityFactor(ref.agg.body, falling ? gFall : 0);
                if (!falling) applyPD(ref, hk * (1 - ref.wS), time);
                else bodyWake(ref.agg.body);
            }

            buildVis(dt);

            syncInactiveToBlend(time);

            const tracked = new Map();
            for (const bone of skelRef.bones) {
                const parent = bone.getParent();
                const P = parent ? tracked.get(parent) : null;
                const ref = boneToRef.get(bone);
                if (ref) {
                    const bw = ref.visMesh.getWorldMatrix();
                    const R = bw.getRotationMatrix();
                    const off = BABYLON.Vector3.TransformNormal(ref.bindCenter, R);
                    const pWorld = ref.visMesh.getAbsolutePosition().subtract(off);
                    const tPos = pWorld.subtract(meshPos0).scaleInPlace(oneOverS);
                    const T = BABYLON.Matrix.ComposeToRef(BABYLON.Vector3.One(), ref.visMesh.absoluteRotationQuaternion || BABYLON.Quaternion.Identity(), tPos, new BABYLON.Matrix());
                    let Lmat;
                    if (P) { const invP = P.clone(); invP.invert(); Lmat = T.clone().multiply(invP); }
                    else Lmat = T.clone();
                    const sc = new BABYLON.Vector3(); const ro = new BABYLON.Quaternion(); const po = new BABYLON.Vector3();
                    Lmat.decompose(sc, ro, po);
                    bone.position = po;
                    bone.setRotationQuaternion(ro, BABYLON.Space.LOCAL);
                    tracked.set(bone, T);
                } else {
                    let rest = bone.getLocalMatrix();
                    const rb = refBoneMap.get(bone.name);
                    if (rb) rest = rb.getLocalMatrix();
                    const W = (P && rest) ? rest.clone().multiply(P) : (rest ? rest.clone() : BABYLON.Matrix.Identity());
                    tracked.set(bone, W);
                }
            }
            const rootVis = rootRef.visMesh;
            rootVis.computeWorldMatrix(true);
            const boxRotMat = rootVis.getWorldMatrix().getRotationMatrix();
            const centerOff = BABYLON.Vector3.TransformNormal(rootRef.bindCenter, boxRotMat);
            const boneOriginPhys = rootVis.getAbsolutePosition().subtract(centerOff);
            skinnedMesh.position.copyFrom(boneOriginPhys.subtract(pelvisOffset));
            skinnedMesh.computeWorldMatrix(true);

            updateModeIndicator();

            frameCounter++;
            if (frameCounter % ANGLE_LOG_FRAMES === 0 && !logPaused) {
                logAllAngles();
                updateSelectedLocalPos();
            }
        });

        statusEl.innerHTML = `v26: моментум-нокдаун (${colliderRefs.length} тел)`;
        infoEl.innerHTML = `D=Δv·sens·h·horiz/K; K=8 по умолчанию`;
        angleCountEl.textContent = colliderRefs.length;
    }

    impulseBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (selectedIndex < 0 || selectedIndex >= colliderRefs.length) { logToUI('Сначала загрузите модель', 'warn'); return; }
        const speed = parseFloat(impulseSpeedInput.value) || 6;
        let target = colliderRefs[selectedIndex];
        if (target.kinematic) target = target.parentRef;
        if (!target || target.kinematic) { logToUI('Нет динамической цели', 'warn'); return; }
        applyImpulseTo(target, speed, randomPushDir());
    });
    randomBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const dyn = dynamicRefs();
        if (!dyn.length) { logToUI('Сначала загрузите модель', 'warn'); return; }
        const speed = parseFloat(impulseSpeedInput.value) || 6;
        applyImpulseTo(dyn[Math.floor(Math.random() * dyn.length)], speed, randomPushDir());
    });
    throwBtn.addEventListener('click', () => { spawnBall(); });
    fallBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (!colliderRefs.length) return;
        triggerFall('вручную');
    });
    standBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (!colliderRefs.length) return;
        falling = false;
        instability = 0;
        for (const r of colliderRefs) r.w = Math.min(r.w, 0.3);
        updateModeIndicator();
        logToUI('🧍 Встаю: гравитация выкл, PD тянет к позе', 'success');
    });
    resetBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (!skinnedMesh) return;
        falling = false;
        instability = 0;
        activePulses = [];
        for (const b of balls) { try { b.agg.dispose(); b.mesh.dispose(); } catch (_) {} }
        balls = [];
        for (const ref of colliderRefs) {
            ref.w = 0; ref.wS = 0;
            ref.pendingW = null;
            ref.braceAt = -10; ref.braceAmp = 0;
            ref.smPos = null; ref.smRot = null;
            if (!ref.kinematic) {
                const d = decomposeM(ref.restWorld);
                try { if (ref.agg.body.setTransformation) ref.agg.body.setTransformation(d.pos, d.rot); } catch (_) {}
                bodyZeroVel(ref.agg.body);
                bodySetGravityFactor(ref.agg.body, 0);
                ref.physMesh.position.copyFrom(d.pos);
                ref.physMesh.rotationQuaternion = d.rot.clone();
                ref.physMesh.computeWorldMatrix(true);
            }
        }
        if (buildVis) buildVis(1);
        updateModeIndicator();
        logToUI('⟲ Сброс', 'info');
    });

    function getLocalPositionsAndBBox() {
        if (!rootRef || !colliderRefs.length) return null;
        const invRoot = BABYLON.Matrix.Invert(rootRef.visMesh.getWorldMatrix());
        return colliderRefs.map(ref => {
            const centerLocal = BABYLON.Vector3.TransformCoordinates(ref.visMesh.getAbsolutePosition(), invRoot);
            const corners = ref.visMesh.getBoundingInfo().boundingBox.vectorsWorld.map(v => BABYLON.Vector3.TransformCoordinates(v, invRoot));
            const mn = new BABYLON.Vector3(Infinity, Infinity, Infinity), mx = new BABYLON.Vector3(-Infinity, -Infinity, -Infinity);
            for (const c of corners) {
                mn.x = Math.min(mn.x, c.x); mn.y = Math.min(mn.y, c.y); mn.z = Math.min(mn.z, c.z);
                mx.x = Math.max(mx.x, c.x); mx.y = Math.max(mx.y, c.y); mx.z = Math.max(mx.z, c.z);
            }
            return { name: ref.name, center: centerLocal, min: mn, max: mx };
        });
    }
    function updateSelectedLocalPos() {
        if (selectedIndex < 0 || selectedIndex >= colliderRefs.length) return;
        const data = getLocalPositionsAndBBox();
        if (!data) return;
        const d = data[selectedIndex];
        selectedCenterEl.textContent = `Центр: ${formatPos(d.center)}`;
        selectedBBoxEl.textContent = `BBox: min ${formatVec3(d.min)} max ${formatVec3(d.max)}`;
    }
    function logAllAngles() {
        if (!colliderRefs.length) return;
        const data = getLocalPositionsAndBBox();
        const lines = [];
        lines.push(`<div style="color:#88aacc;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:4px;">🔄 [${new Date().toLocaleTimeString('ru-RU', { hour12: false })}]</div>`);
        for (let i = 0; i < colliderRefs.length; i++) {
            const ref = colliderRefs[i];
            const deg = ref.devDeg;
            const nm = ref.name.length > 14 ? ref.name.slice(0, 12) + '…' : ref.name;
            const posStr = (data && data[i]) ? ` [центр ${formatPos(data[i].center)}]` : '';
            lines.push(`<div class="entry"><span class="name">${nm}${ref.isRoot ? '★' : ''}${ref.kinematic ? '·кин' : ''}</span> <span class="angles">${formatAngles(deg)}</span><span class="pos">${posStr}</span></div>`);
        }
        const maxEntries = 30;
        let combined = lines.join('') + angleLogEl.innerHTML;
        const divs = combined.split('<div class="entry"');
        if (divs.length > maxEntries + 2) combined = divs.slice(0, maxEntries + 1).join('<div class="entry"') + '<div style="color:#666;font-size:10px;">… старые скрыты</div>';
        angleLogEl.innerHTML = combined;
        angleLogEl.scrollTop = 0;
    }
    function populateSelect() {
        colliderSelect.innerHTML = '';
        colliderRefs.forEach((ref, idx) => {
            const opt = document.createElement('option');
            opt.value = idx;
            opt.textContent = `${ref.name}${ref.isRoot ? ' [TOP]' : ''}${ref.kinematic ? ' (кин→' + (ref.parentRef ? ref.parentRef.name : '?') + ')' : ''}`;
            colliderSelect.appendChild(opt);
        });
        selectedIndex = 0;
    }
    colliderSelect.addEventListener('change', () => {
        const idx = parseInt(colliderSelect.value);
        if (!isNaN(idx) && idx >= 0 && idx < colliderRefs.length) selectedIndex = idx;
        updateSelectedLocalPos();
    });

    fileInput.addEventListener('change', async (e) => {
        const file = e.target.files[0];
        if (!file) return;
        logToUI(`📂 Загрузка: ${file.name}`, 'info');
        scene.meshes.forEach(m => { if (m !== ground && !m.name.startsWith('col_') && !m.name.startsWith('vis_') && !m.name.startsWith('throw_ball')) m.dispose(); });
        try {
            const url = URL.createObjectURL(file);
            const [resVis, resRef] = await Promise.all([
                BABYLON.SceneLoader.ImportMeshAsync('', url, '', scene, undefined, '.glb'),
                BABYLON.SceneLoader.ImportMeshAsync('', url, '', scene, undefined, '.glb')
            ]);
            URL.revokeObjectURL(url);

            const skeleton = resVis.skeletons[0];
            const mesh = resVis.meshes.find(m => m.skeleton) || resVis.meshes[0];
            const refSkeleton = resRef.skeletons[0] || null;
            const refMesh = resRef.meshes.find(m => m.skeleton) || resRef.meshes[0];
            if (refMesh) {
                refMesh.isVisible = false;
                if (refMesh.getChildMeshes) refMesh.getChildMeshes().forEach(m => m.isVisible = false);
            }
            if (resRef.animationGroups && resRef.animationGroups.length) {
                for (const ag of resRef.animationGroups) ag.start(true);
                logToUI('▶️ Слой1: анимация запущена', 'info');
            }

            if (!skeleton) throw new Error('Нет скелета');
            if (!mesh) throw new Error('Нет меша');

            mesh.computeWorldMatrix(true);
            const bbox = mesh.getBoundingInfo().boundingBox;
            const size = bbox.maximumWorld.subtract(bbox.minimumWorld);
            const maxDim = Math.max(size.x, size.y, size.z);
            if (maxDim > 10) { mesh.scaling.scaleInPlace(2.0 / maxDim); mesh.computeWorldMatrix(true); }
            const bb2 = mesh.getBoundingInfo().boundingBox;
            mesh.position.y -= bb2.minimumWorld.y;
            mesh.computeWorldMatrix(true);
            mesh.visibility = 1.0;

            createRagdoll(skeleton, mesh, refSkeleton, refMesh);
        } catch (err) {
            logToUI(`❌ ${err.message}`, 'error');
            console.error(err);
        }
    });

    engine.runRenderLoop(() => scene.render());
    window.addEventListener('resize', () => engine.resize());
    logToUI('🟢 Загрузите GLB/GLTF — v26', 'success');
</script>
</body>
</html>
разбери код.
